The framework was evaluated using five-fold cross-validation and internal test cohorts across TLS, blood-vessel, and gland segmentation tasks, with an additional independent 30-WSI held-out cohort for TLS. Positive-only, all-tissue, random-negative, and matched random Top$K$ sampling strategies served as comparators. Segmentation-derived phenotypes were further explored in external TCGA cohorts.

</details>


### [34] [Controllable Image Captioning with Prompt-Conditioned Scene Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00709)
*Jongyeop Hyun,Taeyoung Kim,Hyounghun Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FoCUS是一种可控图像描述生成方法，通过基于场景图对齐组件的提示条件控制目标（结合GRPO优化），让用户能用自然语言控制提示指定描述应强调的属性、关系和图像区域，并引入SCoPE基准来评估可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型视觉-语言模型生成的图像描述虽然流畅，但缺乏语义控制能力，用户无法可靠地指定描述应强调哪些属性、关系或图像区域。

Method: 提出提示条件控制目标，基于场景图对齐的组件分数。生成的描述被解析并对齐到场景图组件（对象、属性、关系），根据请求的强调重点对这些组件进行差异化加权（含负权重），使用GRPO优化，并通过更严格的对象有效性阈值和基于推理的属性/关系评分验证提高可靠性。同时引入SCoPE基准，用于对比Include/Avoid约束下的可控性度量。

Result: 在两个VLM骨干网络上实验表明，FoCUS持续提升了可控性和细粒度描述质量，且不降低通用描述性能。

Conclusion: FoCUS通过场景奖励机制实现了细粒度的可控图像描述，为VLM提供了一种不牺牲通用性能的情况下增强语义控制能力的有效方案。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models produce fluent image descriptions but offer limited semantic control: users cannot reliably specify whether captions should emphasize attributes, relations, or particular image regions. We present Fine-grained Captioning Control Using Scene Rewards (FoCUS), a controllable image captioning method that lets users steer captions toward specific semantic emphases through natural-language control prompts. The core idea is a prompt-conditioned control objective based on scene-graph-aligned component scores. Generated captions are parsed and aligned to scene-graph components such as objects, attributes, and relations. These components are differentially weighted, including negative weights, according to the requested emphasis. We optimize this objective with GRPO and further improve its reliability through a stricter object validity threshold and reasoning-based verification for attribute and relation scoring. To evaluate controllability, we introduce Semantic Control and Precision Evaluation (SCoPE), a benchmark with contrastive Include/Avoid constraints for measuring both target content coverage and out-of-scope suppression. Experiments on two VLM backbones show that FoCUS consistently improves controllability and fine-grained caption quality without degrading general caption performance.

</details>


### [35] [EarthLD: Towards Unified Open-World Landslide Understanding via Vision-Language Guided Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00712)
*Yuanchao Su,Lianru Gao,Mengying Jiang,Jiangyi Chen,Jiaxin Cheng,Yicong Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EarthLD是一个基于视觉-语言引导的扩散框架，用于开放世界滑坡理解，统一实现滑坡识别、制图和触发因素解读。该框架将滑坡理解形式化为从噪声潜变量中逐步推断滑坡存在、范围和像素级边界的扩散过程，并结合视觉观察与上下文知识进行去噪，同时构建了全球尺度的开放世界滑坡基准数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 滑坡在遥感影像中的自动化检测与制图面临诸多挑战：形态不规则、光谱特征模糊、跨成像平台存在显著域偏移，导致现有方法难以统一识别、分割和解释滑坡事件。

Method: EarthLD将滑坡理解构建为扩散过程，从噪声潜表示中渐进推断滑坡的存在、空间范围和像素级边界；在去噪过程中融合视觉观测与语境知识（视觉-语言引导），实现图像级识别、映射和置信度感知的预测；同时系统性地整合多个机构的公开遥感数据，构建全球尺度的开放世界滑坡基准。

Result: 在跨区域、跨传感器和跨触发事件的广泛实验中，EarthLD持续优于现有滑坡检测方法，验证了其作为全球地质灾害监测统一解决方案的有效性。

Conclusion: EarthLD展示了扩散框架与视觉语言模型结合在开放世界地质灾害理解中的潜力，可作为全球滑坡监测和应急响应的统一稳健方案。

Abstract: Landslides are widespread geological hazards, yet their automated detection and mapping in remote sensing imagery remain challenging because of their irregular morphology, ambiguous spectral signatures, and substantial domain shifts across imaging platforms. To overcome these challenges, we propose EarthLD, a vision-language-guided diffusion framework for open-world landslide understanding, enabling unified landslide recognition, mapping, and trigger interpretation. At its core, EarthLD formulates landslide understanding as a diffusion process that progressively infers the presence, spatial extent, and pixel-level boundaries of landslides from noisy latent representations. This probabilistic formulation enables the model to jointly perform image-level landslide recognition and mapping while characterizing predictive uncertainty. By integrating visual observations with contextual knowledge in the denoising process, EarthLD distinguishes diverse landslides from backgrounds, produces confidence-aware predictions for suspected regions, and maps landslide ranges. We additionally construct a global-scale open-world landslide benchmark by systematically harmonizing multiple publicly available remote sensing data collected by diverse institutions. Extensive experiments across regions, sensors, and triggering events demonstrate that EarthLD consistently outperforms existing landslide detection methods, highlighting its potential as a unified and robust solution for global geological-hazard monitoring and emergency response.

</details>


### [36] [Efficient and Robust Absolute Pose Estimation via Gravity-Prior-Driven Transformation Decoupling and Pose Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00713)
*Hu Cao,Qianyi Yang,Xinyi Li,Jiong Liu,Yinlong Liu,Alois Knoll*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种利用重力先验的绝对位姿估计算法，通过变换解耦将6-DoF问题简化为4-DoF（1-DoF旋转角+3-DoF平移），采用一维全局投票估计最优旋转角，随后线性求解平移，并引入位姿精化算法提升精度。在合成数据和TUM RGB-D、ETH3D、RobotCar等真实数据集上超越现有SOTA方法，并集成到ORB-SLAM2中在KITTI上验证了重定位效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 绝对位姿估计是机器人应用的核心任务。虽然利用重力方向作为先验信息已成为简化该问题的流行方法，但在大量误匹配下仍缺乏鲁棒高效的求解算法，且利用重力先验从内点对应中获取精确位姿解仍存在研究空白。

Method: 提出变换解耦策略，利用重力先验的几何关系将原始6-DoF绝对位姿估计简化为4-DoF问题：1-DoF旋转角采用一维全局投票算法进行最优估计，获得最优旋转后初步过滤误匹配，随后3-DoF平移作为线性问题易解；最后引入位姿精化算法同时提升旋转和平移精度。

Result: 在合成数据和TUM RGB-D、ETH3D、RobotCar三个公开真实数据集上的大量实验表明，所提方法相比现有SOTA方法表现更强。集成到ORB-SLAM2后在KITTI数据集上的结果显示能有效减少漂移并改善重定位时的轨迹对齐。

Conclusion: 通过重力先验的变换解耦有效简化了绝对位姿估计问题，在效率和精度上均优于现有方法，并展示了在实际SLAM系统中的实用价值。

Abstract: Estimation of the absolute pose of an object is an essential task for various robotic applications. Recently, incorporating gravity direction as prior information has emerged as a popular approach to simplify absolute pose estimation. However, developing a robust and efficient algorithm to solve this challenging problem remains a difficult question due to large amounts of mismatches. In addition, obtaining an accurate pose solution from selected inlier correspondences with gravity prior is still a research gap. In this paper, we propose a novel transformation strategy that exploits geometric relations derived from the gravity prior. Through transformation decoupling, the original 6 degrees of freedom (DoF) absolute pose estimation problem is simplified into a 4-DoFs problem: 1-DoF for the rotation angle and 3-DoFs for translation, significantly improving the efficiency. For the 1-DoF rotation angle, we apply a one-dimensional global voting algorithm for optimal estimation. Once the optimal rotation is obtained, the mismatched correspondences are preliminarily filtered, and translation estimation, a linear problem, can be easily solved. Furthermore, to obtain accurate pose results, we introduce a novel pose refinement algorithm to enhance the accuracy of both rotation and translation. Extensive experiments on synthetic data and three publicly available real-world datasets (TUM RGB-D, ETH3D, and RobotCar) demonstrate that the proposed method achieves stronger performance compared to existing state-of-the-art (SOTA) approaches. To further validate our method, we integrated it into ORB-SLAM2. The results on the KITTI dataset show it effectively reduces drift and improves trajectory alignment during relocalization. The source code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低成本惯性传感器在倾斜角估计中受噪声和漂移影响严重，单传感器（加速度计或陀螺仪）各自存在缺陷，需要一种简单实用且可部署的传感器融合方案来获得稳定准确的倾角估计。

Method: 使用MPU6050获取加速度计与陀螺仪数据，加速度计提供基于重力的倾角直接估计（长期稳定但噪声敏感），陀螺仪提供角速度积分（短期平滑但随时间漂移）。通过卡尔曼滤波器融合两者，在仿真中建模噪声与漂移评估滤波器性能，并在RP2040平台上进行实时硬件实验验证。

Result: 仿真和硬件实验结果表明，卡尔曼滤波融合方法有效降低噪声、抑制长期漂移，同时保持良好的动态响应，倾角估计比单独使用任一传感器更稳定和准确。

Conclusion: 该系统展示了一种实用且易于实现的基于卡尔曼滤波的传感器融合方法，适用于嵌入式倾斜角估计应用。

Abstract: Accurate tilt angle estimation is important in many engineering applications, such as robotics, motion tracking, and embedded control systems. However, measurements from low-cost inertial sensors are often degraded by noise and drift. This paper presents a single-axis tilt angle estimation system based on the MPU6050 inertial measurement unit, implemented on an RP2040 microcontroller platform, with sensor fusion achieved through a Kalman filter. The accelerometer provides a direct estimate of tilt angle from gravity but is sensitive to noise and short-term fluctuations. The gyroscope provides smooth angular rate measurements, but integration over time introduces drift. To overcome these limitations, a Kalman filter is used to combine measurements from both sensors, leveraging the long-term stability of the accelerometer and the short-term smoothness of the gyroscope. Both simulation and hardware experiments are performed. In simulation, sensor noise and drift are modeled to evaluate the filter performance under control conditions. In the hardware implementation, real-time MPU6050 data is acquired and processed by the RP2040 platform, and the estimated tilt angle is compared with accelerometer-only and gyroscope-only outputs. The results show that the proposed method effectively reduces noise measurements and suppresses long-term drift while preserving good dynamic response. Overall, the system provides more stable and accurate tilt estimation than either sensor alone, demonstrating a practical and accessible approach for Kalman filter based sensor fusion in embedded application.

</details>


### [38] [MeRoPE: Metric Rotary Position Embedding for Camera-Controlled Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01252)
*Zhijian Qiao,Xinjiang Wang,Jiajie Chen,Haoming Huang,Meng Li,Chih-Chung Chou,Jing Wang,Shaojie Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MeRoPE（Metric Rotary Position Embedding），一种针对相机控制视频生成中几何感知位置编码的归一化保持相对编码方案，解决了现有齐次投影编码在真实度量相机轨迹上随平移基线增长导致注意力logits和特征范数无界的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有相机控制视频生成中的几何感知位置编码（如齐次投影编码）在真实世界度量相机轨迹上存在尺度依赖的失败模式：随物理平移基线增大，注意力logits和特征范数无界增长，导致模型在大量程轨迹和多样化相机光学系统下性能退化。

Method: MeRoPE包含三个关键设计：(1) 使用正交旋转块对校准观测射线之间的相对方向进行编码；(2) 将原始度量位移映射到多频旋转相位；(3) 沿极线弧添加基于视差的对应先验。整体保证特征范数严格保持、预softmax注意力logits有界于物理平移尺度无关，且对全局刚性坐标变化保持精确不变性。

Result: 在nuScenes（大基线轨迹）和PanShot（多样化相机光学）两个数据集上，MeRoPE相比先前编码方案实现了更强的相机控制能力，在旋转和平移方向上生成的相机运动与条件位姿之间的一致性均为最优。

Conclusion: MeRoPE通过范数保持的相对相机编码，有效解决了尺度依赖的注意力退化问题，为真实世界度量相机轨迹下的相机控制视频生成提供了更鲁棒的位置编码方案。

Abstract: In camera-controlled video generation, geometry-aware positional encodings condition tokens on camera extrinsics and per-token viewing rays. Existing schemes, however, have a scale-dependent failure mode on real-world metric camera trajectories: homogeneous projective encodings cause attention logits and feature norms to grow unbounded with physical translation baselines. We propose MeRoPE (Metric Rotary Position Embedding), a norm-preserving relative camera encoding for attention. MeRoPE encodes relative orientations between calibrated viewing rays with orthogonal rotation blocks, maps raw metric displacements into multi-frequency rotary phases, and adds a disparity-anchored correspondence prior along the epipolar arc. This design strictly preserves feature norms, bounds pre-softmax attention logits regardless of the physical translation scale, and maintains exact invariance to global rigid coordinate changes. Across nuScenes and PanShot, which cover large-baseline trajectories and diverse camera optics, respectively, MeRoPE achieves stronger camera control than prior encodings, with the best consistency between generated camera motion and conditioning poses in both rotation and translation. Code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [39] [Mind the Rift: Cross-Scale Coupling Mismatch for AI-Generated Video Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00742)
*Siyu Li,Jin Yang,Weiheng Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RIFT提出了一种新的AI生成视频检测框架，基于跨尺度耦合失配信号（宏观语义动力学与微观像素残差之间的耦合断裂），通过Gram-Schmidt正交化保证双流信息提取的独立性，在两个大规模基准上达到99%+的F1分数，并展现出卓越的编码器无关性与跨生成器泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI视频生成器达到电影级真实性，可靠的检测机制对于维护数字信任至关重要。现有方法仅单独检测单一尺度的伪影，而作者发现自然视频中宏观时间动力学与微观残差模式通过统一的成像物理管线内在耦合，AI生成器因训练目标未显式保持联合分布而系统性违反此耦合——这是一个新的取证信号。

Method: RIFT由三个互锁组件构成：(1) 宏流——通过微分几何和持久同调在学习到的流形轨迹上构建预期时间演化的动态基线；(2) 微流——通过隐写分析滤波和时序建模作为敏感的取证探针；(3) 耦合发散模块——量化两流之间的条件依赖性。Gram-Schmidt正交化保证该测量的信息论有效性。

Result: 在VidProM（120K视频，7个生成器）上F1达99.33%，GenVidBench（68K视频，4个生成器）上F1达99.72%；留一法评估中未知生成器检测率达97.87%；编码器无关性显著——从ViT-S/14（22M）扩展到ViT-L/14（300M）F1变化小于0.1%，切换至DINOv1仅降低0.73个百分点。

Conclusion: 跨尺度耦合失配是一种鲁棒且有效的AI生成视频取证信号，RIFT通过正交双流架构实现了高精度、跨生成器泛化与编码器无关的检测性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: As AI video generators achieve cinematic realism, reliable detection becomes essential for safeguarding digital trust. We identify cross-scale coupling mismatch as a new forensic signal, where scale refers to the level of abstraction (semantic dynamics vs. pixel-level residuals): in natural videos, macro-level temporal dynamics and micro-level residual patterns are intrinsically coupled by the unified imaging physics pipeline, whereas AI generators, whose training objectives do not explicitly preserve this joint distribution, systematically violate this coupling. Detecting such mismatch is challenging because it requires independently extracting information at both scales while simultaneously quantifying their cross-scale relationship. We propose RIFT (Representation Inconsistency Forensics on Trajectories), an orthogonal forensic framework that addresses this through three interlocking components: a macro stream that builds a dynamic baseline of expected temporal evolution via differential geometry and persistent homology on learned manifold trajectories, a micro stream that acts as a sensitive forensic probe via steganalytic filtering and temporal modeling, and a coupling divergence module that measures the conditional dependency between the two streams. Gram-Schmidt orthogonality guarantees the information-theoretic validity of this measurement. Experiments on two benchmarks (VidProM, 120K videos, 7 generators; GenVidBench, 68K videos, 4 generators) demonstrate that RIFT achieves 99.33% and 99.72% F1-score respectively, with 97.87% unseen-generator detection rate in leave-one-out evaluation, while exhibiting encoder agnosticism: scaling from ViT-S/14 (22M) to ViT-L/14 (300M) changes F1 by less than 0.1%, and switching to a different encoder family (DINOv1) reduces F1 by only 0.73 pp. Code is available at https://github.com/Litsay/RIFT

</details>


### [40] [Feed-Forward Multi-view Multi-person Reconstruction with Contrastive Human-Aware 3D Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00745)
*Yuanwang Yang,Buzhen Huang,Zongxuan Ren,Jing Huang,Kun Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的自顶向下(top-down)多视角多人重建范式，通过维护统一的实例中心3D空间，结合跨模态对比学习，同时实现相机标定、跨视角关联和人体重建，在严重遮挡下保持鲁棒性，并以前馈方式回归SMPL参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自底向上(bottom-up)的多人重建方法依赖精确相机标定和显式跨视角匹配，在未约束环境中面对严重遮挡和歧义时鲁棒性不足，需探索更统一高效的范式。

Method: 提出实例中心的统一3D空间，将多视角观测提升并融合其中，在实例层面联合编码几何结构、视觉外观和人体语义；引入空间对比学习策略，对齐同一人体实例在不同视角和模态下的3D特征，同时分离不同实例；最后从实例级3D人体token前馈回归SMPL参数。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在具有挑战的现实场景中实现了鲁棒、准确且高效的多视角人体重建。

Conclusion: 统一实例中心3D空间与跨模态对比学习相结合，能够在严重遮挡下原生地进行对应推理、语义聚合和实例判别，显著提升跨视角一致性与鲁棒性，形成更实用的多人重建方案。

Abstract: Multi-view human reconstruction has been extensively studied under simplified settings, yet robust and efficient multi-person reconstruction in unconstrained environments remains challenging. Existing bottom-up methods often rely on accurate camera calibration and explicit cross-view matching, and therefore struggle with severe occlusions and ambiguities. We propose a new top-down paradigm that maintains a unified, instance-centric human-aware 3D space, enabling simultaneous camera calibration, cross-view association, and human reconstruction via cross-modal contrastive learning. Observations from multiple views are lifted and fused into this shared 3D space, where geometric structure, visual appearance, and human-centric semantic cues are jointly encoded at the instance level. We further introduce a spatial contrastive learning strategy that aligns 3D features corresponding to the same human instance across different views and modalities while separating different instances. This enables correspondence reasoning, semantic aggregation, and instance discrimination to be performed natively in 3D, improving cross-view consistency and robustness under severe occlusions. Finally, structured human body models are recovered in a feed-forward manner by regressing SMPL parameters from instance-level 3D human tokens. Extensive experiments demonstrate robust, accurate, and efficient multi-view human reconstruction in challenging real-world scenarios.

</details>


### [41] [VOIM: Training-Free Open-Vocabulary 3D Instance Mapping for RGB-D and Monocular SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00775)
*Sangmin Song,Sarath Kodagoda,Marc G. Carmichael,Karthick Thiyagarajan,Amal Gunatilake,Kelly Prentice,Jodi Martin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VOIM是一种无需训练、基于体素的开放词汇3D实例地图管理方法，支持RGB-D或纯单目RGB输入，通过延迟实例与标签决策、跨视角在体素上累积软证据来构建3D实例地图，在ScanNet++和Replica上超越现有最强的在线RGB-D系统OVO-SLAM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线3D实例分割系统通常在首次检测时就提交实例与标签决策，而此刻证据最弱，导致错误传播。作者主张应将标签与实例决策推迟到来自现成感知模型的软证据在每个体素上跨视角累积之后再做出，且纯单目RGB的免训练方案此前无人涉及。

Method: VOIM作为训练无关的体素级实例管理器：(1)使用现成的、未经修改的感知模型（区域描述子、检测器标签先验、掩码来源）在每帧生成软证据；(2)将证据按体素在多个视角间累积；(3)延迟联合推断实例分割与开放词汇标签，直到证据充分（体素累积后）再做出最终决策；(4)输出占用网格并可解析自由形式的查询到具体实例。系统不修改任何感知骨干，纯靠地图阶段的设计获得增益。

Result: 在ScanNet++上跨四种感知配置，VOIM比最强在线RGB-D系统OVO-SLAM高4.8~11.7 mIoU。对齐协议下VOIM达44.07 mIoU对基线的32.37，十场景全胜（pooled 33.31 vs 25.97）。同一系统无需改动可直接用于纯单目RGB，在Replica上与基线pooled持平（27.80 vs 27.50）。Replica全类打分下结果分叉：pooled 28.60 vs 27.50胜出，per-scene均值24.59 vs 30.11落后。感知并不中立——替换基线自己的描述子族会损失4.1的mIoU。

Conclusion: 地图阶段（而非特定感知模型）承担了主要性能差异；体素级证据累积与延迟决策是开放词汇3D实例建图的关键机制。VOIM是首个支持纯单目RGB的免训练在线开放词汇3D实例建图系统。局限性：房间尺度受标签限制、建筑尺度受漂移限制；标注并非实时（受全词表逐类检测主导）。

Abstract: We present Voxel-Grounded Online Instance Manager (VOIM), a training-free voxel-grounded instance manager that builds open-vocabulary 3D instance maps from RGB-D or from monocular RGB alone, a regime no prior training-free system addresses. Online systems typically segment object instances and label them at first detection, committing when evidence is weakest. VOIM instead defers label and instance decisions until soft evidence from unmodified, off-the-shelf perception has accumulated per voxel across views. We show that the mapping stage, rather than the particular perception models, carries the result: across four perception configurations on ScanNet++, varying the region descriptor, the detector label prior and the mask source, the map exceeds the strongest online RGB-D system, OVO-SLAM, by between 4.8 and 11.7 mIoU. Perception is not neutral, and substituting that baseline's own descriptor family costs 4.1 of the margin, yet the baseline carries the marginally better 2D descriptor (33.7 vs. 31.5 mIoU over three scenes) and still realizes the weaker map. Under a like-for-like protocol VOIM reaches 44.07 mIoU on ScanNet++ against 32.37, winning all ten scenes and both aggregations (pooled 33.31 vs. 25.97), and the same system runs unchanged to fully monocular RGB, matching that baseline pooled on Replica (27.80 vs. 27.50). The advantage is regime-specific: under Replica's all-classes scoring, matched inputs give a split result, 28.60 vs. 27.50 pooled against 24.59 vs. 30.11 on the per-scene mean. Room scale is label-limited and building scale drift-limited. Labeling does not run in real time, dominated by per-class detection over the full vocabulary. The maps export occupancy grids and resolve free-form queries to object instances.

</details>


### [42] [Solaris: Towards Interfaces That Are Generated, Not Coded](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00776)
*Yuval Alaluf,Omri Avrahami,Guy Bukchin Leshem,Michal Geyer,Kfir Goldberg,Elad Richardson,Diego Alarcón,Alejandro Alvarez,Cole Garry,Anastasis Germanidis,Tenaya Goldsen,Corina Gurau,Robin Kahlow,Joel Kwartler,Kathleen Lewis,Alejandro Matamala Ortiz,Eugene McMahon,Thon Prom,Sarah Saltonstall-Wurm,Jamie Umpherson,Hudson Yeo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Solaris是一个"界面世界模型"，摒弃传统代码/中间表示实现UI的方式，而是逐帧直接生成交互式UI画面，以响应鼠标操作作为条件信号实现自回归的视觉状态生成，结合语言模型进行交互意图理解和场景规则推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数字界面通过代码等中间表示实现，需预先指定外观和行为，难以支持开放式的适应性交互。作者希望通过直接生成界面的世界模型，打破预先编程的固定状态集合的限制。

Method: Solaris将鼠标交互作为条件信号，自回归地合成交互后的视觉状态；结合少步蒸馏和模型自身输出训练实现实时生成并维持长程交互的视觉连贯性；语言模型负责解释用户意图并指定交互如何影响生成环境，将高层推理与视觉渲染分离。

Result: Solaris能在交互速度下实时逐帧生成UI视觉状态，支持无需预先显式编程的开放式交互。

Conclusion: 界面世界模型代表了软件的一种新范式：界面围绕用户意图持续生成和适应，而非实现为预定义状态的固定集合。该方向为未来软件交互方式提供了新思路。

Abstract: Digital interfaces are traditionally implemented through intermediate representations such as code, requiring their appearance and behavior to be specified in advance. We introduce Solaris, an interface world model that instead generates an interactive UI directly, frame by frame, in response to user actions. Solaris treats mouse interactions as conditioning signals and autoregressively synthesizes the resulting visual state at interactive speeds. To enable real-time generation while maintaining visual coherence over extended interactions, we combine autoregressive frame generation with few-step distillation and training on the model's own outputs. A language model complements the visual world model by interpreting user intent and specifying how interactions should affect the generated environment, separating high-level reasoning from visual rendering. By generating both the appearance and behavior of an interface dynamically, Solaris enables open-ended interactions that need not be explicitly programmed in advance. We view interface world models as a step toward a new paradigm for software, where interfaces are generated and adapted continuously around user intent rather than implemented as fixed collections of predefined states and

</details>


### [43] [Forbid Your Attention: Fooling Multimodal Large Language Models by Selectively Removing Intrinsic Focus in Spectral Domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00788)
*Daizong Liu,Junhao Dong,Zhiyuan Ma,Xiaoye Qu,Xiang Fang,Runwei Guan,Keke Tang,Jianfeng Dong,Yew-Soon Ong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于相位感知的对抗攻击框架（phase-aware adversarial attack），针对多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的视觉组件进行攻击。作者发现MLLMs的预测对频域中的相位信息高度敏感（相位编码结构性和语义线索），据此将对抗扰动限制在结构相关的相位区域，并引入辅助对抗提示学习模块引导多模态错位，实现有效且不易察觉的攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对MLLMs的对抗攻击主要使用全局扰动，缺乏对模型内部如何理解视觉结构的理解。作者希望从频域角度揭示MLLMs对视觉结构的内在关注点，从而设计更有效且更隐蔽的攻击方法。

Method: 1) 频域分析：发现MLLMs预测对相位信息敏感，相位编码结构性和语义线索；2) 相位感知对抗攻击框架：将对抗扰动显式限制在结构相关的相位区域内，抑制MLLM的注意力；3) 辅助对抗提示学习模块：引导多模态错位围绕相位敏感区域，误导模型关注目标结构模式。

Result: 在多个代表性MLLM模型和数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法相比现有攻击具有更优的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过频域分析揭示了MLLMs对相位信息的敏感性，并据此提出了更有效、更隐蔽的对抗攻击方法，验证了结构敏感攻击在多模态模型上的优势。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have extended the capability of large language models (LLMs) to process more contextual multimodal information, showing remarkable progress in diverse realistic multimodal applications. Despite their strong perception and reasoning abilities, recent studies reveal that MLLMs remain highly vulnerable to adversarial inputs, especially those targeting visual components. However, existing attacks mainly focus on global perturbations, lacking an understanding of how MLLMs internally interpret visual structures. In this paper, we make the attempt to investigate the intrinsic focus of MLLMs in the frequency domain and discover that their predictions are particularly sensitive to phase information, which encodes essential structural and semantic cues. Based on this observation, we propose a novel phase-aware adversarial attack framework that explicitly restricts adversarial perturbations to structure-relevant phase regions to suppress the MLLMs' focus for effective and imperceptible attacks. To further amplify the structural influence, we also introduce an auxiliary adversarial prompt learning module to guide multimodal misalignment around phase-sensitive regions, misleading the MLLM's attention toward targeted structural patterns. Extensive experiments on multiple representative MLLM models and datasets demonstrate the superior effectiveness of our method compared to existing attacks.

</details>


### [44] [Advanced Pixel Diffusion Model with Guided Sparse Global Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00798)
*Weiyi You,Jinhua Zhang,Xingyu Zhou,Wei Long,Junyu Lou,Shuhang Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PixSGR是一种像素空间扩散框架，通过稀疏全局精炼直接在像素域建模自然图像分布，在ImageNet 256×256上达到FID 1.51，512×512上达到FID 1.60。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 像素空间扩散在原始像素域直接建模高保真图像潜力大，但计算代价极高。现有方法要么用大patch tokenization牺牲细节，要么将精炼局限于单个patch内，导致patch边界结构连续性差且缺乏长程token交互，精炼质量受限。

Method: PixSGR包含三部分：(1) 有监督低通道瓶颈高效捕获自然图像低维流形；(2) 逐步扩展通道维度和空间分辨率以恢复细粒度结构；(3) 空间精炼阶段利用粗尺度注意力图预选全局相关交互，对细尺度注意力进行预稀疏化，从而实现超越孤立patch的非局部精炼，同时避免密集注意力的二次方计算成本。

Result: 在ImageNet上验证有效，256×256分辨率达到FID 1.51，扩展到512×512仍保持性能，达到FID 1.60。

Conclusion: PixSGR通过稀疏全局精炼解决了像素扩散中patch内精炼的结构连续性与长程交互限制，在不付出密集注意力二次方代价的前提下实现了高效的非局部像素级精炼。

Abstract: Pixel-space diffusion has recently emerged as a promising direction for high-fidelity image generation by modeling images directly in the original pixel domain. However, pixel-space diffusion is computationally demanding due to the extremely high dimensionality of natural images. For efficiency, existing pixel diffusion models either compromise fine details with large-patch tokenization or confine subsequent refinement within individual patches. Such intra-patch refinement inevitably restricts structural continuity across patch boundaries and long-range token interactions, limiting refinement quality. To address these issues, we propose PixSGR, a novel Pixel diffusion framework with Sparse Global Refinement tailored for modeling the distribution of natural images directly in pixel space. PixSGR starts from a supervised low-channel bottleneck to efficiently capture the low-dimensional manifold of natural images. It then progressively expands the channel dimensionality and spatial resolution to recover increasingly fine-grained structures. At the spatial refinement stage, coarse-scale attention maps preselect globally relevant interactions to pre-sparsify fine-scale attention, enabling non-local refinement beyond isolated patches without the quadratic cost of dense attention. Extensive experiments on ImageNet validate the effectiveness of PixSGR. It achieves an FID of 1.51 at 256$\times$256 and maintains performance when scaled to 512$\times$512, attaining an FID of 1.60.

</details>


### [45] [ReBridge-Flow: Re-Coupling Posterior Bridges in Flow Matching for Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00811)
*Jiaqi Zhang,Yiqi Wang,Hongjie Wu,Bohan Guo,Xinan Wang,Zichen Luo,Taotao Cai,Zhi Chen,Mingkai Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ReBridge-Flow，一种用于图像复原的后验桥重耦合方法，通过同时锚定干净端并重耦合源端，解决预训练Flow模型中测量约束局部修正导致的端点耦合不兼容问题，在自然和医学图像复原任务上改善了结构一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于Flow Matching的图像复原方法通过局部修正融入测量约束，但这种修正会破坏预训练Flow隐式编码的源-干净端点耦合，导致修正后的端点对与当前状态不兼容，影响复原质量。

Method: ReBridge-Flow首先从当前状态解码对应的局部源端和干净端点，通过干净端锚定融入测量信息，同时同步重耦合源端点，得到具有改进局部桥兼容性的测量感知端点对；重新耦合的端点定义后验信息传输方向以推进采样过程。引入Posterior Bridge Defect联合刻画测量误差、流先验偏差和桥不匹配，并导出干净端锚定与源端重耦合的显式更新。

Result: 在多个自然图像和医学图像复原任务上的大量实验表明，ReBridge-Flow有效缓解了桥不匹配问题，并提升了复原图像的结构一致性。

Conclusion: 通过重新耦合端点而非简单局部修正测量约束，ReBridge-Flow解决了预训练Flow中测量信息融入与端点耦合兼容性之间的矛盾，为基于生成先验的图像复原提供了更鲁棒的采样框架。

Abstract: Flow Matching provides an efficient generative prior for image restoration by learning continuous transport between source and data distributions. However, existing methods typically incorporate measurement constraints through local corrections. Such corrections may disrupt the source-clean endpoint coupling implicitly encoded by the pretrained flow, making the corrected endpoint pair incompatible with the current state. To address this issue, we propose ReBridge-Flow, a posterior bridge re-coupling method. Specifically, given the current state, ReBridge-Flow first decodes the corresponding local source and clean endpoints. It then incorporates measurement information through clean-side anchoring and synchronously re-couples the source endpoint, yielding a measurement-aware endpoint pair with improved local bridge compatibility. The re-coupled endpoints further define a posterior-informed transport direction for advancing the sampling process. We also introduce the Posterior Bridge Defect, which jointly characterizes measurement error, deviation from the flow prior, and bridge mismatch, and leads to explicit updates for clean-side anchoring and source-side re-coupling. Extensive experiments on multiple natural and medical image restoration tasks demonstrate that ReBridge-Flow effectively alleviates bridge mismatch and improves the structural consistency of restored images.

</details>


### [46] [RingMoClaw: An Experience-Inspired Multi-Agent Framework for Self-Evolving Research in Remote Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00814)
*Kaiyue Kang,Qixuan He,Peijin Wang,Yingchao Feng,Chao Ren,Kangxin Wang,Wenhui Diao,Yixiao Wang,Liangjin Zhao,Kaiwen Wei,Nayu Liu,Xian Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RingMoClaw是一个受经验启发的自进化多智能体框架，用于遥感视觉解译。它整合研究分支、质量控制分支和双流动态经验总线，构建了覆盖策略生成、实验执行、独立评审和经验积累的闭环优化流程，在四个遥感下游任务上取得一致性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感视觉模型的改进仍依赖大量人工试错，现有基于智能体的方法主要聚焦任务执行和工作流编排，缺乏自主研究迭代以持续优化性能的能力。

Method: 提出RingMoClaw自进化多智能体框架，包含研究分支、质量控制分支和双流动态经验总线，形成闭环优化流程。采用异构Critic机制进行分阶段诊断与反馈，双流经验总线融合外部知识和内部实验经验以引导策略演化并消除无效搜索。

Result: 在目标检测、场景分类、语义分割和变化检测四个遥感下游任务上验证了有效性。相比基线，目标检测提升1.84% mAP50，其余三个任务也获得一致增益，同时相比现有研究自动化框架减少了超过40%的演化步骤。

Conclusion: RingMoClaw提供了一条从任务执行走向持续研究驱动的模型演进的可行路径，展示了多智能体自主科研循环在遥感领域的潜力。

Abstract: Remote sensing visual models have continuously advanced various interpretation tasks. However, the research process behind model improvement still heavily relies on manual expertise, requiring extensive trial-and-error iterations in model design, data processing, and performance diagnosis. Existing agent-based approaches mainly focus on task execution and workflow orchestration, while lacking the capability of autonomous research iteration for continuous performance optimization. To address this issue, we propose RingMoClaw, an experience-inspired self-evolving multi-agent framework for remote sensing visual interpretation. RingMoClaw integrates a research branch, a quality-control branch, and a dual-stream dynamic experience bus to establish a closed-loop optimization process covering strategy generation, experiment execution, independent review, and experience accumulation. The heterogeneous Critic mechanism provides stage-wise diagnosis and feedback, while the dual-stream experience bus incorporates external knowledge and internal experimental experience to guide strategy evolution and eliminate ineffective searches. Extensive experiments on four remote sensing downstream tasks, including object detection, scene classification, semantic segmentation, and change detection, demonstrate the effectiveness and generalization of RingMoClaw. Compared with the corresponding baseline models, RingMoClaw improves performance by 1.84\% mAP$_{50}$ on object detection and achieves consistent gains across the other three tasks, while reducing the required evolution steps by over 40\% compared with existing research automation frameworks. These results suggest that RingMoClaw offers a feasible route from task execution toward continuous research driven model evolution in remote sensing.

</details>


### [47] [Can Scene Text Recognition Read Rare Compositions?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00816)
*Genpei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 场景文字识别在标准基准上已达89-97%准确率看似饱和，但当测试图像按词频和字符n-gram新颖度分层时，罕见词×罕见trigram角落在13个(语言,模型)对上一致出现10-18pt的性能下降。该下降不是容量瓶颈（模型扩大6倍不改善），而是自回归解码器词汇先验导致的，仅架构切换为CTC解码（+2.5pt, p=0.02）能恢复部分差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示场景文字识别看似饱和下的长尾缺陷：稀有词和稀有字符组合的输入在现有模型上系统性失败，而现有评估基准掩盖了这一缺陷。作者希望定位失败根源并探索可行的缓解方案。

Method: (1) 用参考语料库将测试图像按词频×字符n-gram新颖度划分为5x5网格进行分层分析；(2) 在9种英文识别器和13个(语言,模型)对上验证性能梯度；(3) 将CLIP4STR从Base扩展到Huge（158M→1.0B）测试容量假说；(4) 通过四个探针（分层探针、错时置信度、注意力再平衡、跨文种commit-vs-abstain误差拆分）定位到自回归解码器词汇先验；(5) 测试16种非架构缓解方法及AR→CTC架构切换；(6) 构建置信度路由的AR-CTC集成。

Result: 罕见词×罕见trigram角落比q3/q3中心低10-18pt，13/13个(语言,模型)对方向一致。模型扩容（158M→1.0B）提升聚合准确率但不改善应力角落（86.9→86.5，在噪声内）。16种非架构缓解方案中最大的q5/q5平均增益仅+1.3pt，未超过配对bootstrap噪声下限；唯一的有效干预是从自回归切换到CTC解码（SVTRv2, +2.5pt, p=0.02, n=474）。置信度路由的AR-CTC集成仅增加方向一致的+0.6pt且在噪声内。

Conclusion: 场景文字识别的异常输入长尾是由解码架构（自回归词汇先验）而非模型容量造成的，修复需要架构层面的改变而非增加参数或简单的训练/解码技巧。同样重要的是，没有一种配置能同时改善组合角落和聚合准确率，说明这两个目标需要权衡。

Abstract: Scene text recognition is reported as 89--97% accurate on the six standard benchmarks, and the problem is widely treated as saturated. We present an alternative reading. When the same test images are stratified jointly by ground-truth word rarity and character n-gram novelty against a reference corpus, accuracy at the rare-word x rare-trigram corner of the resulting 5x5 grid drops 10--18 pt below the q3/q3 centre across nine English specialised recognisers, and the same direction (corner below centre) holds on all 13 of 13 (language, model) pairs we test across four writing systems (Latin, Han, Han+kana, Arabic). The drop is not a capacity bottleneck. A 6x vision-backbone scale-up (CLIP4STR-Base 158M -> CLIP4STR-Huge 1.0B, OpenCLIP ViT-H/14 LAION-2B) leads every benchmark in aggregate accuracy yet leaves the stress corner unchanged (86.9 -> 86.5, within paired-bootstrap noise). Four converging probes--layer-wise probing, confidence-when-wrong, attention re-balancing, and a cross-script commit-vs-abstain error split--localise the failure to the autoregressive decoder's lexical prior. We then ask how much of the gap existing techniques recover. Of 16 non-architectural mitigations, the largest mean q5/q5 gain is +1.3 pt and none clears the paired-bootstrap noise floor; the only intervention that does is the architectural shift from autoregressive to CTC decoding (SVTRv2, +2.5 pt, p=0.02, n=474). A confidence-routed AR-CTC ensemble adds a directionally consistent +0.6 pt that stays within noise, and its dominant learned coefficient is each model's own minimum-softmax confidence--independently echoing the mechanism above. No configuration we test improves both the compositional corner and aggregate accuracy. The rare-input long tail thus points to architectural change rather than added capacity.

</details>


### [48] [Visual Attention Faithfulness in Vision-Language Models is Heterogeneous](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00830)
*Xurui Song,Weishi Wang,Zhongqi Yue,Kuluhan Binici,Tao Bai,Hongxin Shao,Daniel Dahlmeier,Jun Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过因果扰动分析探究视觉语言模型（VLM）中注意力权重的忠实性，发现视觉注意力呈现三种处理模式（Faithful-Sufficient、Faithful-Distributed、Non-Focal），并与人类标注的真实区域存在系统性分歧，表明VLM的视觉注意力忠实性随任务需求和架构变化而非统一忠实或不忠实。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NLP领域已广泛关注注意力权重是否忠实反映模型推理，但视觉模态中这一问题在VLM中尚未被充分探索。理解视觉注意力的忠实性对于可解释性和模型诊断至关重要，尤其是当模型处理视觉信息时。

Method: 对当前VLM进行因果扰动分析，通过评价注意力排名视觉token的全面性（comprehensiveness）和充分性（sufficiency）差距来检验视觉注意力忠实性。实验覆盖VQAv2通用VQA任务以及VRDU和ChartQA文档任务，并结合人类标注的ground-truth区域进行对比。

Result: 视觉注意力忠实性是异质的，存在三种处理模式：Faithful-Sufficient（top-k注意力token既必要又充分）、Faithful-Distributed（必要但需更广视觉上下文）、Non-Focal（无局部注意力区域单独必要，但视觉信息是预测的关键触发因素）。人类标注区域仅在约60%的情况下满足全面性，揭示模型视觉依赖与人类直觉的系统性分歧。这些模式随处理需求和模型架构系统变化。

Conclusion: VLM的视觉注意力忠实性并非统一的有或无，而是随任务处理需求和模型架构呈现系统性的异质模式。这一发现为解释VLM视觉推理机制提供了新的分析框架，同时也揭示了模型与人类视觉注意力之间的显著差距。

Abstract: Whether attention weights faithfully reflect model reasoning has been actively debated in NLP, yet this question remains largely unexplored for the visual modality in Vision-Language Models (VLMs). We address this gap through causal perturbation analysis on current VLMs, evaluating both the comprehensiveness and sufficiency gap of attention-ranked visual tokens. Our analysis reveals that visual attention faithfulness is heterogeneous, manifesting in three distinct processing modes: Faithful-Sufficient, where top-$k$ attention tokens are both necessary and sufficient for prediction; Faithful-Distributed, where they are necessary but broader visual context remains required; and Non-Focal, where no localized attention region is individually necessary while visual information remains an essential trigger for prediction. Furthermore, human-annotated ground-truth regions satisfy comprehensiveness in only $\sim 60$% of cases compared with model attention rankings, revealing systematic divergence between model visual reliance and human intuition. We demonstrate these patterns across both general VQA on VQAv2 and document tasks on VRDU and ChartQA, showing that visual attention faithfulness varies systematically with processing demands and model architectures rather than being uniformly faithful or unfaithful.

</details>


### [49] [Residual Kalman Dynamics for Event-Based UAV Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00839)
*Per Nyblom,Hannes Ovrén,David Gustafsson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究FRED事件相机数据集上UAV框的短期与中期预测。以恒定速度卡尔曼滤波器作为强物理基线，训练残差模型从近期框历史、滤波状态特征和局部事件表示中预测类似加速度的修正量。事件条件模型表现最佳，并引入去相关子集作为诊断压力测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升无人机（UAV）目标框的短期与中期预测精度，并剖析已有FRED基线结果中视觉证据与数据集特定运动先验的混淆作用，以更可靠地评估事件相机在预测任务中的真实价值。

Method: 采用全中心-尺寸框状态的恒定速度卡尔曼滤波器作为物理基线；训练残差模型预测加速度类修正。输入包括近期框历史、滤波状态特征以及局部事件表示。为诊断偏差，构造去相关子集以削弱位置和速度捷径，检验事件条件残差模型的鲁棒性。

Result: 简单残差公式持续优于卡尔曼基线；事件条件模型在所有评估方法中表现最强。残差目标的部分可从锚点位置和速度单独预测，说明FRED结果混合了视觉证据与数据集运动先验；去相关测试中事件条件残差模型在捷径减弱时仍保留有效预测信号。

Conclusion: 事件相机表示结合残差Kalman框架可有效提升UAV框预测，并且事件条件模型对数据集特定捷径具有鲁棒性，为后续在去偏差数据集上的事件视觉预测研究提供基础。

Abstract: We study short- and mid-horizon UAV bounding-box forecasting on the FRED event-camera dataset. We use a constant-velocity Kalman filter over a full center-size box state as a strong physical baseline, and train a residual model to predict acceleration-like corrections from recent box history, filtered state features, and local event representations. This simple residual formulation consistently improves over the Kalman baseline, with event-conditioned models giving the strongest results among the evaluated methods. We further show that part of the residual target is predictable from anchor position and velocity alone, indicating that canonical FRED results can reflect both visual evidence and dataset-specific motion priors. To analyze this effect, we introduce decorrelated subsets as a diagnostic stress test, showing that event-conditioned residual models retain useful predictive signal even when measured position- and velocity-based shortcuts are weakened.

</details>


### [50] [An Intelligent Decision Support System for Emotion Monitoring using Microscopic Fixational Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00846)
*Xiangyu Shen,Feiyang Deng,Zijian Dai,Aibin Chen,Jizheng Yi,Jie Li,Hongbo Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于智能眼镜与智能手机的边缘计算智能决策支持系统，通过提取微观视觉注视模式（微扫视、眼动漂移、眼微震颤）实现无侵入、可解释的实时情绪推断，经60名志愿者的严格留一被试交叉验证，个性化F1达83.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情绪监测依赖面部或生理信号，存在侵入性强和隐私问题，需要一种无侵入、隐私友好且可解释的连续情绪推断方案。

Method: 系统从智能眼镜捕捉的注视轨迹中提取并分解三种神经生理学微运动（微扫视、眼动漂移、眼微震颤），通过融合多头注意力、极端梯度提升与支持向量机的可解释混合AI流水线提取深度时间特征、量化生理重要性并在设备端进行分类；同时引入小样本个性化机制以桥接普适生理基线与个体情绪异质性。

Result: 60名志愿者在严格留一被试交叉验证协议下，于受控和自然流动场景中验证框架有效性；消融实验证明微运动比传统宏观特征更具情绪判别力；个性化F1达83.6%。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了生理学可解释、无侵入且可部署的连续实时情绪监测范式，为可穿戴场景下的情感计算提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: The rising prevalence of psychological disorders necessitates effective emotion monitoring, yet current methods relying on facial or physiological signals often suffer from intrusiveness and privacy issues. This paper proposes an intelligent decision support system and pervasive edge-computing framework that leverages smart glasses and a companion smartphone to infer emotional states from microscopic visual fixation patterns. Moving beyond traditional macroscopic gaze metrics, the proposed system extracts and decomposes three distinct neurophysiological micro-movements: microsaccades, ocular drifts, and ocular microtremors. We introduce an interpretable hybrid artificial intelligence pipeline combining a multi-head attention mechanism, extreme gradient boosting, and a support vector machine to extract deep temporal features, quantify their physiological importance, and perform efficient on-device classification. Through an extensive evaluation involving 60 volunteers, we rigorously validate the framework under a strict leave-one-subject-out cross-validation protocol across both controlled and naturalistic mobile scenarios. Ablation studies unequivocally demonstrate that these fixational micro-movements are substantially more discriminative for emotion inference than traditional macroscopic features. Furthermore, aligned with contemporary affective science, the system incorporates a few-shot personalization mechanism to bridge universal physiological baselines with individual emotional heterogeneity, achieving a highly robust personalized F1-score of 83.6%. This work establishes a physiologically interpretable, unobtrusive, and deployable paradigm for continuous real-time emotion monitoring.

</details>


### [51] [ADGNet: Asymmetric Dual-text Guided Network for Infrared Small Target Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00853)
*Tongtong Wang,Mingzhu Xu,Chenglong Yu,Jing Wang,Xiaohui Lin,Weili Guan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ADGNet，一种面向红外小目标检测的非对称双文本引导网络。针对现有多模态方法用单一文本提示同时描述目标与背景、缺乏区域引导且忽视红外语义非对称性的问题，设计了图像无关的抽象目标提示和图像特定的详细背景提示组成的非对称双文本提示（ADP），并配合非对称双分支交互（ADBI）模块和自适应特征聚合（AFA）模块，在21个SOTA方法中取得最佳性能。还构建了AITIR多模态数据集（对三个公开数据集进行非对称文本标注）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 红外小目标检测中，视觉-only方法仅靠像素信息难以区分目标与杂波。现有多模态方法用单一文本提示同时描述目标和背景，缺乏区域级引导，忽略了红外语义的非对称性（目标小且缺乏纹理，背景复杂多变），导致背景抑制信息不足且特征优化冲突严重，使小目标被噪声淹没。

Method: （1）提出非对称双文本提示ADP：图像无关的抽象目标提示+图像特定的详细背景提示，匹配红外语义非对称性。（2）设计非对称双分支交互ADBI模块，分别用各自的文本先验引导视觉特征，保护目标免受噪声干扰同时抑制背景杂波。（3）提出自适应特征聚合AFA模块动态融合两个分支的特征。（4）构建AITIR多模态数据集，为IRSTD-1K、NUDT-SIRST、SIRST提供非对称文本标注。

Result: 在三个公开红外小目标检测数据集上，ADGNet优于21个SOTA方法，代码已开源。

Conclusion: 非对称双文本提示策略能有效解决红外小目标多模态检测中背景抑制不足和特征优化冲突的问题，验证了在红外感知中考虑语义非对称性的重要性。

Abstract: InfRared Small Target Detection (IRSTD) is a challenging task. Relying solely on pixel-level information, vision-only methods struggle to distinguish targets from clutter. Current multimodal methods typically describe both targets and backgrounds with a single textual prompt. Such an approach lacks dedicated regional guidance and ignores infrared semantic asymmetry. Consequently, it provides insufficient background suppression information and introduces severe feature optimization conflicts, overwhelming small targets with noise. To address these issues, we propose a novel Asymmetric Dual-text Guided Network (ADGNet). Specifically, accounting for the infrared semantic asymmetry, we first design the Asymmetric Dual-text Prompt (ADP), comprising an image-agnostic abstract target prompt and an image-specific detailed background prompt. To leverage these prompts, we introduce an Asymmetric Dual-Branch Interaction (ADBI) module to separately guide visual features with their respective text priors, protecting targets from noise while suppressing background clutter. Subsequently, we introduce an Adaptive Feature Aggregation (AFA) module to dynamically fuse features from the two branches. Furthermore, we construct a multimodal Asymmetric Image-Text Infrared (AITIR) dataset by providing asymmetric text annotations for three public datasets (IRSTD-1K, NUDT-SIRST, and SIRST). Extensive experiments demonstrate that ADGNet outperforms 21 state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods. Code is available at https://github.com/iLearn-Lab/MM26-ADGNet.

</details>


### [52] [Training-Free Inpainting Across Domains with a Frozen Text-to-Image Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00862)
*Zhenhuan Wang,Fengyi Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Step-PI方法，通过测试时的潜在空间控制（已知区域投影增强、边界-内部潜在反馈、持久PI状态及预定义四阶段释放调度），使冻结的通用文生图扩散模型无需任何针对inpainting的训练或适配即可跨三个自然图像域进行条件inpainting。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练式inpainting系统虽指标领先但依赖大量针对inpainting的离线优化和权重新训练；而训练自由的inpainting方法（如LanPaint、PILOT）性能不足。本文旨在探索能否仅通过测试时的潜在控制，将冻结的通用文生图扩散模型直接复用于跨域条件inpainting，避免inpainting特定训练与领域适配。

Method: Step-PI在冻结扩散模型的reverse trajectory上：1）用已知区域投影（known-region projection）保持已知像素；2）增加边界-内部潜在反馈（boundary-interior latent feedback）；3）维护持久PI状态（persistent PI state）；4）采用预定义的四字段释放调度（four-field release schedule），沿逆向轨迹调制控制器信号，替代均匀释放。控制器仅在CelebA-HQ开发，直接迁移到AFHQ和Places2。

Result: 在3,500个case上，两个field-identical对比中，增加持久状态及替换均匀释放为预定义调度均改善全部15个数据集-指标单元；所有五个指标的95% bootstrap区间均排除零。与使用vanilla SD1.5的训练自由基线（LanPaint、PILOT）相比，Step-PI在全部五个等数据集macro指标上领先。inpainting训练系统仍保持绝对指标领先但依赖大量inpainting特定离线优化。

Conclusion: Step-PI提供了一种互补方法：通过测试时潜在控制将冻结的通用文生图扩散模型复用于跨域inpainting，证明了无需inpainting特定权重训练或领域适配即可实现领域泛化。

Abstract: We show that a frozen generic text-to-image diffusion model can perform conditional inpainting across three evaluated natural-image domains with one fixed controller configuration, without inpainting-specific weight training, dataset-specific weight adaptation, or learned inpainting-specific conditioning channels. Step-PI augments known-region projection with boundary-interior latent feedback, persistent PI state, and a predefined four-field release schedule that modulates controller signals along the reverse trajectory. Developed only on Main35-disjoint CelebA-HQ pilots, the controller transfers unchanged to AFHQ and Places2. Across two field-identical comparisons on the same 3,500 cases, adding persistent state and replacing uniform release with the predefined schedule each improve all 15 dataset-metric cells; 95% bootstrap intervals exclude zero for all five metrics in both comparisons. In descriptive native-route comparisons, Step-PI leads LanPaint and PILOT (the closest evaluated training-free baselines using vanilla SD1.5) on all five equal-dataset macro metrics. Inpainting-trained systems retain the absolute metric leads but rely on substantial inpainting-specific offline optimization. Our method provides a complementary approach for repurposing a frozen generic text-to-image model for cross-domain inpainting through test-time latent control.

</details>


### [53] [Benchmarking Vision-Language Models for Automated Pathology Diagnosis and Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00866)
*Yumi Lee,Harim Oh,Hyoryung Kim,Minji Kim,Eunsu Kim,Hyeseong Lee,Junya Fukuoka,Andrey Bychkov,Jijgee Munkhdelger,Rajiv Kumar Kaushal,Ayushi Sahay,Rajni Yadav,Bharathi Prabakaran,Sulen Sarioglu,Serdar Balcı,Ilknur Turkmen,Yuri Tolkach,Christian Harder,Julian Westerdorf,Reinhard Buettner,Audun Ljone Henriksen,Sepp De Raedt,Byung Hyun Lee,Sungjin Lim,Joohoon Lee,Gwanghyun Kim,Se Young Chun,Suryakant Singh,Saarthak Kapse,Prateek Prasanna,Kyung A Kim,Yousun Kang,Sehwan Yoo,Sungman Hong,Shubham Innani,Michael Feldman,Spyridon Bakas,Ujjwal Baid,Prasad Dutande,Suhas Gajare,Bhakti Baheti,Serkan Sökmen,Ece Tuğba Cebeci,Ahmet Halıcı,Musa Balcı,Kardelen Peçenek,Srividhya Sainath,Kyongseok Jang,Messi H. J. Lee,Noorul Wahab,Bodong Du,Jiaming Zhang,Qixiang Zhang,Jang-Hwan Choi,Sangjeong Ahn*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了约10,500对WSI-报告的泛亚病理学数据集，并通过MICCAI挑战赛建立了REG 2025基准，系统评估了多种多模态模型在病理报告生成中的表现，发现结构化报告表示、层次化诊断分解和多模态接地是取得优异性能的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算病理学中基于WSI的报告生成受限于缺乏大规模WSI-报告配对数据集，以及空间分布视觉模式到结构化临床文本映射的复杂性。

Method: 构建了来自五个机构的约10,500对临床标注的Pan-Asia WSI-报告数据集，通过MICCAI挑战赛建立REG 2025基准，系统分析多种方法（包括预训练VLM、多实例学习、层次化专家模型、检索增强生成和跨模态Transformer）。

Result: 结果显示单纯依赖VLM并不足以获得优越性能；顶尖方法受益于结构化报告表示、层次化诊断分解和有效的多模态接地。同时识别出关键局限：定量属性估计不稳定（如数值幻觉）以及诊断过度指定倾向。

Conclusion: REG 2025可作为评估WSI结构化报告生成和视觉语言理解的基准，为设计临床实用型多模态病理模型提供见解。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of vision-language models (VLMs) has accelerated progress in computational pathology; however, whole-slide image (WSI)-based pathology report generation remains limited by the scarcity of large-scale WSI--report datasets and the complexity of mapping spatially distributed visual patterns to structured clinical text. To address this, we introduce a clinically curated Pan-Asia WSI--report dataset of approximately 10,500 pairs from five institutions and establish the REG 2025 benchmark through a MICCAI challenge for systematic evaluation of multimodal models. We analyze submitted methods spanning pretrained VLMs, multiple-instance learning frameworks, hierarchical expert models, retrieval-augmented generation, and cross-modal Transformers. Rather than indicating that VLM use alone was sufficient for superior performance, the results suggest that top-performing methods benefited from structured report representations, hierarchical diagnostic decomposition, and effective multimodal grounding. We identify key limitations, including instability in quantitative attribute estimation (e.g., numeric hallucination) and a tendency toward diagnostic overspecification, with some errors resembling known diagnostic pitfalls in routine pathology. These findings establish REG 2025 as a benchmark for evaluating WSI-based structured report generation and vision-language understanding in computational pathology, providing insights for the design of clinically grounded multimodal pathology models.

</details>


### [54] [The Visual Insensitivity Gap: Diagnosing When Vision-Language Models Fail to Use Visual Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00868)
*Genpei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现视觉-语言模型在多模态基准上的评估隐含假设——模型使用了视觉输入——在40%-97%的样本上不成立：模糊问题相关视觉区域后，next-token分布几乎不变。作者将此命名为视觉不敏感性缺口（Visual Insensitivity Gap），并用逐样本的视觉敏感性指数（VSI）量化。VSI是样本属性而非模型属性（跨模型排名相关rho=+0.40），机制上源于视觉编码器与LLM之间的信息断层。VSI在强推理模型的多选推理上有诊断价值（AUROC=0.85-0.87），但并非普适的最优弃权信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM评估假设模型确实使用了视觉输入，但这一假设在大量样本上失效。需要系统化地量化并定位这种视觉忽略失败，以改进评估方法和模型诊断。

Method: 提出逐样本Visual Sensitivity Index（VSI）：模糊问题相关视觉区域后测量next-token分布变化。在6个VLM和3个感知基准上系统测量，并用线性探针分析视觉编码器与LLM之间的信息差距，通过细胞级别的VSI诊断效用分析（结合模型类型、任务类型）识别VSI的强/弱适用场景。

Result: 40%-97%样本对视觉扰动不敏感；VSI跨模型排名相关rho=+0.40（p<10^-3），为样本固有属性；视觉塔线性探针能区分扰动/干净图像（0.72-0.79），但模型argmax词仅变化2%-11%，编码器-LLM差距超过0.65；VSI在强模型多选推理上AUROC达0.85-0.87，但在校准事实性任务上不如softmax置信度。

Conclusion: VSI不是通用的最优弃权信号，而是样本内在的视觉忽略失败指标，应作为条件化集成组件使用。该工作为理解VLM的视觉利用失败提供了可量化的诊断工具，揭示了视觉编码器与LLM之间的系统性信息断层。

Abstract: Vision-language models are evaluated by aggregate accuracy on multimodal benchmarks, a practice that implicitly assumes the model uses its visual input. We show this assumption fails on 40%--97% of samples across six VLMs and three perceptual benchmarks: blurring the question-relevant visual region leaves the next-token distribution nearly unchanged. We name this phenomenon the Visual Insensitivity Gap and quantify it with a per-sample Visual Sensitivity Index (VSI). The gap is a property of samples, not of models: VSI ranks correlate across models (grand-mean Spearman rho=+0.40, permutation p<10^-3), so the same samples are flagged insensitive by VLMs sharing no architectural detail beyond a contrastively pretrained vision tower. The mechanism is concrete: on the insensitive samples, a linear probe on each model's own vision tower distinguishes perturbed from clean images at 0.72--0.79 accuracy, yet the model's argmax token changes on only 2%--11% of the same samples, an encoder--LLM gap above 0.65 on every model. Mapping VSI's diagnostic utility cell by cell surfaces a strong regime (multi-choice reasoning on capable VLMs: AUROC=0.85--0.87) and a weak regime (well-calibrated factuality, where softmax confidence already leads). VSI is not a universal best abstention signal; it is a sample-intrinsic indicator of vision-ignoring failure, best used as a conditional ensemble component.

</details>


### [55] [Vision-Language-Guided Pseudo-Labels for Unsupervised Domain Adaptation in Semantic Segmentation for Waste Sorting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00898)
*Udo Schlegel,Shubhangi,Gabriel Dax,Sai Rahul Kaminwar,Florian Karl,Thomas Seidl*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于跨模态伪标签的无监督域自适应分割管线，利用SAM生成区域提案、EVA-CLIP分配语义标签、置信度过滤保证伪标签可靠性，并可选BLIP进行语言验证。在合成到真实自动驾驶和实验室到工厂垃圾分选两类域迁移中均优于源域基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义分割在自动驾驶、工业垃圾分选等实际应用中获取标注数据成本高昂且难以规模化，需要无需目标域标注的无监督域自适应方法来自动化标注。

Method: 构建pseudo-labeling管线：(1) SAM生成类无关的区域提案；(2) EVA-CLIP基于区域-文本相似度分配语义标签；(3) 置信度过滤确保只有可靠伪标签用于自训练分割模型；(4) 可选BLIP对模糊区域进行语言接地验证以提升伪标签质量。

Result: 在两个域迁移设置（合成到真实自动驾驶、实验室到工厂垃圾分选）上，该方法持续优于source-only基线。实验表明伪标签质量而非数量是域迁移下自训练的决定性因素。

Conclusion: 跨模态语言接地为部署关键应用中可靠自动标注提供了实用路径，无需目标域任何标注即可实现域自适应分割。

Abstract: Obtaining labeled data for semantic segmentation in applied settings (e.g., autonomous driving, industrial waste sorting) is expensive and often infeasible at scale. We present a cross-modal pseudo-labeling pipeline that enables unsupervised domain adaptation without any target-domain annotations. The pipeline is built on two core foundation models: SAM generates class-agnostic region proposals, and EVA-CLIP assigns semantic labels based on region-text similarity, with confidence filtering ensuring that only reliable pseudo-labels are used for self-training a segmentation model. As an optional extension, BLIP provides language-grounded verification for ambiguous regions, thereby improving pseudo-label quality without altering the overall pipeline. Evaluated on two domain shifts, synthetic-to-real autonomous driving and, with a primary focus, lab-to-factory industrial waste sorting, the pipeline consistently improves over source-only baselines. Our results demonstrate that pseudo-label quality, not quantity, is a decisive factor in self-training under domain shift, and that cross-modal language grounding offers a practical path to reliable automatic annotation in deployment-critical applications.

</details>


### [56] [HELIOS: From midnight to noon, continuous outdoor urban scene relighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00901)
*Hala Djeghim,Nathan Piasco,Luis Roldão,Moussab Bennehar,Dzmitry Tsishkou,Céline Loscos,Désiré Sidibé*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HELIOS提出了一种无需配对图像的真实世界图像重打光方法，利用基于反照率的条件约束和循环一致性扩散管道，结合GPS太阳角度进行精细光照控制，在夜间与白天图像互相转换任务中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大部分驾驶图像数据集在特定时间段采集，现有重打光方法依赖合成数据或配对多光照监督，难以泛化到真实世界的多样和挑战性条件。

Method: 将基于反照率的条件约束集成到循环一致性扩散管道中以防止身份坍塌并确保准确的域转换；提出鲁棒反照率蒸馏策略以从白天域传递结构稳定性来处理低可见度夜间条件；用基于GPS太阳角度的细粒度控制机制替代传统文本提示，实现在昼夜周期上的平滑连续光照操控。

Result: 在夜间到白天和白天到夜间任务中均产生结构一致且逼真的结果，通过广泛评估和用户研究证明优于最先进方法。

Conclusion: HELIOS证明无需配对数据即可实现高质量的真实世界图像重打光，为驾驶场景多光照泛化提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Modifying the illumination of driving images is a fundamental challenge, as most datasets are captured at specific times of day. Existing methods rely on synthetic data or paired multi-illumination supervision, which limits their generalization to the diverse and challenging conditions of real-world scenarios. To address this, we propose HELIOS, a novel image relighting approach that relies on unlabeled real-world datasets without requiring any paired images for training. Our approach integrates albedo-based conditioning into a cycle-consistent diffusion pipeline to prevent identity collapse and ensure accurate domain translation. To handle low-visibility nighttime conditions, we introduce a robust albedo distillation strategy that transfers structural stability from the daytime domain. Additionally, we replace traditional text prompts with a fine-grained control mechanism based on GPS-derived solar angles, enabling smooth and continuous lighting manipulation across the day-night cycle. Through extensive evaluation and a user study, we demonstrate that HELIOS produces structurally consistent and realistic results in both night-to-day and day-to-night tasks, outperforming state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [57] [A multicenter benchmark and clinically structured metric for coronary CTA report generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00909)
*Zhiyu Ye,Yue Sun,Limiao Zou,Cheng Xu,Keting Xu,Tong Hu,Yue Yu,Hairong Zheng,Yining Wang,Tong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文建立了包含4个中心、3021个CCTA序列、818对患者-报告的标准化基准，并提出临床结构化评估指标CSM_CCTA（与放射科医生评分Pearson r=0.97），用于评估7个开源三维视觉语言模型在冠状动脉CT血管造影报告生成中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化CCTA报告生成的可靠评估需要标准化的多中心基准和临床结构化指标，现有方法缺乏统一评估框架和临床对齐的度量标准。

Method: 建立了四中心基准（3021个CCTA序列），提出CSM_CCTA临床结构化评估指标，基于临床指南定义患者、血管、节段三级变量，在最细共享解剖层级比较报告对，并通过70例专家评分估算各临床组件权重，30例非重叠病例验证临床对齐性。

Result: CSM_CCTA与放射科医生评分强相关（r=0.97），比次优指标FORTE高0.27；160次成对比较中115次符合专家偏好（71.9%）。在基准中C2RG模型在所有四家医院取得最高分但远非最优，通用模型输出中CCTA无关报告占比高达98.7%。

Conclusion: 该基准提供了标准化的模型比较场景，CSM_CCTA实现了临床结构化的发现一致性和解剖特异性评估，支持更临床对齐、解剖解析的CCTA报告生成评估方法。

Abstract: Reliable evaluation of automated coronary computed tomography angiography (CCTA) report generation requires standardized multicentre benchmarks and clinically structured metrics. We established a four-centre benchmark comprising 3,021 CCTA series from 818 patient-report pairs to evaluate seven open-source three-dimensional vision-language models. We developed CSM$_{\text{CCTA}}$, a clinically structured metric for CCTA report evaluation, with patient-, vessel-, and segment-level variables defined according to clinical guidelines. Report pairs are compared at the finest shared anatomical level, and the contributions of different clinical components are weighted based on expert assessments. We estimated these weights using 70 expert-scored cases and evaluated clinical alignment in a non-overlapping set of 30 cases. CSM$_{\text{CCTA}}$ showed a strong correlation with radiologist scores (Pearson's $r=0.97$, $p<0.001$), exceeding the next-best metric, FORTE ($r=0.70$), by 0.27, and agreed with expert preferences in 115 of 160 pairwise comparisons (71.9\%). Under controlled perturbations, CSM$_{\text{CCTA}}$ remained stable to clinically equivalent wording and decreased monotonically with progressive information omission. In the multicenter benchmark, the CCTA-trained C2RG model achieved the highest CSM$_{\text{CCTA}}$ scores across all four hospitals, although its performance remained far from optimal. In contrast, CCTA-irrelevant reports accounted for up to 98.7\% of the outputs from generalist models. Together, the benchmark provides a standardized setting for model comparison, while CSM$_{\text{CCTA}}$ enables clinically structured evaluation of finding agreement and anatomical specificity. These results support a more clinically aligned and anatomically resolved approach to evaluating CCTA report generation. Code is available at https://openi.pcl.ac.cn/OpenMedIA/CSM_CCTA.

</details>


### [58] [On-the-Fly3R: Towards Robust Online 3D Reconstruction with Feed-Forward 3R Models for Large-Scale UAV Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00923)
*Zhe Shen,Liyuan Lou,Yifei Yu,Guanbo Wang,Quanjian Ji,Xin Wang,Zongqian Zhan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了On-the-Fly3R，一个无需训练的渐进式在线3D重建框架，通过检索引导的动态子集构建、验证-拒绝-重试机制和基于回环检测的位姿图优化，将前馈3D重建方法扩展到大规模、无任意顺序的无人机图像重建场景，支持超过5000张图像的平方公里级场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈3D重建方法受限于Transformer注意力的显存开销，无法应用于大规模无人机测绘；而现有可扩展流式3D方法假设输入在时间和空间上连续，无法处理跨航带无人机作业中常见的弱有序或无有序图像流。

Method: 1) 检索引导的动态子集构建：从无序输入中自适应选择空间相关的图像子集；2) 验证-拒绝-重试机制：预集成一致性检查，自动拒绝未对齐图像并重试替代子集，保证全局一致性；3) 受VSLAM启发，基于检索回环的位姿图优化来缓解相机漂移。

Result: 在多个无人机基准上，On-the-Fly3R成功将多种3R模型扩展到超过5000张图像的平方公里级无人机场景，相比多个SOTA流式3R方法在精度上有大幅提升。

Conclusion: 通过训练自由的框架设计，有效解决了前馈3D重建在真实大规模无人机测绘中的可扩展性问题，为无序无人机图像流的在线3D重建提供了可行方案。

Abstract: While feed-forward 3D reconstruction (3R) offers efficient end-to-end modeling, its application in large-scale UAV mapping is hindered by the prohibitive memory cost of Transformer attention. Current scalable streaming 3R methods assume temporally and spatially continuous inputs, rendering them ineffective for the weakly ordered or unordered image streams common in cross-strip UAV operations. To address this, we propose On-the-Fly3R, a training-free, progressive online 3D reconstruction framework for large-scale UAV images that upgrades various 3R backbones for large-scale UAV scenarios. Our method enables reconstruction from unordered inputs via retrieval-guided dynamic subset construction, which adaptively selects spatially relevant images. To further improve the robustness, a validation-rejection-retry mechanism is designed to guarantee global consistency, performing a pre-integration consistency check and automatically rejecting misaligned images and retrying with alternative subset. Finally, inspired by VSLAM, pose graph optimization based on the retrieval loop closure is employed to mitigate camera drift. Evaluations on several UAV benchmarks show that our On-the-Fly3R successfully scales various 3R models to over 5,000 images across square-kilometer UAV scenes, delivering substantially superior accuracy compared to several SOTA streaming 3R methods. Code is available at https://github.com/Sh1nZzz/On_the_Fly3R

</details>


### [59] [Beyond the Image Plane: World-Grounded Queries for Multi-Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00924)
*Orcun Cetintas,Guillem Brasó,Tim Meinhardt,Laura Leal-Taixé*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PLANET是一个端到端多目标跟踪器，将2D跟踪提升到3D世界空间：通过将重建的3D场景几何嵌入查询构造的特征和位置编码中，并引入辅助3D位置预测任务与双分辨率时序记忆，在两个2D跟踪数据集基础上提升3D标注，在三个基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目视频本质上是3D场景在2D图像平面上的投影，丢失了深度与空间关系信息。现有多目标跟踪器主要在图像平面上利用外观和几何进行关联，继承了这一歧义，缺乏3D世界感知能力。

Method: (1) 将现有2D跟踪数据集提升为3D标注作为启用步骤；(2) 将重建的3D场景几何嵌入到查询形成的特征与位置编码中，形成world-grounded查询；(3) 引入辅助3D位置预测任务，训练时激励查询编码目标位置；(4) 采用互补的双分辨率时序记忆以跨更长时间间隙保留证据。

Result: PLANET在三个多样化的基准上达到最先进水平（SOTA）。

Conclusion: 通过将场景3D几何显式注入查询并配合3D位置监督，可以超越图像平面表达限制，显著提升多目标跟踪的世界感知能力与性能。

Abstract: Monocular videos record 3D scenes as sequences of 2D image-plane projections, obscuring depth and spatial relationships. Multi-object trackers localize and associate objects primarily using appearance and geometry observed only in the image plane, inheriting these ambiguities. To address this limitation, we introduce PLANET, an end-to-end multi-object tracker designed to move beyond the image plane. As an enabling step, we lift existing 2D tracking datasets into 3D. We then form world-grounded queries by embedding reconstructed 3D scene geometry into the features and positional encodings used during query formation. An auxiliary 3D location prediction task further encourages the queries to encode object positions during training. A complementary dual-resolution temporal memory preserves this evidence across longer temporal gaps. As a result, PLANET achieves state-of-the-art performance across three diverse benchmarks.

</details>


### [60] [From Terminology to Diagrams: Visual-Instruction Generation for Scientific Diagram Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00948)
*Raul Ortega,José Manuel Gómez-Pérez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SciGram框架，利用科学课程术语系统化提取领域概念、合成原子事实、检索网络图表并生成图文指令数据，构建了包含194K图表和1.4M视觉指令的数据集，微调后显著提升TQA、ScienceQA和AI2D等图表推理基准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在自然图像上表现优异，但在科学图表上仍存在明显不足，因为图表旨在传达功能或关系性含义而非字面场景。现有方法缺乏大规模、高质量的图表图文指令数据来训练模型理解科学图表。

Method: 提出术语锚定的指令数据生成框架：从科学课程中提取领域概念→合成原子事实→从网络检索相关图表→生成多模态监督信号（图表标题和多项选择题），构建SciGram数据集，涵盖生命、地球和物理科学。

Result: 尽管依赖噪声网络数据和合成标注，微调后的模型在TQA、ScienceQA和AI2D等图表基准上取得显著提升，超越或匹敌SOTA VLM且使用更少训练样本。将SciGram与LLaVA OneVision结合刷新了图表问答SOTA。

Conclusion: 术语锚定的指令生成为改善科学领域视觉语言推理提供了通用且有效的策略，数据集与模型已开放以支持后续研究。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have demonstrated strong performance in visual question answering with natural images. However, they continue to struggle with scientific diagrams, which are designed to convey functional or relational meaning rather than literal scenes. We therefore introduce a framework for generating large-scale diagram-grounded instruction data by leveraging terminology derived from scientific curricula. Our approach systematically extracts domain concepts, synthesizes atomic facts, retrieves relevant diagrams from the web, and generates multimodal supervision in the form of diagram captions and multiple-choice questions. Using this pipeline, we construct SciGram, a dataset of over 194K diagrams and 1.4M visual instructions across life, earth, and physical sciences. Despite relying on noisy web data and synthetic annotations, models fine-tuned on SciGram achieve substantial improvements on diagram-centric benchmarks, including TQA, ScienceQA, and AI2D, outperforming or matching state-of-the-art VLMs while using fewer training instances. Furthermore, augmenting existing models such as LLaVA OneVision with SciGram establishes new state-of-the-art performance on diagram question answering. Our results highlight the effectiveness of terminology-grounded instruction generation as a general strategy for improving vision-language reasoning in scientific domains. To support future research in scientific diagram understanding, we release both the SciGram dataset and models.

</details>


### [61] [CERF: Communication-Efficient and Retraining-Free Collaborative Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00951)
*Jiuwu Hao,Ziyi Ni,Liguo Sun,Yuting Wan,Yueyang Wu,Ti Xiang,Haolin Song,Pin Lv*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CERF提出了一种通信高效且无需重新训练的开放异构协同感知框架。通过引入名为"Poture"的虚拟模态（从其他智能体的感知输出生成）来增强自车BEV特征，并使用基于卡尔曼滤波的跟踪器和运动预测模型补偿传输延迟，在多种下游任务上将通信开销降低95%，同时性能接近主流中间层协同方法，并支持无缝集成未知异构智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协同感知方法大多依赖稠密特征图的传输与融合，带来不可避免的通信开销和异构性挑战，限制了实际部署。需要一种通信高效、能处理开放异构智能体且无需重新训练的方法。

Method: 1) 提出新的虚拟模态"Poture"，由其他智能体的感知输出生成，用于增强自车BEV特征；2) 采用基于卡尔曼滤波的跟踪器和运动预测模型，从历史感知结果推导当前预测以缓解传输延迟；3) 设计无需重训练的异构智能体集成机制。

Result: 在多种下游任务上性能与主流中间层协同方法相当，同时将通信开销降低95%；支持未知异构智能体无额外训练成本地无缝集成到现有协同框架中。

Conclusion: CERF证明通过虚拟模态和运动预测可以有效替代稠密特征图的传输，在保持感知性能的同时大幅减少通信成本，并为开放异构协同感知提供了轻量级、即插即用的解决方案。

Abstract: Collaborative perception shares information among multiple agents to obtain a comprehensive scene representation, enhancing the perceptual capability of individual agents. However, most existing methods rely on transmitting and fusing dense feature maps for collaboration, which incurs inevitable communication overhead and heterogeneity challenges, limiting their practicality for real-world deployment. To address these challenges, we propose CERF, a novel Communication-Efficient and Retraining-Free framework for open heterogeneous collaborative perception. In CERF, we introduce a new virtual modality (termed Poture), which is generated from the perception outputs of other agents, to augment the extracted Bird's Eye View (BEV) features of the ego agent. To mitigate transmission delays, we employ a Kalman-filter based tracker and a motion forecasting model to derive the current predictions from historical perception results. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CERF achieves performance comparable to mainstream intermediate-collaboration methods while reducing communication overhead by 95% across various downstream tasks. Furthermore, CERF enables seamless integration of unknown heterogeneous agents into the existing collaborative framework without additional retraining costs. Code is available at https://github.com/uestchjw/CERF.

</details>


### [62] [ASSERT: Adaptive Stochastic Sampling for Robust Diffusion Models on Analog Compute-in-Memory Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00955)
*Yuannuo Feng,Yizhe Chen,Wenshuai Yao,Yuxin Xie,Ngai Wong,Wenyong Zhou,Wang Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究扩散模型在模拟存内计算(CIM)硬件上的推理鲁棒性。通过多块物理CIM芯片校准的噪声模型，发现早期高噪声去噪阶段对硬件空间变化（权重扰动）最敏感，原因是固定硬件映射导致的预测误差相关传播。据此提出ASSERT，一种免训练采样器，早期注入更高随机性并平滑过渡到确定性去噪，通过改变激活轨迹减少与持久空间误差的对齐。在多个设置中FID最多降低2.58倍（高分辨率数据集）和7.68倍（CIFAR-10步数研究），且不改变模型参数或网络评估次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟CIM硬件可加速矩阵-向量乘法，但其空间存储变化会扰动权重并在采样过程中累积。与常规神经网络不同，扩散模型对硬件噪声的时序敏感性尚未被充分研究。理解哪一阶段的去噪对硬件噪声最脆弱，以及如何在不改模型的前提下缓解，是推动扩散模型在CIM硬件上高效部署的关键问题。

Method: 1) 构建基于多块物理CIM芯片测量的校准噪声模型，用于扩散推理研究。2) 通过一阶轨迹分析揭示：固定硬件映射导致的相关预测误差在早期高噪声阶段反复传播，解释其高脆弱性。3) 提出ASSERT，一个免训练的采样器：早期使用较高随机性（更大随机扰动），后期平滑过渡到确定性DDIM去噪，从而改变激活轨迹使其与持久空间误差的排列对齐度降低。

Result: ASSERT在高分辨率数据集上相比确定性DDIM将FID降低最多2.58倍；在CIFAR-10步数研究中FID降低最多7.68倍。方法不需要改变模型参数或网络评估次数，完全免训练。

Conclusion: 扩散模型在CIM硬件上的硬件噪声脆弱性集中在早期去噪阶段，原因是固定硬件映射导致的相关误差反复传播。通过时序自适应地注入随机性（早期高、后期低），可以显著缓解硬件噪声对生成质量的影响，为在模拟CIM硬件上部署扩散模型提供了一种高效且免训练的解决方案。

Abstract: Diffusion models achieve strong image generation quality but incur high iterative denoising costs. Analog compute-in-memory (CIM) can accelerate matrix-vector multiplications, yet spatial memory variations perturb weights and accumulate during sampling. Unlike conventional neural networks, diffusion models' temporal sensitivity to hardware noise remains underexplored. We investigate diffusion inference using a noise model calibrated and validated against measurements collected from multiple physical CIM chips. Our results show that the early, high-noise denoising stage is substantially more vulnerable than the final refinement stage. A first-order trajectory analysis attributes this behavior to the repeated propagation of correlated prediction errors induced by a fixed hardware mapping. Based on this observation, we propose ASSERT, a training-free sampler that uses higher stochasticity early and smoothly transitions to deterministic denoising. The injected stochasticity changes subsequent activation trajectories and thereby reduces their alignment with persistent spatial errors. Across the evaluated settings, ASSERT achieves up to 2.58$\times$ lower FID than deterministic DDIM on high-resolution datasets and 7.68$\times$ lower FID in the CIFAR-10 step-count study, without changing model parameters or the number of network evaluations.

</details>


### [63] [PredErase: Training-Free Object-and-Effect Removal with Predictive Latent Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00956)
*Waikit Xiu,Qiang Lu,Junbiao Chen,Xiying Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PredErase是一种无需训练的推理过程，基于冻结的FLUX.2和I-JEPA模型，通过接触带扩展与掩码token预测来联合擦除物体及其投影阴影和接触阴影，解决传统Freeze-Fill方法只编辑实例掩码而遗漏物体光度足迹的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的训练免费编辑方法将实例掩码M_obj视为全部可编辑区域，但物体的投影阴影和接触阴影位于掩码之外，导致物体被移除后其光度痕迹仍残留在支撑平面上。监督式擦除器需要配对数据，而冻结模型没有有效的机制预测被遮挡场景，需要一种无需训练的联合擦除方法。

Method: PredErase将两件事分离：Fill在何处可以重写像素，以及洞中应填充什么结构。1) 通过接触带扩展M_flux暴露支撑平面上的局部残差；2) 利用预训练用于掩码token预测的I-JEPA提供上下文条件的洞目标表征；3) 在实例内部施加稀疏投影梯度使解码的Fill完成结果对齐该目标，同时锁定打包支持区域外的坐标。

Result: 在RemovalBench、RORD-Val和DEFACTO-Val数据集上，仅使用实例掩码时，PredErase优于原生FLUX.2主干。在部分全图外观指标上监督式擦除器仍更强，PredErase的定位是无训练的对象及效应联合编辑，而非替代配对数据擦除器。

Conclusion: PredErase证明了训练免费的Fill模型可以联合擦除物体及其光度足迹（投影阴影和接触阴影），通过I-JEPA的表征空间引导实现对被遮挡场景的合理预测，是一种有效的无训练对象擦除方案，但不能完全取代监督式方法。

Abstract: Removing an object is not the same as filling its mask. Cast shadows and contact shading usually lie outside the user-provided instance mask M_obj, so a frozen Fill model that edits only that mask leaves the object's photometric footprint on nearby surfaces. Supervised removers learn this joint erasure from paired clean plates. Training-free editors freeze pretrained weights, yet most still treat M_obj as the entire editable support and steer sampling with CLIP or DINO energies that do not predict the occluded scene. We present PredErase, a training-free inference procedure on frozen FLUX.2 and I-JEPA. The method separates where Fill may rewrite pixels from what structure should occupy the hole. A contact-band expansion M_flux of M_obj exposes local residuals on the supporting plane. I-JEPA, pretrained for masked token prediction, supplies a context-conditioned hole target in representation space; sparse projected gradients align decoded Fill completions with that target inside the instance, while coordinates outside the packed support stay locked. Under instance-only masks on RemovalBench, RORD-Val, and DEFACTO-Val, PredErase improves the native FLUX.2 backbone. Supervised removers remain stronger on several full-image appearance metrics; the supported claim is training-free object-and-effect editing of frozen Fill, not replacement of paired-data erasers.

</details>


### [64] [Candidate-Expanding Routing with Permutation-Stabilized Experts for Mixed-Format Medical VQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00959)
*Hai-Dang Nguyen,Huy-Hieu Pham*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文针对混合格式医疗VQA（多选题+开放性文本输出）的两种失效模式——多选题对选项符号/位置敏感、开放答案序列化格式错误导致自动评估失败——提出了答案文本记忆、置换稳定的视觉-语言专家、以及稀疏候选扩展路由器。内部回顾性分析显示二元路由器从88.95%提升至91.73%（+2.78pp），但视觉消融显示大量文本依赖，且未涉及临床正确性验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗VQA的混合格式输出中，多选题预测受选项符号和位置影响而不稳定，而开放文本答案即使临床合理也可能因序列化格式错误在自动评估中失败。需要同时解决选项选择稳定性和机器可读输出契约的有效性。

Method: 提出三个组件：(1) 答案文本记忆用于稳定开放输出；(2) 置换稳定化的视觉-语言专家用于多选题；(3) 稀疏候选扩展路由器，将专家top-2作为可路由候选加入记忆和专家top-1。采用循环调度训练，使top-2成为可路由候选而非仅靠二元选择。

Result: 在1,403例内部回顾性分析中，扩展改进了匹配的二元路由器（88.95%→91.73%，+2.78pp，95% CI 1.57-3.99），挽救56个错误、17个回归。Oracle覆盖率从90.31%升至96.15%，最终提交配置达92.23%。开放问题在475/475个schema有效输出上无修复/重试/硬门失败。视觉消融显示大量文本依赖。

Conclusion: 候选扩展提供了主要的受控路由增益；开放路径证据确立了输出契约有效性而非临床正确性。这意味着该方法在医疗部署中验证的是格式合规性，而非临床准确性，适用范围有限。

Abstract: Mixed-format medical visual question answering (VQA) requires stable option selection and machine-readable free-text output. The two formats fail differently: multiple-choice predictions can change with option symbols or positions, while clinically plausible open answers can fail automated evaluation when serialization is malformed. We address both challenges with an answer-text memory, a permutation-stabilized vision--language expert, and a sparse candidate- expanding router. The cyclic schedule follows prior work; our contribution is to make expert top-2 a routable candidate alongside memory and expert top-1. On a 1,403-case retrospective internal analysis, this expansion improves a matched binary router from 88.95% to 91.73% (+2.78 percentage points; 95% CI 1.57--3.99), with 56 rescued errors and 17 regressions. Oracle coverage rises from 90.31% to 96.15%, and the final submitted configuration reaches 92.23% on the same retrospective split. For open questions, strict generation and deterministic guards produce 475/475 schema- valid participant-facing outputs without repair, retry, or hard-gate failure. Visual ablations reveal substantial textual dependence. Candidate expansion supplies the principal controlled routing gain; open-path evidence establishes output-contract validity rather than clinical correctness in medical use or deployment.

</details>


### [65] [Conditional Flow Matching for Cross-Field MRI Harmonisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00960)
*Baris Imre,Aram Salehi,Levente Baljer,Andrew Webb,Marius Staring,Efe Ilicak*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 面向MRIxFields2026挑战赛Task3，提出一个单一条件流匹配模型，实现5种场强、3种对比度下任意定向配对（共60种组合）的跨场脑MRI翻译。模型学习从源切片直接到目标切片的velocity field，而非从噪声开始；仅用3对配对受试者，通过三级训练（退化桥预训练、跨场微调、对抗细化）学习映射；推理时用Heun二阶求解器数步集成。6.3M参数模型在评测集上达到平均SSIM 0.909，超越相同网络的回归与扩散基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同一受试者的MRI在不同场强下影像差异显著，阻碍跨站点数据比较与合并。作者旨在解决MRIxFields2026挑战的Task3：用单一模型翻译任意两个方向的五场强×三对比度组合，同时克服配对数据极度稀缺（仅3个受试者）的难题。

Method: 方法核心为条件流匹配（conditional flow matching），因源与目标体素已空间配准，直接学习将源切片带向目标切片的velocity field而非从噪声起步。三级训练策略：(1) 退化桥预训练——从大量未配对回顾性队列中蒸馏恢复先验；(2) 跨场微调——在配对队列上覆盖所有有向配对；(3) 对抗细化——锐化输出。推理用二阶Heun求解器，每切片5步。模型仅6.3M参数，覆盖所有60种场强-对比度组合。

Result: 仅靠未配对数据训练得到的恢复先验即达平均SSIM 0.837，每级训练阶段均进一步提升。评测集上模型达平均SSIM 0.909（跨三对比度平均），在全部三个挑战指标上超越基于相同网络架构的回归与扩散基线。推理仅需每切片5个求解器步骤。

Conclusion: 该工作证明即使仅有3个配对受试者，通过退化桥预训练等多种策略，单一轻量模型仍可有效完成跨场强、跨对比度的多向MRI翻译，且优于传统回归与扩散方法，为多站点医学影像数据整合提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Magnetic resonance images of the same subject look markedly different across field strengths, which complicates the comparison and pooling of data across sites. We address cross-field brain-MRI translation for the MRIxFields2026 challenge, and in particular its Task~3: a single model that translates between any directed pair of the five field strengths and across three contrasts. We phrase the problem as a conditional flow matching path: because the source and target volumes are spatially registered, we learn a velocity field that carries the source slice directly to the target slice, rather than starting from noise. To learn this mapping from only three paired subjects, the unified model is trained in three stages: a degradation-bridge pretraining that distills a restoration prior from the abundant unpaired retrospective cohort, a cross-field finetuning over all directed pairs on the paired cohort, and an adversarial refinement that sharpens the output. At inference, we integrate the learned velocity with a second-order Heun solver in a handful of steps. A restoration prior learned without any paired data already reaches a mean SSIM of 0.837, and each subsequent training stage improves on it. A single 6.3M-parameter model thereby covers all 60 field-pair and contrast combinations, with inference in five solver steps per slice. On the challenge evaluation set the model reaches a mean SSIM of 0.909, averaged over the three contrasts, outperforming regression and diffusion baselines built on the identical network on all three challenge metrics.

</details>


### [66] [ReFlowSET: Representation-Aligned Latent Flow Matching for SAR-to-EO Image Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00968)
*Jeonghyeok Do,Seungchul Lee,Munchurl Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReFlowSET提出一种SAR-to-EO图像翻译的端到端概率流程匹配框架，通过联合SAR-EO重构审计自动选择潜在编码器，并从头训练更小的条件DiT，利用冻结视觉基础模型做噪声-干净特征对齐进行语义引导（仅训练时使用），在QXS-SAROPT和SAR2Opt上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在扩散方法通常继承预训练的自动编码器，但不同码本/模态下重构保真度差异大。潜在编解码器影响SAR条件与EO目标的双向保真度，是基础性设计选择，而现有方法多依赖基于自然图像预训练的自编码器，未针对SAR-EO任务进行适配。

Method: 1) 通过联合SAR-EO重构审计（round-trip reconstruction audit）选择最优潜在编码器；2) 在选定潜空间从头训练一个显著更小的条件DiT，采用双流SAR条件注入+联合特征精炼；3) 训练过程中利用冻结的视觉基础模型将中间噪声EO特征与干净目标EO表示对齐，提供语义引导，仅训练使用无额外推理开销。

Result: 在QXS-SAROPT与SAR2Opt两个数据集上，ReFlowSET在多种感知保真度与分布指标上取得SOTA性能，代码与预训练权重已公开。

Conclusion: 通过针对SAR-EO任务联合重构审核选择编解码器、并用视觉基础模型做免推理成本的训练期语义对齐，可以显著提升SAR到EO图像翻译的质量，且无需继承重型预训练生成器。

Abstract: SAR-to-EO image translation aims to generate electro-optical (EO) imagery from synthetic aperture radar (SAR) observations. Existing latent diffusion approaches typically inherit a predetermined autoencoder, although reconstruction fidelity can vary substantially across codecs and modalities. Because the latent codec affects the round-trip preservation of both SAR conditions and EO targets, codec selection constitutes a fundamental design choice; nevertheless, existing methods largely rely on codecs pretrained on natural images. To remedy this, we introduce ReFlowSET, a conditional latent flow-matching framework that selects its codec through a joint SAR--EO reconstruction audit. Rather than inheriting a heavyweight pretrained generator, ReFlowSET trains a substantially smaller conditional DiT from scratch in the selected latent space, using dual-stream SAR conditioning followed by joint feature refinement. To provide semantic guidance for this from-scratch training, intermediate noisy-EO features are aligned with clean target-EO representations extracted by a frozen vision foundation model. This alignment is used only during training and introduces no additional inference cost. Experiments on QXS-SAROPT and SAR2Opt demonstrate state-of-the-art performance across diverse perceptual fidelity and distributional metrics. Code and pretrained weights are publicly available at https://github.com/KAIST-VICLab/ReFlowSET.

</details>


### [67] [Semi-Supervised Virtual Staining via Morphology Preservation and Histopathological Realism Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00984)
*Baoshun Wang,Weiping Lin,Linwu Wang,Yihuang Hu,Baptiste Magnier,Liansheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个稳定的半监督虚拟染色框架，利用少量配对数据和大量无配对源图像，通过Hessian形态保持和病理学现实主义约束来抑制结构/外观漂移，在H&E转IHC（Ki67、HER2）和FFPE-to-H&E任务上取得一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有虚拟染色方法高度依赖严格配对且精确配准的训练数据，这在常规病理实践中获取困难且成本高昂。降低对配对数据的依赖是临床落地的重要需求。

Method: 半监督框架：1）Hessian导出的形态保持损失从未配对源图像提取结构线索，约束输出保留组织形态；2）病理学现实主义约束引导输出具备目标染色特征。两者联合抑制结构/外观漂移，防止退化为轮廓增强或纯颜色变换。

Result: 在H&E-to-IHC（Ki67、HER2）和FFPE-to-H&E三项翻译任务上，图像质量、形态保持、鲁棒性和下游诊断性能均取得一致改进。

Conclusion: 半监督虚拟染色框架能有效利用未配对数据，在降低配对数据依赖的同时保持甚至提升形态保真与诊断价值，代码将开源。
