Motivation: 现有基于适配器（如LoRA）的概念擦除方法为每个目标概念分配静态的单一擦除方向，但复杂概念往往包含多个潜在语义原型（不同物体、场景或关系），需要不同的局部擦除方向。单一LoRA更新对这些异构擦除需求取平均，导致困难原型擦除不足、邻近良性语义被过度编辑。

Method: 1) 在提示特征空间中拟合高斯混合模型（GMM）以估计目标概念的潜在语义原型；2) 推理时将输入提示投影到特征空间，计算其高斯后验责任（posterior responsibilities）；3) 后验责任条件化核心生成器（core generator），生成提示特异的、范数有界的LoRA秩空间残差重构；4) 以单个轻量适配器实现原型自适应的概念擦除。

Result: 相比各指标最强基线：平均攻击成功率（ASR）降低7.95%，COCO FID降低14.72%，CLIP Score提升4.98%。额外实验展示了对对抗性提示的鲁棒性、多身份和多风格擦除的可扩展性，以及与SDXL和FLUX的兼容性。

Conclusion: Gaussian Core LoRA通过分布感知的、提示自适应的低秩适配框架，有效解决了复杂概念擦除中静态单一方向假设的缺陷，在擦除效果、生成质量和语义保持上均取得显著改进，且保持单一轻量适配器的部署效率。

Abstract: Concept erasure aims to suppress unsafe, privacy-sensitive, or undesirable generations in text-to-image diffusion models while preserving benign semantics, visual quality, and deployment efficiency. Existing adapter-based methods, such as Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), typically freeze the diffusion backbone and learn lightweight parameter updates to steer generation away from target semantics. However, these methods usually assign a static semantic erasure direction to each target concept. This assumption is overly coarse for broad and complex target concepts, since a concept often contains multiple latent semantic prototypes involving different objects, scenes, or relations, and requires different local erasure directions. A single LoRA update averages these heterogeneous erasure demands, leading to under-erasure on difficult prototypes and over-editing of nearby benign semantics. To address this limitation, we propose Gaussian Core LoRA, a distribution-aware low-rank adaptation framework. It fits a Gaussian mixture model in the prompt feature space to estimate latent semantic prototypes within the target concept. During inference, each input prompt is projected into this feature space to compute its Gaussian posterior responsibilities, which condition the core generator to produce a prompt-specific, norm-bounded residual reconfiguration of the shared LoRA rank space. This enables prototype-adaptive erasure with a single lightweight adapter. Compared with the strongest baseline on each metric, Gaussian Core LoRA reduces average Attack Success Rate (ASR) by 7.95%, lowers COCO Fr'echet Inception Distance (FID) by 14.72%, and improves CLIP Score by 4.98%. Further experiments show robustness to adversarial prompts, scalability to multi-identity and multi-style erasure, and compatibility with SDXL and FLUX.

</details>


### [103] [RadMatch: Auditable Radiology Report Evaluation via Finding-Level Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01470)
*Charles Corbière,Léo Machado,Aubin Charley,Baptiste Callard,Pierre Manceron,Corentin Dancette*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RadMatch 是一种多阶段、基于 LLM 的评估指标，将放射学报告比较分解为结构化发现级匹配，并在七个临床属性维度上进行意义感知打分与错误刻画。核心得分是可操作错误数（actionable-error count），兼具可解释性和可审计性，并支持分诊、安全召回/精确率等部署视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于 LLM 的评估指标虽与放射科医生判断相关性最好，但输出单一不透明分数，临床医生和模型构建者难以解释或审计。

Method: 多阶段 LLM 指标：将报告分解为结构化 finding 级匹配，结合意义感知打分（significance-aware scoring），在状态、位置、严重程度、形态、确定性、纵向比较、测量七个临床属性维度上进行错误刻画。候选 finding 评为正确/部分/不正确，未匹配的记为遗漏或幻觉，主得分为可操作错误计数，并附加 triage 与 safety recall/precision 等视角，仅依赖 few-shot prompting。

Result: 在两个专家基准上，RadMatch 是最符合临床对齐的指标：在 ReXVal 上匹配放射科医生间一致性，在更难的 RadEvalExpert 上比此前最优指标提升一倍以上。

Conclusion: RadMatch 提供可解释、可审计的放射学报告评估方案，拟作为开源代码并配交互式仪表盘发布，设计上可扩展到其他模态和解剖区域。

Abstract: As AI systems are increasingly used to draft radiology reports, reliably evaluating their clinical quality remains a critical challenge. Large language model (LLM)-based metrics are now the best-correlated with radiologist judgment, yet they output a single opaque score that neither a clinician nor a model builder can easily interpret or audit. We introduce RadMatch, a multi-stage, LLM-based metric that decomposes report comparison into a structured finding-level matching with significance-aware scoring and error characterization across seven clinical attribute dimensions (status, location, severity, morphology, certainty, longitudinal comparison, and measurement). The main score is the actionable-error count, both interpretable and auditable. Candidate findings are graded correct, partial, or incorrect, and unmatched findings are counted as missed or hallucinated. Triage and actionable safety recall/precision and per-subset views add complementary, deployment-oriented lenses. Across two expert benchmarks, RadMatch is the most clinically aligned metric, matching inter-radiologist agreement on ReXVal and more than doubling the best prior metric on the harder RadEvalExpert. Relying only on few-shot prompting, it is designed to extend to other modalities and anatomies. We will release RadMatch as open-source code with an interactive dashboard for inspecting results.

</details>


### [104] [CameraEditor: Camera-Controlled Image Editing via Video-Prior Sequential Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01479)
*Xin Shen,Chengyou Jia,Keshuo Xing,Zifeng Zhu,Changliang Xia,Bowen Ping,Zhuohang Dang,Hangwei Qian,Minnan Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CameraEditor将相机参数控制的图像编辑问题重新表述为时间序列预测任务，利用视频扩散模型的时间一致性，结合显式几何感知模块和动态参考路由机制，通过动态全景裁剪构建几何严谨的视觉参考对，并插入中间过渡帧分解大视角变化，实现高精度的相机控制编辑。论文还构建了5,760个训练实例和462个测试用例的CamEditor-Bench评估套件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像编辑模型虽然擅长语义和风格操作，但在显式相机参数控制上表现不佳。面对大视角变换时，指令驱动模型要么产生结构撕裂，要么生成忽略几何指令的保守输出，缺乏对相机几何参数的精确控制能力。

Method: 将相机控制编辑从空间问题转化为时间序列预测任务：1) 利用视频扩散模型的时间一致性；2) 集成显式几何感知模块和动态参考路由机制；3) 通过动态全景裁剪构造几何严谨的视觉参考对，克服文本指令的歧义性；4) 策略性插入中间过渡帧分解大视角变化，提供临时缓冲区以保持内容身份和空间一致性。

Result: 构建了5,760个实例的训练数据集和462个测试用例的模型无关评估套件CamEditor-Bench。大量实验表明CameraEditor在相机控制精度和源身份保持方面达到最先进水平，优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过将相机编辑重构为时间预测问题并利用视频扩散模型的时间一致性，CameraEditor有效解决了大视角变化下的结构撕裂和指令忽略问题，实现了精确且身份保持的相机控制编辑。

Abstract: Beyond semantic content, camera parameters play a pivotal role in dictating the geometric perspective and appearance of any given image. While recent image editing models excel at semantic and stylistic manipulation, they struggle with explicit camera parameter control. When handling large perspective shifts, instruction-driven models face a dilemma: they either suffer from structural tearing or generate conservative outputs that ignore geometric instructions. To address this, we introduce CameraEditor, a framework that reformulates camera-controlled editing from a spatial problem into a temporal sequence prediction task. By leveraging the temporal coherence of video diffusion models, our approach integrates an explicit geometric perception module with a dynamic reference routing mechanism. This allows us to construct geometrically rigorous visual reference pairs via dynamic panorama cropping, overcoming the ambiguity of text-based instructions. Furthermore, CameraEditor strategically inserts intermediate transition frames to decompose large perspective shifts, providing a robust temporal buffer that preserves content identity and spatial coherence. We construct a training dataset of 5,760 instances. As an independent contribution, we introduce CamEditor-Bench, a model-agnostic evaluation suite of 462 test cases. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CameraEditor achieves state-of-the-art camera control precision and source identity preservation, outperforming existing methods.

</details>


### [105] [Benchmarking Spatial, Spectral, and Self-Supervised Cues for Face Forgery Detection under Realistic Degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01511)
*Lucas Cunha,Lucas Sotomaior,Lucas Gasperin,Beatriz Caldas,Eduardo Pianovski,Rayson Laroca*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于MFFI数据集的人脸伪造检测标准化基准，评估六种模型家族（CNN、Transformer、冻结DINOv3自监督骨干）在清晰与退化（压缩、缩放、模糊）测试分区上的表现。结果表明干净集性能不能可靠反映鲁棒性：Xception在RGB上取得最佳干净性能（0.884 ROC-AUC）但退化严重，而冻结DINOv3仅训练线性分类头即在退化集取得最强结果（0.726）。频域线索需与RGB结合才有效，CNN关注局部伪影而DINOv3依赖更广泛的面部结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度伪造检测器在受控基准上表现优异，但现实中的图像退化（压缩、缩放、模糊）会显著影响其可靠性。缺少标准化基准来系统评估人脸伪造检测在退化条件下的鲁棒性，导致对模型实际泛化能力的误判。

Method: 使用MFFI数据集构建干净与退化的标准测试分区；对比六种模型家族（CNN、Transformer、冻结DINOv3自监督骨干）；评估空间、频谱、混合三种输入表示；同时进行表示分析（傅里叶与RGB的互补性）和定性归因图分析。

Result: 干净集上的最佳性能（Xception RGB，0.884 AUC）在更难退化分区上大幅下降；冻结DINOv3仅在训练线性头的情况下取得退化集最强结果（0.726 AUC）；纯频谱输入始终弱于空间表示，但频域线索与RGB结合有效；归因图显示CNN关注局部伪影而DINOv3利用更广泛的面部结构信息。

Conclusion: 干净评估不足以衡量伪造检测的真实鲁棒性，需要退化评估协议；自监督视觉表示（如DINOv3）是鲁棒人脸伪造检测的有希望方向，即使仅用线性分类头也能抵抗多种图像退化。

Abstract: Face forgery detectors often achieve strong results on controlled benchmarks, but their reliability under realistic image degradations remains limited. This paper presents a standardized benchmark for face forgery detection using the Multi-Dimensional Face Forgery Image (MFFI) dataset and evaluates performance on both clean and degraded test partitions. We compare six model families, including convolutional networks, transformer-based models, and a frozen self-supervised DINOv3 backbone, across spatial, spectral, and hybrid input representations. The results show that clean-set performance is not a reliable indicator of robustness under compression, resizing, and blurring. Xception with RGB obtains the best clean performance, reaching 0.884 mean ROC-AUC, but degrades substantially on the harder partition. In contrast, frozen DINOv3 achieves the strongest degraded-set result, with 0.726 mean ROC-AUC, while training only a linear classification head. The representation analysis indicates that Fourier-domain cues are most useful when combined with RGB information, whereas purely spectral inputs consistently underperform spatial representations. Qualitative attribution maps further suggest that convolutional detectors focus on localized artifacts, while DINOv3 relies on broader facial structure. These findings reinforce the need for degraded evaluation protocols and highlight self-supervised visual representations as a promising direction for robust face forgery detection. Our source code is publicly available at https://github.com/lucasdocunha/FaceForgery-Benchmark/.

</details>


### [106] [TempCloze: Can Video-LLMs Identify the Missing Middle?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01515)
*Wenqi Pei,Henry Hengyuan Zhao,Yilai Liu,Jiahao Meng,Han Chen,Ziyu Wang,Hongyang Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TempCloze基准，通过视频完形填空（给定首尾片段、从四个候选中选出真实中间片段）来评估Video-LLMs的视觉时序推理能力，规避语言捷径。包含1,521个精心筛选的视频，沿语义（什么事件）、对齐（何时发生）、递进（如何展开）三个维度构建干扰项。对31个模型（10专有+21开源）的评估发现对齐（Alignment）是主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频时序推理基准常被语言中介，模型可利用选项措辞、答案相关性或语言先验等捷径，而非真正进行视觉时序推理。需要一种能最大限度减少语言捷径、直接评估视觉时序推理能力的基准。

Method: 设计TempCloze视频完形填空基准：给定视频首尾片段，模型需从四个同源干扰候选中识别真实缺失片段。干扰项沿三个维度构建：Semantic（什么事件应发生）、Alignment（何时发生）、Progression（如何展开），并共享场景与物体以减少外观线索。视频来源包括长镜头和第一人称视频，共1,521个样本。评估10个专有和21个开源Video-LLMs，并对代表性模型进行错误模式与行为敏感性分析。

Result: 评估发现Alignment是主要瓶颈：模型通常能识别合理的语义内容和局部事件递进，但在时序对齐上表现困难。进一步通过TempCloze-Mixed和TempCloze-Hard上的错误模式和行为敏感性分析，考察了候选顺序、上下文方向、可见跨度、帧密度和测试时缩放对模型选择的影响。

Conclusion: TempCloze揭示了Video-LLMs在视觉时序推理（尤其是时间对齐）上的显著短板，为未来改进提供了基准和方法论指导。

Abstract: Temporal reasoning benchmarks for Video-LLMs are often mediated by language, leaving room for linguistic shortcuts from option wording, answer correlations, or language priors. To reduce such shortcuts, we introduce TempCloze, a video cloze benchmark for evaluating visual temporal reasoning in Video-LLMs. Given the beginning and ending clips of a video, models must identify the true missing middle from four candidates. TempCloze contains 1,521 carefully filtered videos from seven sources, mainly long-take and egocentric videos. We construct same-source distractors along three dimensions: Semantic asks what event should happen, Alignment probes when it should occur, and Progression tests how it should unfold, while shared scenes and objects reduce appearance cues. Our evaluation of 10 proprietary and 21 open-source Video-LLMs reveals Alignment as the primary bottleneck: models often recognize plausible semantic content and local event progression but struggle with temporal alignment. We further conduct error pattern and behavioral sensitivity analyses on TempCloze-Mixed and TempCloze-Hard with four representative models to examine where errors arise and how candidate order, context direction, visible span, frame density, and test-time scaling influence model choices.

</details>


### [107] [DualDiff3D: Dual Structure-Appearance Diffusion Priors for Reliability-Enhanced 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01516)
*Qian Wang,Yu Wang,Weiqi Li,Xinhua Cheng,Xiandong Meng,Ronggang Wang,Jian Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DualDiff管线，利用双扩散先验（一个分支提取结构信息，一个分支保证外观一致性）配合结构-外观注意力模块来细化低质量3DGS新视角渲染结果，并集成到DualDiff3D重建框架中，通过可靠性增强的Render-Refine-Optimize循环逐步优化3D Gaussian Splatting重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS在有限输入视角场景下重建质量差、新视角渲染存在伪影。现有方法将渲染视角和参考视角在额外维度拼接后送入单一扩散网络，忽视了不同视角应保持外观相似但结构因视角变化而不同的内在特性，导致两种属性冲突产生模糊。

Method: 提出DualDiff管线：(1) 双扩散分支结构——一个分支从低质量新视角中提取结构信息，另一分支与参考视角保证外观一致性；(2) 结构-外观注意力（SAA）模块引入参考引导；(3) DualDiff3D框架集成可靠性增强的Render-Refine-Optimize（RRO）循环，逐步稳健地融合细化后的新视角，优化3DGS。

Result: 大量实验证明该方法在仅推理（inference-only）设置下即超越SOTA方法，通过训练可进一步提升性能。代码和预训练权重已开源。

Conclusion: 通过解耦扩散先验中的结构与外观信息处理，有效解决有限视角3DGS重建中的模糊与伪影问题，证明了双分支扩散+迭代细化循环在稀疏视角重建中的有效性。

Abstract: While 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has revolutionized 3D reconstruction and novel-view synthesis, scenarios with limited input views often lead to poor reconstruction quality and artifacts in rendered novel views. Recent efforts attempt to utilize powerful diffusion priors, yet they typically process rendered and reference views concatenated along an additional dimension in a single network. These methods overlook an inherent nature that different views should maintain appearance similarity but differ in structure due to view shifts, leading to blur caused by conflicts between the two properties. In this paper, we propose DualDiff, a novel pipeline that leverages dual diffusion priors with a Structure-Appearance Attention (SAA) module to introduce reference guidance for refining low-quality novel views rendered from flawed 3D representations. Specifically, we retain one diffusion branch to focus on extracting structural information from the low-quality novel views, while introducing another branch to ensure appearance consistency with reference views. Furthermore, we present a 3D reconstruction framework named DualDiff3D, which integrates a reliability-enhanced Render-Refine-Optimize (RRO) loop to progressively and robustly incorporate the refined novel views, yielding more accurate 3DGS. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach outperforms state-of-the-art methods even in the inference-only setting, with further performance gains achievable through training. Our code and pre-trained weights are available at https://github.com/Akaneqwq/DualDiff3D.

</details>


### [108] [Revisiting Cross-View Completion: Self-Supervised Pre-Training via Reconstruction Error Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01530)
*Thibaut Loiseau,Guillaume Bourmaud,Vincent Lepetit*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Gekko是一种从零训练的自监督3D视觉预训练网络，将交叉视角重建误差与掩码自编码误差的相对改进作为协同可见性的自监督代理，联合执行交叉视角补全、掩码自编码和逐像素相对改进预测，为所有掩码区域提供额外的双目信号，无需任何3D标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督跨视角补全预训练（如CroCo）在非协同可见区域退化为单目训练信号，参考视图无法为这些区域提供重建信息。作者将这一局限转化为有用信号：相对误差改进可作为协同可见性的代理，从而为掩码区域提供额外的双目监督信号。

Method: Gekko从零训练，网络同时执行三条任务分支：(1) 交叉视角补全；(2) 掩码自编码；(3) 逐像素预测交叉视角重建误差相对掩码自编码误差的改进量。该改进量作为协同可见性的自监督代理：改进大的区域表示协同可见，改进小的表示非协同可见。此外提出简单的基于步长的课程学习策略，可直接从原始视频训练，无需3D预处理。

Result: 在相同架构和训练数据下，Gekko在零样本对应估计、相对位姿估计和点图回归上一致超越CroCo：最严格相对位姿阈值下精度提升达6倍，ETH3D端到端误差降低22%。额外学习到的通道本身是强大的协同可见性检测器，冻结特征优于发布的可比或更大规模跨视角骨干网络。可直接从原始视频训练并与使用精选数据训练的模型匹配。

Conclusion: Gekko通过将交叉视角预训练的局限性转化为协同可见性监督信号，无需3D标注即可提供双目训练信号，在多个3D视觉任务上持续超越现有方法，并可泛化到未见场景，代码和预训练模型已公开。

Abstract: Self-supervised pre-training via cross-view completion learns strong features for 3D vision from co-visible regions of image pairs. However, the reference view provides little information for reconstructing non-co-visible patches, implicitly yielding a monocular training signal in these regions. We introduce Gekko, which turns this limitation into a useful signal. The relative improvement of the cross-view reconstruction error over a masked-autoencoder error is a self-supervised proxy for co-visibility: large improvements indicate co-visible regions, negligible ones non-co-visible areas. Gekko is a network, trained from scratch, that jointly performs cross-view completion, masked autoencoding, and per-pixel prediction of this relative improvement, providing an additional binocular signal for all masked regions without any ground-truth 3D annotation. Under identical architectures and training data, Gekko consistently outperforms CroCo on zero-shot correspondence estimation, relative pose estimation, and pointmap regression, with up to 6 times higher accuracy at the strictest relative-pose threshold and a 22% drop in end-point error on ETH3D. The extra channel it learns is itself a strong co-visibility detector on unseen scenes, and Gekko's frozen features outperform released cross-view backbones of comparable or larger size. It can also be trained directly from raw videos with a simple stride-based curriculum, removing the cumbersome 3D preprocessing prior methods require while matching models trained on curated data. Code and pre-trained models are publicly available.

</details>


### [109] [What, Where, and How: Probing Spatiotemporal Representations in Video Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01551)
*Sharon S. Musa,Fereshteh Forghani,Harrish Thasarathan,Sonia Joseph,Matthew Kowal,Konstantinos G. Derpanis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对自监督视频基础模型V-JEPA 2和VideoMAE-v2进行逐层分析，探究其学习到的时空表征（相机运动、直觉物理、异常检测）在Transformer层中的分布与几何组织，并提出基于样条的潜在空间插值方法实现相机运动操控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督视频基础模型虽能学习丰富的时空表征，但其编码的具体视觉概念、这些概念在层间的分布位置以及表征的几何组织结构仍不清晰。理解这些问题有助于更好地解释和操控这些模型。

Method: 使用轻量级探针逐层分析V-JEPA 2和VideoMAE-v2，训练三类时间性分类器：相机运动理解、直觉物理和异常检测。同时分析单视频时间特征在高维表征空间中的轨迹几何，并基于此提出几何感知的样条插值方法进行相机运动操控。

Result: 两个模型都能编码相机运动（网络深度60-70%处达到>90% ROC AUC），异常检测表现中等（>60% ROC AUC），但直觉物理任务接近随机水平。时间特征形成平滑的低维轨迹，样条插值生成的视频比线性插值具有更平滑的轨迹和更连贯的时间进展。

Conclusion: 自监督视频基础模型在特定深度层编码了可线性解码的相机运动表征，且这些表征具有几何组织性，可通过几何感知插值进行操控。然而模型对深层物理推理的编码仍有限。

Abstract: Self-supervised video foundation models learn rich spatiotemporal representations, yet it remains unclear what visual concepts these representations encode, where they emerge across transformer layers, and how they are geometrically organized. In this work, we tackle these three questions through a systematic layer-wise analysis of V-JEPA 2 and VideoMAE-v2. We leverage lightweight probes trained to discover three temporally grounded properties: (i) camera motion understanding, (ii) intuitive physics, and (iii) anomaly detection. Both models encode camera motion, with best results ($>90$ ROC AUC) emerging at 60-70% of network depth, and achieve moderate anomaly detection performance ($>60$ ROC AUC), but remain near chance on intuitive-physics tasks, suggesting a limited encoding of deeper physical reasoning. Beyond classification, we find that temporal features from individual videos form smooth low-dimensional trajectories in representation space, suggesting that camera motion is not only linearly decodable but also geometrically organized. Based on these results, we apply geometry-aware spline-based steering in the model's latent representations to interpolate camera motion, yielding steered videos with smoother trajectories and more coherent temporal progression than linear interpolation.

</details>


### [110] [BS: Take the Hint - Interactive Multitracer PET/CT Lesion Segmentation with a Scribble-Conditioned ResEnc U-Net](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01554)
*Marven Sherif,Amgad Elmasry,Youssef Ghazal,Ayman Elghotni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于scribble交互的全身体PET/CT病灶分割方法：采用scribble条件化的残差编码器U-Net，输入CT、PET和一个稀疏前景/背景scribble图，从autoPET-III获胜权重初始化并扩展为四通道输入。PET强度通过CT分割出来的主动脉血池参考进行归一化，消除示踪剂和中心差异。五个fold模型集成，经五次修正后平均Dice从0.554升至0.751，病灶级F1从0.528升至0.733。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全身体PET/CT中的自动化病灶分割因生理性示踪剂摄取模式多样性和不同示踪剂间病灶外观差异而极具挑战性。autoPET/CT V挑战通过引入交互式分割来应对这一问题：用户提供标记前后景的scribble，期望算法充分利用这些交互信号提升分割性能。

Method: 提出scribble条件化的残差编码器U-Net，使用四个输入通道：CT、PET、前景scribble稀疏图和背景scribble稀疏图。网络从autoPET-III获胜权重初始化，新增的两个scribble通道零初始化以精确保留预训练表示。每个fold从对应autoPET-III fold检查点微调。PET强度使用CT分割的每扫描主动脉血池参考归一化。推理时五个fold模型通过滑动窗口patch的softmax输出平均集成，再进行高斯加权拼接。

Result: 在挑战赛五折划分上，无scribble时平均Dice为0.554，病灶级F1为0.528；五次修正后分别提升至0.751和0.733。约85%的增益来自第一次scribble交互。fold模型间的差异在交互过程中缩小五倍，表明交互作用在很大程度上补偿了模型单独分割性能的好坏。

Conclusion: 交互式scribble条件化分割能够有效提升全身体PET/CT病灶分割性能，且交互过程中模型间差异显著缩小。基于CT的主动脉血池归一化有效解决了跨示踪剂和跨中心的PET强度尺度问题，从autoPET-III权重初始化结合零初始化的scribble通道是一种有效的迁移策略。

Abstract: Automated lesion segmentation in whole-body PET/CT is complicated by the variety of physiological tracer uptake patterns and by the differing appearance of lesions across tracers. The autoPET/CT V challenge addresses this by making segmentation interactive: user scribbles marking foreground and background are supplied alongside the image, and the algorithm is expected to exploit them. We present our submission, a scribble-conditioned residual encoder U-Net operating on four input channels: CT, PET, and a sparse scribble map for each of foreground and background. The network is initialised from the autoPET-III winning weights and extended from two to four input channels, with the two scribble channels zero-initialised so that the pretrained representation is preserved exactly at initialisation. Every model is fine-tuned per fold from the corresponding autoPET-III fold checkpoint, so that no validation case is seen during pretraining. PET intensities are normalised against a per-scan aorta blood-pool reference derived from a CT segmentation, which removes tracer- and centre-specific scaling without requiring lesion labels. At inference the five fold models are ensembled by averaging their softmax outputs per sliding-window patch, before Gaussian-weighted stitching. On the challenge's five-fold split, with each fold evaluated on its own validation cases, mean Dice is 0.554 and mean lesion-level F1 is 0.528 without scribbles, rising to 0.751 and 0.733 after five correction rounds. About 85% of that gain follows the first scribble, and the spread between fold models narrows five-fold over the same rounds, so interaction largely compensates for how well or badly a given model segments unaided.

</details>


### [111] [H3-World: Turning Language Understanding into World Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01560)
*Danze Chen,Zeqing Wang,Ziyue Lin,Xingyi Yang,Yeying Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: H3-World将33B的MiniMax-H3视频生成器改造为交互式世界模型，通过将动作表示为角色与相机的结构化指令组合，并结合时间注意力路由实现精确的时序控制，仅需少量训练即可实现角色与相机控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视频生成器已具备通过自然语言实现零样本控制角色行为和相机运动的能力，但这种语言接口是粗糙的，缺乏精确的时间锚定。作者希望不需要引入专用动作模块，而是将这种涌现的粗粒度语言控制转化为精确、时序化的交互式世界控制能力。

Method: 1) 将每个动作表示为角色指令与相机指令的结构化组合，并与对应的时序视频隐变量对齐；2) 引入时间注意力路由（temporal attention routing），将每条指令限制在其目标时间区间内，减少跨动作的控制泄漏；3) 直接复用大规模视频预训练中学到的语义表征，仅需轻量适配。

Result: 仅用8,000个游戏样本、10,000步LoRA优化和0.199%的可训练参数，即实现有效的角色与相机控制，同时保持强生成质量，并能泛化到未见场景。

Conclusion: 大型视频生成器中涌现的控制能力可以被高效地转化为交互式世界控制，语言正成为大型视频生成器的自然控制接口。

Abstract: We present H3-World, an efficient framework that turns the 33B MiniMax-H3 video generator into an interactive world model. Our key finding is that, as large video generators become more capable, language is emerging as a natural interface for control. MiniMax-H3, for example, already supports zero-shot control of character behavior and camera motion through natural-language instructions. Building on this, H3-World turns this coarse language interface into precise, temporally grounded world control, without introducing dedicated action modules. Specifically, we represent each action as a structured combination of character and camera instructions, and align them with the corresponding temporal video latents. To make the control temporally precise, we further introduce temporal attention routing, which restricts each instruction to its intended time interval and reduces control leakage across actions. Importantly, H3-World directly reuses the semantic representations learned during large-scale video pretraining and requires only lightweight adaptation. With only 8,000 gameplay samples, 10,000 LoRA optimization steps, and 0.199% trainable parameters, H3-World achieves effective character and camera control while preserving strong generation quality. It also generalizes to unseen scenarios. These results show that the control capabilities emerging in large video generators can be efficiently transformed into interactive world control.

</details>


### [112] [SpatialGuard: Harness-Guided Verifiable Spatial Reasoning for Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01582)
*Ziyun Qian,Zizhi Chen,Yizhou Liu,Mingyang Sun,Dingkang Yang,Lihua Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SpatialGuard是一个面向复杂3D空间文本到图像生成的结构化布局引导框架，通过解析提示词生成3D布局、将其视觉化，并用批评者验证一致性，同时引入Layout Harness维持多轮迭代中的约束稳定，实现规划、实现、验证、修复的可验证生成流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示驱动或布局条件的文本到图像生成方法虽提升了可控性，但在视觉采样前缺乏可优化、可验证的空间中间表示，导致多轮生成中物体关系、遮挡、可见性和相机约束逐渐退化。

Method: SpatialGuard包含三个核心组件：Spatial Layout Architect将提示解析为合成导向的3D布局；Visual Realizer将布局实现为视觉条件和候选图像；Visual Alignment Critic验证提示、布局和图像之间的一致性。此外引入Layout Harness组织规则约束、工具调用、共享知识和反馈循环，使约束在可编辑布局状态上跨迭代保持稳定。

Result: 综合实验表明SpatialGuard在复杂3D空间布局生成上达到最先进性能，并在空间忠实度上优于现有文本到图像和布局控制基线。

Conclusion: 该工作将复杂空间生成从隐式的提示跟随转变为包含规划、实现、验证和修复的可验证过程，为高度依赖空间结构一致性的生成任务提供了更可靠的框架。

Abstract: Complex 3D spatial text to image generation requires models to convert natural language into stable visual geometry, not merely semantic appearance. Existing prompt-driven or layout-conditioned methods improve controllability, but often lack an optimizable and verifiable spatial intermediary before visual sampling. As a result, object relations, occlusion, visibility, and camera constraints can decay during multi-round generation. This paper presents SpatialGuard, a structured layout-guided framework for complex 3D spatial text-to-image generation. SpatialGuard parses prompts into image synthesis-oriented 3D layouts through a Spatial Layout Architect, realizes them as visual conditions and candidate images through a Visual Realizer, and uses a Visual Alignment Critic to validate consistency among prompt, layout, and image. To keep constraints stable across iterations, SpatialGuard introduces a Layout Harness that organizes rule constraints, tool invocation, shared knowledge, and feedback loops around the editable layout state. This design turns complex spatial generation from implicit prompt following into a verifiable process of planning, realization, validation, and repair. Comprehensive experiments show that SpatialGuard achieves state-of-the-art performance in complex 3D spatial layout generation and improves spatial faithfulness over existing text-to-image and layout control baselines.

</details>


### [113] [A Benchmark for Vehicle Attribute Classification in Cross-Domain Surveillance Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01584)
*Sergio M. Silva,Otavio T. Remer,Gabriel E. Lima,Lucas Wojcik,Rayson Laroca,David Menotti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出UVIB基准，用于在无约束（真实监控）场景下评估车辆三大运行属性分析任务：前后朝向、VMMR适用性和颜色清晰度。包含84,835张来自七个巴西公开数据集的车辆图像，按监控与一般采集域分组，并统一了二进制标注。四种代表性架构在混合域、跨域和跨数据集协议下评估，结果显示域偏移对性能影响大于架构选择，跨域场景下性能显著退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在受控条件下训练的车辆属性分析模型在真实监控场景中常因视角、遮挡、光照和传感器差异而性能退化，但现有基准缺乏对这种运行鲁棒性的显式评估。作者旨在建立统一基准来衡量车辆分析任务在域偏移下的实际表现。

Method: 构建UVIB基准，整合七个巴西公开数据集（84,835张图像），统一三个任务的二进制标注（朝向、VMMR适用性、颜色清晰度），区分为监控域与一般采集域。在混合域、跨域、跨数据集三种协议下评估EfficientNetV2-S、ResNet-50、ViT/B-16和YOLO11s-cls四种架构。

Result: 域偏移对性能的影响大于架构选择；跨域设定下性能显著退化，尤其VMMR适用性和颜色清晰度。朝向任务泛化较可靠，VMMR适用性受类别不平衡和遮挡影响，颜色清晰度对光照与传感器模态高度敏感。

Conclusion: 标准域内准确率不足以反映运行鲁棒性，需要显式测量域偏移下性能的基准和评估协议。UVIB作为首个统一多任务车辆属性运维基准公开发布。

Abstract: Vehicle attribute analysis is a key component of Intelligent Transportation Systems (ITS), supporting applications such as vehicle identification, traffic monitoring, and forensic investigation. However, models trained under controlled conditions often degrade in real surveillance scenarios due to changes in viewpoint, occlusion, illumination, and sensor characteristics. This paper introduces Unconstrained Vehicle Identification Benchmark (UVIB), a benchmark for evaluating three operational vehicle-analysis tasks: front/rear orientation, occlusion-related suitability for Vehicle Make and Model Recognition (VMMR), and color clarity. The benchmark contains 84,835 vehicle images from seven public Brazilian datasets, grouped into surveillance and general acquisition domains, with unified binary annotations that were not jointly available in the original sources. Four representative architectures, EfficientNetV2-S, ResNet-50, ViT/B-16, and YOLO11s-cls, are evaluated under mixed-domain, cross-domain, and cross-dataset protocols. The results show that domain shift has a stronger impact than architecture choice, with substantial degradation in cross-domain settings, especially for VMMR suitability and color clarity. While orientation generalizes more reliably, VMMR suitability remains affected by class imbalance and ambiguous occlusions, and color clarity is highly sensitive to illumination and sensor modality. These findings highlight the need for benchmarks and evaluation protocols that explicitly measure operational robustness beyond standard in-domain accuracy. The proposed benchmark is publicly available at https://github.com/UFPR-IPASP-PR/uvib-vehicle-attributes/.

</details>


### [114] [UI-VISA: U-Net Initialized Vascular Image Segmentation Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01598)
*Asees Kaur,Suzanne S. Sindi,Erica M. Rutter*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出UI-VISA混合分割管线，结合U-Net像素级分割与CNN引导的区域生长算法，在DSA血管图像上通过U-Net预测作为种子点初始化，迭代细化分割以增强血管连通性，在26张DSA图像上5折交叉验证取得最高Dice与clDice，clDice提升具有统计学显著性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DSA图像中血管结构细长、分支复杂，纯像素级深度学习（如U-Net）容易产生断裂或不连续的分割结果，因为它们没有显式强制结构连通性；而区域生长算法虽能保持空间上下文与拓扑连续性，但对种子点初始化高度敏感且计算开销大。需要一种结合两者优势的方法。

Method: UI-VISA混合管线：首先用U-Net生成前景预测并作为种子点信息，随后使用CNN引导的区域生长算法进行迭代细化，在每个生长步骤中强制局部连通性，恢复U-Net遗漏或过度预测的细节血管。

Result: 在26张DSA图像上使用5折交叉验证，与独立U-Net及先前区域生长方法VISA对比。UI-VISA在所有折中获得最高平均Dice和clDice分数；配对Wilcoxon符号秩检验显示clDice改善具有统计学显著性（p=0.023），而Dice改善未达显著（p=0.104）。

Conclusion: UI-VISA通过将U-Net与区域生长结合，有效改善了血管分割的连通性，在clDice指标上获得显著提升，验证了混合方法在保留血管拓扑连续性方面的设计目标。但Dice提升不显著，说明整体精度增益有限，属于医学影像分割领域的增量改进工作。

Abstract: Accurate segmentation of vascular structures in digital subtraction angiography (DSA) images remains challenging due to the thin, elongated, and branching nature of blood vessels. Pixel-wise deep learning approaches such as U-Net achieve strong general-purpose segmentation performance but often produce fragmented or discontinuous predictions in fine vascular regions, since they do not explicitly enforce structural connectivity. Region growing algorithms preserve spatial context and topological continuity, but are highly sensitive to seed point initialization and can be computationally expensive. We propose UI-VISA (U-Net Initialized Vascular Image Segmentation Architecture), a hybrid pipeline that combines the complementary strengths of both approaches. UI-VISA uses U-Net's foreground predictions as informed seed points for a CNN-guided region growing algorithm, which then iteratively refines the segmentation by enforcing local connectivity and recovering fine vessel details that U-Net alone tends to miss or over-predict. We evaluate UI-VISA against standalone U-Net and a prior region-growing-based method (VISA) using 5-fold cross-validation on 26 DSA images. UI-VISA achieves the highest mean Dice and clDice scores across folds, and a paired Wilcoxon signed-rank test shows the improvement in clDice is statistically significant ($p=0.023$), consistent with the method's design goal of preserving vascular connectivity, while the improvement in Dice does not reach significance ($p=0.104$).

</details>


### [115] [Uncovering Understanding-Generation Synergy in Native Unified Multimodal Models: From Representation, Task to System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01607)
*Penghao Wu,Haiwen Diao,Weichen Fan,Lewei Lu,Dahua Lin,Ziwei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文在无预训练视觉先验的受控原生设置下，从表征、任务和系统三个层面研究统一多模态模型（UMM）中视觉理解与生成两大目标的关系，发现它们可相互提供有用信号但在同一计算路径下会相互竞争，任务解耦架构可避免不对称退化，端到端UMM优于planner-exectuor流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然统一多模态模型（UMM）可在单个模型中联合执行视觉理解与生成，但功能上的统一并不保证学习上的协同——两个目标可能互相强化、争夺容量、或仅共存。理解这种关系有助于指导UMM架构设计。

Method: 在受控、结构原生设置下（不含预训练视觉先验）进行三层面分析：表征层面考察各目标对另一目标特征学习的贡献及共享计算路径下的支配效应；任务层面通过三个案例研究检验双向迁移；系统层面比较端到端UMM与planner-executor流水线在需要同时理解和生成的复杂任务上的表现。

Result: 生成可丰富用于理解的视觉特征，理解可增强用于生成的视觉-语言对齐；但当两个目标强制通过同一计算路径时一方会支配另一方，任务解耦架构可避免这种不对称退化。三个案例研究表明理解与生成共享知识时出现正的双向迁移。端到端UMM在需要同时理解和生成的复杂任务上优于匹配的planner-exectuor流水线。

Conclusion: UMM的价值超越统一接口：适当的专业化（任务解耦）、共享任务知识和端到端优化可将共存转变为协同。

Abstract: While unified multimodal models (UMMs) jointly perform visual understanding and generation within a single model, functional unification does not guarantee learning synergy: the two objectives may reinforce each other, compete for capacity, or merely coexist. We investigate their relationship at the representation, task, and system levels in a controlled, structurally native setting without pretrained vision priors. At the representation level, we find that each objective provides useful signal to the other: generation enriches the visual features learned for understanding, while understanding strengthens vision--language alignment for generation. However, when both objectives are forced through the same computation path, one tends to dominate. A task-decoupled architecture that specializes conflicting visual computation while preserving semantic interaction avoids this asymmetric degradation. At the task level, through three case studies, we find positive bidirectional transfer when understanding and generation tasks rely on shared knowledge. At the system level, we show that an end-to-end UMM outperforms a matched planner--executor pipeline on complex tasks that explicitly require both image understanding and generation. Together, these results show that the value of UMMs extends beyond a unified interface: appropriate specialization, shared task knowledge, and end-to-end optimization can turn coexistence into synergy.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [116] [Risk-Aware Decision-Making for Autonomous Overtaking: A World Model-Based Mixture-of-Experts Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00385)
*Yongzhi Liu,Sunan Zhang,Jinchang Xu,Jiawei Wang,Yushu Qiu,Chen Lv,Weichao Zhuang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出WM-RMoE框架，利用世界模型进行并行多步轨迹级安全评估，结合层次化门控机制动态协调长期、短期和规则基安全专家模块，并集成高斯混合模型保留多模态机动分支，用于自动驾驶高速超车的风险感知决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准安全强化学习方法依赖隐式基于价值的安全估计而非显式动力学建模，难以在多变的高速超车场景中准确捕捉多步时域的风险传播，导致局部安全但长期潜在高风险的行为。

Method: 1) 学习潜在动力学模型支持并行多步rollout，将安全评估从动作级提升到轨迹级累积风险评价；2) 层次化门控机制动态协调长视野、短视野及规则基安全专家模块；3) 集成高斯混合模型保留多模态机动分支避免行为模式平均化。

Result: 实验表明WM-RMoE在安全合规性、决策稳定性和泛化能力上显著优于代表性基线，且能生成具有前瞻性和语义差异化特征的不同交通密度下的超车策略。

Conclusion: 世界模型结合风险感知的轨迹级评估与多专家动态协调，可有效提升自动驾驶高速超车场景下长期安全决策的质量和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Autonomous highway overtaking demands foresighted decision-making to handle complex interactions, stochastic traffic evolution, and temporal risk accumulation. However, standard safe reinforcement learning approaches typically rely on implicit value-based risk estimations rather than explicit dynamics modeling, thereby struggling to accurately capture complex risk propagation over multi-step horizons. This limitation frequently results in behaviors that are locally safe but induce substantial latent risks in the long term. To address this, a World Model-based Risk-aware Mixture-of-Experts (WM-RMoE) framework is proposed. First, a learned latent dynamics model facilitates parallel multi-step rollouts, elevating safety assessment from the action level to the trajectory level via cumulative risk evaluation. Second, to enhance robustness under varying interaction intensities, a hierarchical gating mechanism dynamically coordinates experts across long-horizon, short-horizon, and rule-based safety modules. Furthermore, a Gaussian Mixture Model is integrated to preserve multimodal maneuvering branches, thereby mitigating the issue of behavioral mode averaging. Experimental results demonstrate that WM-RMoE significantly outperforms representative baselines in terms of safety compliance, decision stability, and generalization capability. Furthermore, benefiting from the risk-aware formulation, the proposed framework uniquely exhibits the ability to generate foresighted and semantically distinct overtaking maneuvers across diverse traffic densities.

</details>


### [117] [Beyond Object Selection:Markerless Gaze-based Robot Placement at Arbitrary Position](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00478)
*Yuzhi Lai,William Marx,Shenghai Yuan,Peizheng Li,Zhuoyu Ran,Andreas Zell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向注视点（gaze）辅助机械臂操控的跨设备对齐框架，引入基于图参考选择的方法，并提出任务级评估指标GSIE（Gaze–Surface Intersection Error），用于衡量注视点指定目标的实际空间误差，替代传统位姿精度指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统注视点操控中，头显与机器人之间需要精确的空间对齐，但位姿精度不一定转化为任务精度——平移和旋转误差会共同影响注视射线且可能相互补偿。因此需要从任务导向视角研究跨设备对齐问题。

Method: 提出markerless交互框架和跨设备数据集；提出基于图的参考选择（Graph-based Reference Selection）以应对稀疏机器人参考点；在统一协议下开发并基准测试多个任务特定对齐管线；引入GSIE指标直接度量注视点指定目标的空间误差。

Result: 实验表明，在传统位姿度量下排名靠前的对齐方法在GSIE指标下并非最优，说明任务级评估对注视点操控的重要性。

Conclusion: 注视点操控的评估应以任务级精度为准，而非单纯的位姿精度，本文的GSIE指标和基准数据集为后续跨设备对齐研究提供了任务导向的评估基础。

Abstract: Gaze-based assistive manipulation typically supports object selection, while arbitrary-position placement requires accurate spatial alignment between the headset and robot. However, for gaze-based manipulation, pose accuracy does not necessarily translate into task accuracy: translational and rotational errors jointly affect the transformed gaze ray and may compensate for each other. To study cross-device alignment from this task-oriented perspective, we present a markerless interaction framework and a dedicated cross-device dataset. We propose Graph-based Reference Selection to address sparse robot references. We further develop and benchmark multiple task-specific alignment pipelines under a unified protocol. Specifically, we introduce Gaze--Surface Intersection Error (GSIE), which directly measures the spatial error of the gaze-specified target. Experiments show that alignment methods ranked highly by conventional pose metrics are not always optimal in GSIE, demonstrating the importance of evaluating gaze-based manipulation at the task level.

</details>


### [118] [Exploring Nonlinear Body Oscillations for Natural Quadruped Gaits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00539)
*Annika Schmidt,Davide Calzolari,Florian Loeffl,Arne Sachtler,Daniel Seidel,Milan Herrmann,Robert Burger,Thomas Gumpert,Antonin Raffin,Tristan Ehlert,Maximilian Pries,David Wandinger,Florian Schmidt,Manuel Keppler,Jinoh Lee,Alin Albu-Schäffer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个利用非线性共振实现多步态涌现的四足机器人设计框架。作者在高度柔顺的四足机器人eBert上，通过新的理论工具识别出六个非线性正规模态（NNMs），并结合黑盒优化确定步长，使每个NNM在仿真和硬件上自然发展成不同速度的步态，展示了通过机械共振和形体智能实现高效运动控制的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数四足机器人没有专门利用机械共振来简化控制，因为非线性动力学复杂且需要专用运动控制器。作者希望通过利用机器人的形体智能（embodied intelligence），使多步态运动从非线性共振中自然涌现，从而减少主动控制的依赖。

Method: 作者提出了一个使机器人非线性动力学在设计阶段可预测的理论框架，在高度柔顺的四足机器人eBert上识别六个非线性正规模态（NNMs），用黑盒优化确定步长，使每个模态在仿真中发展为不同步态，并验证其在硬件上的迁移。

Result: 仿真和硬件实验均验证了六个NNMs的存在；仿真中每个NNM自然涌现为不同速度的步态，这些步态在很大程度上迁移到了实际的eBert机器人硬件上，展示了利用机械特性生成任务特定运动的能力。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了通过将非线性动力学变成可预测的设计知识，机器人可以利用机械共振和形体智能自发产生多种步态，为设计新一代敏捷高效的机器人提供了基础方向。

Abstract: Animals' body morphology shapes the gait patterns they can perform, where mechanical resonance reduces the need for active control. By tuning posture and muscle stiffness, they leverage their embodied intelligence to achieve effective gaits for different speeds. In contrast, most quadruped robots are not specifically designed to exploit mechanical resonance due to the complexity of nonlinear dynamics and require dedicated locomotion controllers. To provide an alternative, we present a proof of concept framework making the nonlinear dynamics of a robot predictable in the design process and show how this knowledge can be leveraged such that multi-gait locomotion can emerge from nonlinear resonances, shaped by gravity, inertia, and elasticity. We present the highly compliant quadruped robot eBert, on which we identify six nonlinear normal modes (NNMs) using our new theoretical tools and validate their existence in simulation and hardware. With black-box optimization to determine step length, simulations show how each NNM naturally develops into a distinct gait, manifesting different speeds, which also largely transfers to the robotic hardware. Our experiments show that eBert can exploit its mechanics to generate task-specific movements which may serve as foundation for designing a new generation of agile and efficient robots leveraging embodied intelligence.

</details>


### [119] [Potential-Guided Particle Steering for Negation-Constrained Dexterous Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00555)
*Geonho Kim,SooGon Kim,Jongmin Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个推理时框架，解决语言驱动的灵巧抓取模型中"避免某区域"的约束指令系统性失败问题。通过结合序贯蒙特卡洛和分类器自由引导，在不需负面训练样本的情况下引导采样避开禁区。构建NegGrasp基准（含正/负配对指令和约束感知指标），将最强制基线的违例率从57.9%降至17.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言驱动的灵巧抓取模型（如DextER）仅在指令指定"抓哪里"时表现良好，但遇到"避免哪里"（如"抓住手柄但避开机身"）的否定指令时系统性失败。训练语料（如DexGYSNet）几乎不含回避指令，为每种约束收集样例不可行；且模型中出现的每个部位都是接触目标，模型可能把禁止部位误解为另一个抓取区域。

Method: 提出一个无需否定专用训练样例的推理时框架。核心思路：(1) 结合序贯蒙特卡洛（SMC）与分类器自由引导（CFG），引导采样朝向指定部位，同时剪枝指向禁止区域的候选；(2) 使用冻结的3D部位接地模型从语言指令中定位禁止区域；(3) 构建NegGrasp基准，包含配对的正/负指令及约束感知评估指标（仅在既完成任务又遵守约束时才给予抓取认可）。

Result: 在NegGrasp基准上，该方法将最强基线的违例率从57.9%降至17.2%，同时提升了约束感知成功率和物理成功率。

Conclusion: 通过推理时的SMC+CFG框架和3D部位接地模型，无需否定训练样例即可有效处理灵巧抓取中的否定约束，显著降低了违例率并提升整体任务表现。

Abstract: Language-driven dexterous grasp models, such as DextER, perform well when instructions specify where to grasp, but we find they fail systematically when an instruction also specifies where not to grasp (e.g., "grasp the handle but avoid the body"). Existing training corpora, DexGYSNet among them, contain virtually no avoidance instructions, and collecting examples for every possible constraint is impractical. Moreover, because every part mentioned during training denotes a contact target, models may interpret a forbidden part as another region to grasp rather than one to avoid. We therefore introduce an inference-time framework for negation-constrained dexterous grasping that requires no negation-specific training examples. Combining Sequential Monte Carlo with classifier-free guidance, our method guides sampling toward the instructed part while pruning candidates headed for the forbidden region, without any negation examples during training. A frozen 3D part-grounding model localizes the forbidden region from the language instruction. To evaluate this setting, we construct NegGrasp, a benchmark of paired positive/negative instructions with constraint-aware metrics that credit a grasp only if it both accomplishes the task and respects the stated constraint. On NegGrasp, our method reduces the violation rate of the strongest baseline from 57.9% to 17.2% while improving both constraint-aware and physical success.

</details>


### [120] [DSG: Dynamic 3D Scene Graph Construction for Embodied Agents in Changing Indoor Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00619)
*Ming Liao,Chao Ye,Jianing Fei,Weiyang Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了DSG，一个动态3D场景图构建框架，通过语义感知的3D高斯场景表示和双视图渲染来检测物体变化并更新节点，同时利用多粒度视觉上下文让大语言模型进行空间关系推理。还构建了基于AI2-THOR的动态场景图基准DynTHOR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内环境中物体位置常因人类活动或智能体交互而改变，导致已有场景图与实际场景不一致，现有方法缺乏在动态环境中更新场景图和推理空间关系的能力。

Method: 1) 构建语义感知的3D高斯场景表示，提出基于双视图渲染的物体变化检测方法，可靠更新场景图节点；2) 提出融合多粒度视觉上下文的空间关系推理方法，利用LLM识别更丰富的物体间空间关系；3) 在AI2-THOR上构建DynTHOR动态室内场景图基准。

Result: 在DynTHOR、3RScan和真实世界场景上的大量实验表明，DSG在物体节点构建和空间关系推理方面均持续优于现有方法，显著提升动态场景图构建的准确性。

Conclusion: DSG通过结合3D高斯表示、双视图变化检测和LLM空间推理，有效解决了动态环境下场景图的一致性问题，并提供了可评估的基准。

Abstract: In indoor environments, object positions frequently change due to human activities or embodied-agent interactions, causing previously constructed scene graphs to become inconsistent with the current scene. To address this issue, we propose DSG, a dynamic 3D scene graph construction framework that detects object changes and performs spatial relationship reasoning. First, we construct a semantic-aware 3D Gaussian scene representation and develop a dual-view rendering-based object change detection method to enable reliable scene graph node updates. Second, we propose a spatial relationship reasoning method that incorporates multi-granularity visual context, enabling a large language model to identify a richer set of interobject spatial relationships. Furthermore, we introduce DynTHOR, a dynamic indoor scene graph benchmark built on the AI2-THOR simulation platform for evaluating scene graph construction in dynamic environments. Extensive experiments on Dyn-THOR, 3RScan, and real-world scenes demonstrate that DSG consistently outperforms existing methods in both object node construction and spatial relationship reasoning, significantly improving the accuracy of dynamic scene graph construction.

</details>


### [121] [AM-Bench: A Modular Simulation Suite and Benchmark for Aerial Manipulation Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00641)
*Yutong Wang,Dongjae Lee,Xiaofeng Guo,Yuanzhu Zhan,Yufei Jiang,Bavin Saravanan,Muqing Cao,Jia Xie,Chenyang Mao,Sebastian Scherer,Junyi Geng,Guanya Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AM-Bench 是一个面向多旋翼空中操纵（AM）策略学习的模块化仿真套件与基准测试，涵盖欠驱动、全驱动和过驱动三种飞行平台，12个任务，以及可配置的气动扰动、执行器饱和、标准底层控制器与基线策略学习算法。论文通过三项仿真研究和真实世界硬件验证，展示了其在系统层面评估本体、控制、扰动与策略选择交互作用的诊断价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操纵基准大多聚焦地面支撑系统，难以覆盖空中操纵（AM）这类动态敏感的领域。AM面临环境扰动、操纵器与浮动机体耦合动力学、自由度受限等系统性挑战，任务性能同时取决于机器人本体、底层控制与高层策略设计，亟需一个支持系统级评估的基准平台。

Method: 构建模块化仿真套件 AM-Bench：包含多种代表性本体（欠驱动/全驱动/过驱动）、12个任务（接触、运输、受约束交互）、可配置气动扰动与执行器饱和模型、标准底层控制器及基线策略学习算法；通过三项仿真研究（高层策略、策略-控制接口、本体差异）加真实世界硬件测试验证其诊断能力。

Result: 三项仿真研究揭示了本体、底层控制器、扰动与高层策略之间的交互作用，验证了AM-Bench作为诊断工具的能力；对建模效应进行了真实世界验证，并完成了学习管线的硬件测试。

Conclusion: AM-Bench填补了空中操纵策略学习缺少系统级基准的空白，支持对"本体-控制-策略"联合设计的诊断评估，对未来AM策略学习研究具有参考价值。

Abstract: Standardized benchmarks have played a central role in advancing robot manipulation learning, yet most focus on ground-supported manipulation systems, which limits their applicability to dynamics-critical domains such as aerial manipulation (AM). AM presents distinct system-level challenges, including environmental disturbances, coupled dynamics between the manipulator and floating base, and constrained degrees of freedom. Consequently, task performance depends jointly on robot embodiment, low-level control, and high-level policy design. We introduce AM-Bench, a modular simulation suite and benchmark for multirotor-based AM policy learning. AM-Bench includes representative embodiments spanning underactuated, fully actuated, and overactuated systems, 12 tasks across contact, transport, and constrained interaction, configurable aerodynamic disturbances and actuator saturation, standard low-level controllers, and baseline policy-learning algorithms. Unlike prior manipulation benchmarks that primarily emphasize end-to-end policy performance, AM-Bench enables system-level evaluation of how embodiment, control, disturbances, and policy choices interact. We demonstrate its diagnostic value through three simulation studies spanning high-level policies, policy--control interfaces, and embodiments, together with real-world validation of modeled effects and a hardware test of the learning pipeline.

</details>


### [122] [Human-robot conversation with multiple participants in noisy public spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00648)
*Divesh Lala,Yogeeswaran Muthukumaran,Vincent Fernandes,Kazushi Kato,Shota Fujiki,Zihao Chi,Masaya Iwasaki,Taiken Shintani,Megumi Kawata,Kazuki Sakai,Koji Inoue,Yuicihiro Yoshikawa,Tatsuya Kawahara*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于嘈杂真实环境（如2025大阪世博会开放公共空间）的音频系统，支持两种场景：与安卓机器人ERICA的注意力聆听系统，以及配备移动Teleco机器人（其中一个作为远程操作员的化身）的对话支持系统。两种系统均支持多方对话，使用单一多通道麦克风阵列，不仅增强多说话人的语音，还提供空间音频以增强化身对话交互的沉浸感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在嘈杂的开放公共空间中，自主机器人和化身系统的口语对话系统需要增强的语音信号，既用于语音识别，也用于向远程操作员传输清晰语音，因此需要设计专门应对嘈杂多说话人环境的音频系统。

Method: 提出一个基于单多通道麦克风阵列的音频系统，通过语音增强和空间音频处理，同时支持机器人语音识别场景和化身远程对话传输场景，并在2025年大阪世博会上进行概念验证演示。

Result: 系统在2025年大阪世博会进行了概念验证演示，成功在嘈杂环境中增强多方对话语音，并提供空间音频以增强化身式对话交互的沉浸感。

Conclusion: 该音频系统证明了单一多通道麦克风阵列可以在真实嘈杂环境中同时服务于机器人语音识别和化身远程通信两个场景，空间音频进一步提升了交互沉浸感。

Abstract: For noisy real-world environments such as those in open public spaces, spoken dialogue systems for both autonomous robots and avatars should be carefully designed to provide enhanced speech signals. These signals can be used either for speech recognition or, in the case of an avatar system, transmitted as clean speech to a remote operator. This work proposes an audio system that can be used for both these scenarios and was demonstrated as a proof-of-concept at the 2025 World Expo in Osaka. The first scenario is an attentive listening system with the android ERICA, and the second is a conversation support system with mobile Teleco robots, with one of them acting as an avatar for a remote operator. Both systems feature multi-party conversation and use a single multi-channel microphone array. We describe how our audio system not only enhances the speech of multiple speakers in a noisy environment, but provides a form of spatial audio which allows for more immersiveness in avatar-based conversational interactions.

</details>


### [123] [Fleets Need a Context Plane: Rethinking Cooperative Perception for Autonomous Drones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00659)
*Liangkai Liu,Xiaoxiao Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对无人机编队协同感知中特征共享策略在运行期自适应能力不足的问题，提出"context plane"——一个有界、结构化的运行时上下文接口。每架无人机以10 Hz发布不超过1 KB的描述符，轻量级可替换策略利用机群上下文决定每架无人机计算、共享和融合的内容。在UAV3D上通过控制DiscoNet检查点的特征交换（无需重训练）验证：任务感知共享仅需5-10%字节即可匹配全共享精度；在ROS 2原型（Jetson AGX Orin）中，context plane仅消耗约0.01%数据平面带宽，每次策略决策仅0.10 ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协同感知系统在设计中固定特征共享策略，或仅对单一上下文信号进行自适应，无法适应无人机机群在执行中变化的带宽、编队几何、任务目标和场景覆盖等多维上下文需求。作者量化了"上下文盲共享"的成本，并指出需要同时考虑多个上下文轴。

Method: 提出"context plane"接口：(1) 每架无人机发布≤1 KB、10 Hz的上下文描述符；(2) 轻量级、可替换的策略模块根据机群上下文决定计算、共享和融合策略；(3) 现有共享方案可转化为该接口中的固定策略。在UAV3D上利用释放的DiscoNet检查点在评估时控制特征交换（无需重训练），并在ROS 2 + Jetson AGX Orin上构建原型验证开销。

Result: (1) 任务感知共享以5-10%的字节达到全共享精度；(2) 最佳peer选择策略随字节预算变化，错误策略最高损失7.7 AP；(3) 在受限预算下，全场景精度相同的两种策略在任务区域内相差5.9 AP，证明多个上下文轴需联合考虑；(4) 原型中context plane开销约为数据平面带宽的0.01%，每次策略决策仅0.10 ms。

Conclusion: 显式上下文接口（context plane）可以在不修改或重训练感知模型的前提下支持低开销的运行时自适应，为无人机编队协同感知提供了一种可扩展、模块化的策略管理框架。

Abstract: Cooperative perception allows a drone fleet to combine observations from multiple viewpoints. However, existing systems typically fix their feature-sharing policies at design time or adapt to only one context signal. This is a poor fit for aerial fleets, whose missions, bandwidth, formation geometry, and scene coverage can change during flight. We quantify the cost of context-blind sharing on UAV3D by controlling feature exchange at evaluation time using a released DiscoNet checkpoint, without retraining. Mission-aware sharing matches full-sharing accuracy while using only 5-10% of the bytes. The best tested peer selection policy changes with the byte budget, and choosing the wrong policy loses up to 7.7 AP. Moreover, under a constrained budget, two policies with the same full-scene accuracy differ by 5.9 AP within the mission region, showing that multiple context axes must be considered jointly. We therefore propose the context plane, a bounded, structured interface for runtime context. Each drone publishes a descriptor of at most 1 KB at 10 Hz, and lightweight, replaceable policies use the fleet context to decide what each drone computes, shares, and fuses. Existing sharing schemes become fixed policies within this interface. In our ROS 2 prototype on a Jetson AGX Orin, the context plane uses approximately 0.01% of the data-plane bandwidth, and each policy decision takes 0.10 ms. These results show that an explicit context interface can support low-overhead runtime adaptation without modifying or retraining the perception model.

</details>


### [124] [Behavior--Realization Separation for Constrained Physical Human--Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00669)
*Yongyan Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出行为层与实现层分离的物理人机交互控制框架：行为层给定期望接触端口加速度，实现层通过滚动时域二次规划（QP）将其转化为满足约束的机器人指令并显式报告期望与实现间的加速度误差。在7自由度Franka FR3（MuJoCo）上验证了阻抗/导纳行为在同一运行层下的无缝切换，以及扭矩可行性约束的跨时域保持优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统物理人机交互软件将期望行为描述与约束实现耦合，导致饱和等约束被隐藏而非显式处理，难以分离调节目标本身的缺陷与约束实现的限制。作者主张将行为规范（behavior layer）与约束实现（realization layer）解耦，并显式报告期望与实现的误差。

Method: 采用滚动时域二次规划（receding-horizon QP）作为实现层，处理无记忆仿射行为（如阻抗和导纳）。行为变更仅修改目标函数系数(Cθ,Gθ)，机器人指令变量与可行集保持不变。引入同目标的无约束反事实来分离正则化与约束干预的影响，并用真实植物数据暴露模型误差。运行时在MuJoCo中冻结任务空间动力学并跨时域强制扭矩可行性。

Result: 在持续20N推力下，松弛工作空间边界保持误差约0.1-0.2mm，而瞬时裁剪的阻抗/导纳分别过冲4.4cm和4.7cm。降额执行器预算下，时域全程扭矩约束保持可行误差2.1×10⁻⁴ N·m，而仅首步约束的消融超出预算11.329 N·m；在真实非线性植物上差距缩小但仍优于消融（0.161 vs 0.380 N·m）。

Conclusion: 实验结果作为行为-实现分离概念的重点验证，证明将行为描述与约束实现解耦，并通过显式误差报告与跨时域可行性保持，能显著改善人机交互的约束遵守行为。

Abstract: Physical human--robot interaction software often couples desired-behavior specification with constrained realization; we treat these as separate layers. A \emph{behavior layer} supplies a desired contact-port acceleration $a_k^{\mathrm{id}}=f_θ(e_k,\dot e_k,F_{h,k})$. A \emph{realization layer} converts it into constrained robot commands and reports total desired-versus-realized acceleration error instead of hiding it in saturation. A same-objective unconstrained counterfactual separates regularization from constraint intervention, while plant data expose model error. This paper implements a receding-horizon quadratic program realizing memoryless affine behaviors. Changing the behavior modifies objective coefficients through $(C_θ,G_θ)$ while the robot-command variable and feasible set remain unchanged. A planar study instantiates impedance and admittance; the same running layer accepts an impedance--admittance--impedance reassignment without reconstruction, under its existing rate limit. On a torque-controlled 7-DOF Franka FR3 in MuJoCo, the runtime freezes task-space dynamics per solve and enforces torque feasibility across its horizon. Under a sustained 20~N push, it holds a slack-relaxed workspace boundary to within approximately 0.1--0.2~mm, versus 4.4~cm (impedance) and 4.7~cm (admittance) overshoot from instantaneous clipping. A derated actuator budget then activates the torque constraint: horizon-wide enforcement keeps its frozen-model plan feasible to $2.1\times10^{-4}$~N$\cdot$m, whereas a first-step-only ablation plans up to 11.329~N$\cdot$m beyond budget; on the executed nonlinear plant, where both share the same local-model error, the gap is smaller but still favors horizon-wide enforcement (0.161 vs.\ 0.380~N$\cdot$m). These results are a focused proof of behavior--realization separation.

</details>


### [125] [ADAPT: Agile Diffusion Action Priors for Robust and Steerable Online Text-Driven Humanoid Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00677)
*Yan Wu,Chenhao Li,Kaifeng Zhao,Gen Li,Marco Hutter,Siyu Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ADAPT是用于交互式、文本条件的人形全身控制的端到端框架，通过扩散动作先验与轻量残差强化学习策略相结合，实现语言指令驱动的连续控制、平滑切换和多样化动作技能执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到动作（text-to-motion）流水线主要生成运动学动作供独立跟踪器执行，缺乏闭环控制能力，无法连续响应变化的语言命令并保持平衡和自然运动。本文旨在解决语言条件的人形全身控制问题。

Method: 1) 从文本标记的人形状态-动作轨迹中学习扩散动作先验，使多样化运动技能可直接由语言命令执行；2) 在冻结的扩散控制器之上训练轻量残差强化学习策略，提升长时间任务鲁棒性和平滑的提示切换；3) 同一扩散策略可复用为可引导的文本条件运动先验，用于下游任务适应。

Result: 实验证明了强健的语言接地技能执行、平滑的交互式转换，以及保持风格的下游控制。

Conclusion: ADAPT展示了端到端闭环框架在人形全身语言控制上的有效性，扩散先验与RL残差结合的架构可同时实现多样化技能、鲁棒性和平滑交互，且策略可复用为下游任务先验。

Abstract: We present ADAPT, an end-to-end framework for interactive, text-conditioned humanoid whole-body control. Unlike dominant text-to-motion pipelines that generate kinematic motions for a separate tracker, ADAPT solves language control with an end-to-end closed-loop control framework, where the robot must continuously respond to changing commands while maintaining balance, natural motion, and smooth transitions. ADAPT learns a diffusion-based action prior from text-labeled humanoid state-action trajectories, enabling diverse motion skills to be directly executed from language commands. To improve long-horizon robustness and smooth prompt switching, we train a lightweight residual reinforcement learning policy on top of the frozen diffusion controller. We further show that the same diffusion policy can be reused as a steerable text-conditioned motion prior for downstream task adaptation. Experiments demonstrate robust language-grounded skill execution, smooth interactive transitions, and style-preserving downstream control.

</details>


### [126] [Context-Aware Intelligent Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00682)
*Liangkai Liu,Shuyao Shi,Mingke Wang,Noah T. Curran,Chuan Li,Fan Bai,Kang G. Shin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了智能车辆中上下文感知（context-aware）方法，主张将“情境-环境因素”作为下一代车辆系统的一等公民原则，并应用于学习、风险评估和闭环控制。涵盖环境理解、规划控制、安全与网络安全、网联车辆四个领域，识别出多模态上下文融合、时间上下文建模、罕见事件处理、协作式上下文共享四大技术挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能车辆在资源受限平台上运行于复杂不确定的动态环境中，面临精度、延迟、成本和可靠性的严苛要求。作者认为现有系统未将“情境-环境因素（context-situational factors）”作为一等公民对待，导致难以满足下一代智能车辆应用的严苛需求。

Method: 这是一篇综述论文。作者系统回顾了四类上下文感知方法：（i）环境理解、（ii）规划与控制、（iii）安全与网络安全、（iv）网联车辆；并通过趋势分析识别设计模式，归纳出构建通用上下文引擎（general contextual engine）的四大技术挑战：多模态上下文融合、时间上下文建模、罕见事件处理和协作式上下文共享。

Result: 通过趋势分析识别出四个关键技术挑战：多模态上下文融合、时间上下文建模、处理罕见事件和协作式上下文共享，并据此提出未来智能车辆需要构建统一的、共享的上下文状态，作为全软件栈中学习、风险评估和闭环控制的基础。

Conclusion: 作者希望该综述能激励鲁棒且高效的上下文感知车辆应用的开发，强调上下文应被制度化为一等公民原则，而非各模块的局部附加功能。

Abstract: Intelligent vehicles increasingly support adaptive applications beyond driving themselves, ranging from context-aware ADAS and automated driving to in-cabin monitoring and fleet management, all under tight requirements on accuracy, latency, cost, and reliability. Meeting these requirements is challenging because vehicles operate in complex, uncertain, and rapidly changing environments while running on resource-constrained computing platforms. This paper argues that context-situational factors that give meaning to sensor signals and constrain decisions-should be treated as a first-class principle for next-generation vehicle systems, and operationalized as a unified, shared state for learning, risk assessment, and closed-loop control across the software stack. We systematically review state-of-the- art (SOTA) context-aware methods spanning (i) environment understanding, (ii) planning and control, (iii) safety and security, and (iv) connected vehicles. Based on a trend analysis of context-aware design, we identify four key technical challenges in building a general contextual engine for future intelligent vehicles: multi-modal context fusion, temporal context modeling, handling rare events, and collaborative context sharing. We hope this survey will motivate the development of robust and efficient context-aware vehicle applications.

</details>


### [127] [One Print, Many Moves: Monolithic Origami-inspired Folding Actuator for Composable Soft Multi-DoF Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00751)
*Jaehyung Jang,Zhenish Zhakypov,Jasmin Elena Palmer,Melissa Klein,Jee-Hwan Ryu,Allison Mariko Okamura*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MONORIGAMI，一种基于仿折纸理念的软体折叠驱动器设计，通过空间编程的刚度各向异性实现精确、可重复、可组合的软体多自由度运动，可单材料单次3D打印免组装，并演示了四个系统：紧致4-DoF可穿戴触觉设备、3-DoF遥操作摇杆、模块化夹爪和水下抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统软体机器人执行器虽具柔性但变形不可控，精度差且难以扩展至多自由度系统，需要一种既保持材料柔顺性又能精确编程运动的设计策略。

Method: 基于刚度分层的空间编程各向异性：将材料按厚度分为多层刚度层级，以折纸启发的几何（面片与折痕）图案化排列，沿期望折叠方向保持柔顺、在非期望方向限制变形，实现无附加增强元件的纯几何编程折叠运动；每个驱动器为可缩放运动基元，通过连接和定向多个基元机械编程多DoF轨迹。

Result: 用同一模块制作了三个软体多自由度机器人系统：4-DoF可穿戴VR触觉设备、3-DoF遥操作力反馈摇杆、可水下运行的模块化抓取器，验证了紧凑多轴集成、可控物理交互和几何编程操作。

Conclusion: MONORIGAMI提供了一种通用、可组合、易于制造、可靠且可扩展的高精度软体多自由度机器人平台，解决软体执行器设计和制造中长期存在的限制。

Abstract: Conventional soft robot actuators excel in compliance, but their uncontrolled deformations compromise accuracy and hinder scaling to multi-degree-of-freedom (DoF) systems. We introduce a MONOlithic ORIGAMI-inspired soft folding actuator design (MONORIGAMI) that establishes a design strategy based on spatially programmed stiffness anisotropy to preserve material compliance along desired folding directions while selectively restricting deformation in unwanted directions. The actuator leverages stiffness tiers based on material thickness, patterned in an origami-inspired geometry with facets and creases, converting unconstrained soft deformation into accurate, repeatable, and composable folding motions without additional reinforcements. The design is fully 3D-printable through a single-material, single-print process that requires no assembly. Each actuator serves as a scalable motion primitive, and linking and orienting multiple actuators mechanically programs multi-DoF trajectories. Using the same fundamental module, we demonstrate three 3D-printed soft multi-DoF robotic systems spanning distinct application domains: (1) a compact 4-DoF wearable haptic device for high-fidelity cutaneous feedback in virtual reality (VR), (2) a 3-DoF joystick for kinesthetic feedback in teleoperation, and (3) a modular robotic gripper capable of underwater operation with geometry-encoded grasp trajectories. These systems demonstrate the module's capabilities for compact multi-axis integration, controlled physical interaction, and geometry-programmed operation across different environments. Together, these results show that MONORIGAMI provides a general, composable, accessible, reliable, and scalable platform for high-precision soft multi-DoF robotics, addressing long-standing limitations in both soft actuator design and fabrication.

</details>


### [128] [A Compact Robotic Finger with 2-DoF MCP Joint Embedding DoF-Selective Passive Continuously Variable Transmission for Wide Force-Speed Operating Range](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00769)
*JaeHyung Jang,Jee-Hwan Ryu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种具有选择性被动无级变速器(CVT)的紧凑型二自由度机器人手指，受人类掌指关节功能分化启发，仅在屈曲-伸展自由度上嵌入力响应式被动CVT，在保持外展-内收直驱的同时实现宽力-速度工作范围，实验显示最大输出力放大4.19倍，平均3.6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多自由度机器人手在紧凑尺寸下难以同时实现宽力-速度工作范围。传统方案需要额外执行器或传感器实现变速，增加体积和复杂度。受人类掌指关节功能分化启发，作者希望在不增加任何执行器、传感器或控制的前提下，实现机构层面的被动变速。

Method: 提出一种输出侧被动CVT，采用移动滑轮启发的线缆布线结构。屈曲路径中的线缆张力作用于滑轮，被动增加屈曲力臂和传动比以响应负载变化，无需额外硬件。外展-内收路径保持直接传动以保留灵巧操作能力。

Result: 在15°至75°屈曲角范围内，最大输出力放大4.19倍，平均放大3.6倍，通过力臂自适应显著扩展力-速度工作范围。球滚动灵巧操作实验验证了被动变速同时保留外展-内收功能。

Conclusion: 该设计展示了紧凑多自由度机器人手的可扩展传动设计策略，通过功能分化的自由度特异性传动配置，实现被动力-速度自适应的轻量化方案。

Abstract: This letter presents a compact two-degree-of-freedom (DoF) robotic finger with a flexion-selective passive continuously variable transmission (CVT) to achieve a wide force-speed operating range. Inspired by the functional differentiation of the human metacarpophalangeal (MCP) joint, the proposed mechanism realizes DoF-specific transmission differentiation by selectively assigning passive CVT to the flexion-extension DoF while preserving direct transmission for abduction-adduction. For a wide force-speed operating range, a force-responsive passive CVT is embedded in the flexion pathway, while direct transmission is preserved for the abduction-adduction pathway. To selectively realize transmission adaptation within a multi-DoF MCP mechanism, an output-side passive CVT employing a moving-pulley-inspired wire-routing structure is introduced. The resultant force generated by the wire tensions acting on the pulley that passively increases the flexion moment arm and transmission ratio according to the applied load without additional actuators, sensors, or control. Experimental results demonstrate a maximum output-force amplification of 4.19-fold and a mean amplification of 3.6-fold across the tested flexion angles ranging from 15 degrees to 75 degrees through moment-arm adaptation, thereby substantially expanding the achievable force-speed operating range. Furthermore, dexterous ball-rolling experiments verify that passive transmission adaptation can be achieved while preserving abduction-adduction functionality. These results demonstrate a scalable transmission design strategy for compact multi-DoF robotic hands.

</details>


### [129] [Non-Prehensile Throwing: A Reinforcement Learning Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00771)
*Abdullah Mustafa,Ryo Hanai,Ixchel G. Ramirez-Alpizar,Floris Erich,Ryoichi Nakajo,Yukiyasu Domae,Tetsuya Ogata*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于强化学习的非抓取式机器人投掷方法，直接优化关节空间轨迹，充分利用滑动和滚动接触模式，在仿真和真实UR5e机器人上均实现高成功率投掷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非抓取式投掷方法依赖基于简化接触模型的优化和低维轨迹参数化，限制了求解质量和可达工作空间。需要一种无需解析接触模型、能充分利用多种接触模式（滑动、滚动）的方法。

Method: 将投掷问题建模为马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），直接优化关节空间轨迹（关节加加速度轨迹）。采用离线低控制率规划+高频插值的部署策略。通过最小加加速度系统辨识缩小sim-to-real动力学差距，并训练感知不确定性的策略以缓解物体建模误差（特别是动摩擦敏感度）。

Result: 仿真中在数千个配置上达到99%成功率并泛化到未见物体。真实UR5e机器人在近极限（5 m/s末端速度）下零样本部署，可投掷重790g、大20x20x28cm的物体，目标距离达350cm或高度180cm，真实世界成功率97%。敏感性分析显示对质量不确定性鲁棒但对动摩擦高度敏感。

Conclusion: 强化学习结合多接触模式（滑动、滚动）的非抓取式投掷方法能够超越基于模型的传统方法，实现更大的工作空间和更好的物体泛化能力，且具备良好的sim-to-real迁移性。

Abstract: Robotic throwing enables fast object transport and extends a robot's reachable workspace beyond traditional pick-and-place. While prehensile (grasp-based) throwing works well for graspable items, non-prehensile (grasp-free) throwing is better suited for large, heavy, and/or deformable objects. Existing approaches rely on model-based optimization with simplified contact models (e.g., dynamic grasping) and low-dimensional trajectory parameterizations, which limit solution quality and reachable workspace. We propose a reinforcement learning approach that additionally leverages sliding and rolling contact modes and directly optimizes joint-space trajectories without analytical contact models or custom parameterizations. The Markov Decision Process (MDP) is formulated as a dynamical system that evolves the robot's joint state conditioned on the throwing target, object model, and initial configuration. Joint-jerk trajectories are planned offline at a low control rate and upsampled into smooth, high-rate velocity commands for deployment. For sim-to-real transfer, we minimize the robot-dynamics gap through minimum-jerk system identification and train uncertainty-aware policies to mitigate object-modeling errors, particularly sensitivity to dynamic friction. In simulation, the policy achieves 99% success across thousands of configurations and generalizes to unseen objects. Sensitivity analysis shows robustness to mass uncertainty but high sensitivity to dynamic friction, consistent with the sliding-based release mechanism. Deployed zero-shot on a UR5e operating near its physical limits (5 m/s end-effector velocity), our method throws diverse objects including heavy (790 g) and large (20x20x28 cm) items to targets up to 350 cm distance or 180 cm elevation, achieving a 97% real-world success rate.

</details>


### [130] [Connectivity-Aware Graph Extension for Decentralized Multi-Robot Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00804)
*Béatrice Garcia Cegarra,Elena Vanneaux,Quentin Picard,David Filliat*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于前沿连通性的探索图扩展策略，用于多无人机在间歇通信条件下的去中心化协同探索。该策略通过扩展探索计划并维持智能体间区域划分的稳定性，对两种最先进的区域划分方法进行了增强，并在仿真中验证了在低通信率下相比现有图扩展方法具有更高的探索效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多无人机在未知环境中探索需要协调，但通信是间歇性的、不可靠的，这使得去中心化成为基本假设。现有图扩展方法在通信中断和空间布局变化时难以维持稳定的区域划分，导致探索效率下降。

Method: 提出一种基于前沿连通性的探索图扩展策略，在去中心化框架下扩展探索计划，同时保持智能体间区域划分对断连和空间布局变化的稳定性与鲁棒性。该扩展方法被应用到两种最先进的区域划分方法中进行集成。

Result: 在仿真实验中，所提出的方法相比现有图扩展方法展现了改进的性能，在低通信率下具有更高的探索效率。

Conclusion: 基于前沿连通性的图扩展策略能有效提升多无人机去中心化协同探索在间歇通信条件下的效率和鲁棒性，且作为通用扩展模块可适配不同的区域划分方法。

Abstract: Exploring unknown environments with multiple UAVs requires coordination under intermittent communication, making decentralized operation a baseline assumption. We propose, within a decentralized framework, a novel exploration graph extension strategy based on frontier connectivity to extend exploration plans and maintain area partitioning among agents stable and robust to disconnections and changes in spatial layout. The proposed extension method is applied to two state-of-the-art area partitioning methods and evaluated in simulation. Experiments show improved performance over existing graph extension approaches with higher exploration efficiency under low communication rate.

</details>


### [131] [Peg-in-Bench: A Modular Benchmark for High-Precision Robotic Insertion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00906)
*Yosel Delgado,José G. Buenaventura-Carreón,Floris Erich,Roman Mykhailyshyn,Tomohiro Motoda,Koshi Makihara,Yukiyasu Domae*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个可重构的peg-in-hole基准测试集，旨在评估高精度插入任务中的泛化能力。该基准包含完全3D打印的模块化组件，支持多种peg几何形状、公差级别和可配置的基座结构，能够组合生成大量不同的插入和装配任务，同时提供场景生成工具以确保可复现性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有peg-in-hole基准测试依赖固定任务配置，无法充分评估机器人在不同插入场景下的鲁棒性和泛化能力，需要一种可系统变化任务配置的基准来评估机器人对未见场景的适应能力。

Method: 设计了一套3D可打印的模块化组件系统，包括多种peg几何形状、不同公差级别和可配置的基座结构；通过变化物体布局、方向和任务结构同时保持物理条件可控，实现系统化评估；提供场景生成工具生成标准化任务配置和机器可读的任务描述。

Result: 提供了完整的基准设计、STL文件以及场景生成工具，代码和组件文件已开源在GitHub仓库中，支持跨不同插入场景的系统性泛化评估。

Conclusion: 该基准为高精度插入任务的泛化能力评估提供了标准化且可复现的框架，有望推动机器人在多样化插入场景下的鲁棒性研究。

Abstract: High-precision insertion remains a fundamental challenge in robotic manipulation due to the strict alignment requirements and contact-rich interactions involved. Although peg-in-hole tasks are widely used for evaluation, existing bench- marks often rely on fixed task configurations, limiting their ability to assess robustness and generalization across different insertion scenarios. This paper introduces a reconfigurable peg-in-hole benchmark designed to evaluate task generalization in high-precision insertion. The benchmark consists of a set of fully 3D-printable modular components, including multiple peg geometries, tolerance levels, and configurable base structures that can be combined to generate a large variety of insertion and assembly tasks. By varying object layouts, orientations, and task structures while maintaining controlled physical conditions, the benchmark enables systematic evaluation of adaptation to unseen scenarios. To support reproducibility, we additionally provide a scenario generation tool capable of producing standardized task configurations and machine-readable task descriptions. The scenario generation tool and the STL files of the benchmark pieces are available through the project repository: https://github.com/aistairc/peg-in-bench.

</details>


### [132] [Knowing When to Stop: Adaptive Action Chunking via Internal Cross-Attention Dynamics in VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00908)
*Runze Xu,Xiaolong Shan,Shuang Dai,Yu Wang,Jincheng Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于交叉注意力熵的自适应动作分块（adaptive action chunking）方法，在VLA推理时动态选择执行步长，无需额外训练，通过检测注意力熵平台期来截断开放循环执行长度，在π0.5和X-VLA上于RoboTwin 2.0、LIBERO和三类真实操控任务中取得优于固定步长基线的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA中固定执行步长存在效率与精度的权衡：短chunk需频繁推理易振荡，长chunk会与新观测状态失配。需要一种在推理时动态适应执行长度的机制。

Method: 核心观察：预测步长延伸时，action-to-observation交叉注意力熵上升并趋向平台期，该模式与高预测误差相关。据此提出training-free的截断机制，在线检测持续高熵平台期并动态选择执行步长；仅利用策略已计算的注意力权重，额外开销可忽略。

Result: 在π0.5和X-VLA上，于RoboTwin 2.0、LIBERO和三个真实世界操控任务中，平均任务成功率优于固定步长和其他自适应chunking基线，同时保持高效闭环控制。

Conclusion: 交叉注意力动态可作为VLA中自适应动作执行的有效内部信号，在不增加训练负担的情况下同时改善精度与效率。

Abstract: Action chunking is a standard execution strategy in modern Vision-Language-Action (VLA) frameworks, but fixed execution horizons impose a trade-off between efficiency and accuracy. Short chunks require frequent inference and may cause oscillatory behavior, whereas long chunks can become misaligned with newly observed states. We address this limitation with an adaptive action chunking approach based on internal cross-attention dynamics in the action expert. We observe that, as the prediction horizon extends, action-to-observation cross-attention becomes increasingly dispersed and its entropy rises toward a plateau. This pattern is associated with higher action prediction error and provides an online signal that the current observation offers limited grounding for further open-loop execution. Based on this observation, we introduce a training-free truncation mechanism that detects sustained high-entropy plateaus and dynamically selects the execution horizon during inference. The method uses attention weights already computed by the policy and introduces negligible additional overhead. Evaluations on $π_{0.5}$ and X-VLA across RoboTwin 2.0, LIBERO, and three real-world manipulation tasks show improved average task success over fixed-horizon and adaptive chunking baselines, while preserving efficient closed-loop control. These results show that cross-attention dynamics can provide a practical internal signal for adaptive action execution in VLAs.

</details>


### [133] [VerNav: Verifier-First Low-Latency Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00920)
*Zhixin Wang,Chengzheyi Yao,Leyuan Liu,Xiaosong Zhang,Yongzhao Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VerNav 提出了一种以验证器为先的低延迟 LLM 视觉-语言导航（VLN）框架。它用批量的动作验证替代逐步自回归生成，仅在不确定时调用基于熵的自适应生成器，并通过 VPO 两阶段对齐和步级强化微调提升导航性能。在 R2R 基准上比自回归方法降低了超过 10 倍的每步决策延迟，同时保持有竞争力的导航性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLN 中基于 LLM 的自回归生成在每一步都产生大量决策延迟，在长程多步导航中累积严重。作者希望在不牺牲指令理解和语义接地能力的前提下，大幅降低决策阶段的延迟。

Method: VerNav 采用验证器优先框架：1) 验证器对候选动作进行批量验证，替代逐步自回归生成以降低延迟；2) 基于熵的自适应生成器仅在验证器不确定时被调用，生成紧凑的状态证据；3) 两阶段对齐方案：VPO 在静态验证器训练中改进局部动作偏好对齐，步级强化微调在动态多步导航中提供密集进度奖励。

Result: 在 Room-to-Room (R2R) 基准上，VerNav 的验证器-only 决策路径在有代表性的 LLM 基础 VLN 智能体中取得了有竞争力的导航性能，同时将每步的平均决策阶段 LLM 延迟相比自回归方法降低了超过 10 倍。

Conclusion: 验证器优先的架构可以有效分离动作验证与生成，在不显著牺牲导航性能的前提下大幅降低 VLN 决策延迟，为 LLM 基础的多步具身导航提供了实用的低延迟路径。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) requires an agent to navigate through unseen 3D environments according to natural-language instructions. Explicit reasoning can improve instruction understanding and semantic grounding, but autoregressive generation at every step accumulates large decision-stage latency over multi-step navigation. We propose VerNav, a verifier-first framework for low-latency LLM-based VLN. The verifier reduces decision-stage latency by replacing per-step autoregressive generation with batched action verification, while an entropy-based adaptive generator is invoked only for uncertain decisions to produce compact state evidence. To further improve navigation performance with the verifier, we introduce a two-stage alignment scheme: (i) VPO improves local action-preference alignment in static verifier training, and (ii) step-level reinforcement fine-tuning provides dense progress rewards over multi-step navigation rollouts during dynamic task execution. Experiments on the Room-to-Room (R2R) benchmark show that the verifier-only decision path of VerNav achieves competitive navigation performance among representative LLM-based VLN agents while reducing average decision-stage LLM latency per step by more than $10\times$ compared with autoregressive methods.

</details>


### [134] [ProxPI: Proximal Prior Injection for Sampling-Based MPC under Learned-Prior Mismatch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00941)
*Euncheol Im,Myotaeg Lim,Yisoo Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ProxPI（近端先验注入）方法，用于策略引导的MPPI控制。与现有将采样分布以策略输出为中心（policy-centered warm-start）的方法不同，ProxPI保留以标称（无策略）为中心MPPI采样，并通过软近端代价将策略先验纳入优化。理论上证明re-centering每次更新都会丢弃优化器的修正，而nominal-centered采样能保留修正并收敛到任务代价与先验共同定义的解集。实验表明在分布内和分布外任务上均表现出鲁棒性，能在策略失效时恢复到vanilla MPPI性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有policy-guided MPPI方法中，以策略输出为中心的warm-start在策略先验不匹配（out-of-distribution）时，会把采样分布限制在不当解附近，阻碍优化器向任务最优恢复。需要一种既能利用学习先验、又能在先验失效时保持优化器恢复能力的机制。

Method: ProxPI保留nominal-centered（任务代价引导的）MPPI采样，通过软近端代价（soft proximity cost）将策略输出作为吸引项引入目标函数，而非直接重新居中采样分布。理论分析比较了两种思路在每次更新中优化器修正量的保留情况。

Result: 在分布内任务上匹配现有prior-injection方案的性能；在策略失准的分布外任务上能逃离错误策略并恢复到vanilla MPPI级别的性能。仿真与真实机器人实验均验证鲁棒性。理论表明re-centering在更大rollout预算下仍然无法修复。

Conclusion: ProxPI提供了一种更稳健的策略-优化器结合范式：软代价注入优于采样分布重定位，在享受先验加速的同时保住优化器的纠错能力，适合安全关键或任务分布可能漂移的场景。

Abstract: Combining learned policies with model predictive control can leverage learned task priors while retaining online adaptation to new objectives and constraints, but performance degrades when the policy is out of distribution. In policy-guided model predictive path integral (MPPI) control, a policy-centered warm-start approach centers the sampling distribution on the policy output. When the prior is mismatched, centering the sampling distribution on the policy output restricts exploration around an unsuitable solution and prevents recovery toward the task optimum. We propose Proximal Prior Injection (ProxPI), which retains nominal-centered MPPI sampling and incorporates the policy through a soft proximity cost. This matches the in-distribution performance of existing prior-injection schemes while enabling the optimizer to escape an inaccurate policy and recover vanilla MPPI-level performance. We theoretically show that re-centering on the prior discards the optimizer's correction at every update, whereas nominal-centered sampling retains it and converges to a solution set by both the task cost and the prior, and that this failure is not removed by a larger rollout budget. Simulations and real-robot experiments demonstrate robust performance under both in-distribution and out-of-distribution tasks.

</details>


### [135] [HitMem: Hierarchical Temporal 3D Memory with Multi-Modal Context-Aware Retrieval for Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00950)
*Ruijie Tang,Chenye Zou,Guoquan Wu,Jun Wei,Wei Chen,Jiaxin Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HitMem是一个层次化时序3D记忆框架，用于动态环境中的具身智能体。它通过连续感知将语义和空间信息统一为轻量级拓扑图，利用时间衰减机制动态调节记忆活跃度，并设计了多模态上下文感知检索机制，在检测到物体位移时结合外部智能体轨迹的空间约束和类别亲和性的语义常识进行两阶段检索，从而解决物体移动导致的记忆-观测冲突。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D记忆框架大多假设静态世界，当物体被人类活动或未观测事件位移时，智能体会遇到记忆-观测冲突，需要昂贵的几何重计算或低效的全局重新探索。缺乏一种能够自适应动态环境变化、高效处理物体位置变化的长时任务执行方案。

Method: HitMem包含三个核心组件：(1) 层次化时序3D记忆框架，通过连续感知将语义和空间信息统一为轻量级拓扑图，捕捉支撑关系；(2) 时间衰减机制，动态调控记忆活跃度以缓解过时表示的影响；(3) 多模态上下文感知检索机制，默认使用语义、空间和时间记忆特征过滤候选，并在检测到物体位移时激活专门的两阶段检索流程，结合外部智能体轨迹推导的空间约束与基于类别亲和性的语义常识，高效定位高概率候选区域。

Result: 在自建的Dyna-THOR基准上进行了广泛评估，结果表明HitMem显著提升了物体重定位准确率，降低了探索成本，并在动态环境中增强了任务执行的性能。

Conclusion: HitMem通过层次化时序3D记忆与多模态上下文感知检索，有效解决了动态环境中物体位移导致的记忆-观测冲突问题，为具身智能体的长时任务执行提供了更鲁棒和自适应的场景记忆方案。

Abstract: Executing long-term tasks in dynamic environments requires embodied agents to maintain robust and adaptive 3D scene representations. However, most existing 3D memory frameworks rely on static world assumptions. When objects are displaced by human activities or unobserved events, agents encounter memory-observation conflicts and often require costly geometric recomputations or inefficient global re-exploration. To address this, we propose HitMem, a hierarchical temporal 3D memory framework with a multi-modal context-aware retrieval mechanism. Through continuous perception, HitMem unifies semantic and spatial information into a lightweight topological graph that captures support relationships, while a temporal decay mechanism dynamically regulates memory activeness to mitigate the impact of stale representations. In addition, the multi-modal context-aware retrieval mechanism defaults to filtering candidates using integrated semantic, spatial, and temporal memory features, and activates a specialized two-stage retrieval process when object displacement is detected. This process combines spatial constraints inferred from external agent trajectories with semantic common sense grounded in class affinities, efficiently identifying high-probability candidate regions. Extensive evaluations on our constructed Dyna-THOR benchmark demonstrate that HitMem significantly improves object relocation accuracy, reduces exploration costs, and enhances task execution performance in dynamic environments.

</details>
