### [136] [Accelerating Reinforcement Learning via MPC Solver-Gradient Guidance for Weights-varying MPC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01061)
*Baha Zarrouki,Arslan Thobani,Jasper Hoffmann,Mattia Piccinini,Rudolf Reiter,Felix Jahncke,Sébastien Gros,Davide Scaramuzza,Johannes Betz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SG-RL（Solver-Gradient Guided Reinforcement Learning），一种结合可微MPC求解器梯度和强化学习的在线MPC代价权重自适应方法。SG-RL以采样的闭环回报为目标函数，利用有界求解器梯度作为辅助指导，通过四种模块化算法注入PPO的actor更新缩放、策略损失、优势估计和价值函数学习。在两个全尺寸自动驾驶赛车上，SG-RL以高达70.6%更少的样本达到PPO最佳闭环回报，比GB-PL基线提升至少54%，并能零样本泛化到未见环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MPC中代价函数权重决定闭环行为，但固定参数化在变化条件下非最优，需要上下文依赖的在线自适应。纯RL方法样本效率低，而基于梯度的策略学习（GB-PL）利用可微MPC的低方差求解器梯度优化代理损失但存在模型失配偏差。作者旨在调和这一偏差-方差权衡，兼顾RL的闭环回报目标与求解器梯度的样本效率。

Method: SG-RL保持采样的闭环回报作为优化目标，将可微MPC求解器导出的有界梯度作为辅助指导信号，注入PPO训练。具体实现四种模块化算法：(1) actor更新缩放，(2) 策略损失注入，(3) 优势估计增强，(4) 价值函数学习增强。该方法在保持RL无偏目标的同时利用求解器灵敏度信息降低方差，提升样本效率。

Result: 在两个全尺寸自动驾驶赛车平台上，在有意引入模型失配的条件下，SG-RL以比PPO少最多70.6%的样本达到相同最佳闭环回报，相比GB-PL基线闭环回报至少提升54%，且能零样本泛化到未见过的环境。

Conclusion: 求解器梯度可以作为有效的辅助指导信号增强RL的MPC代价权重在线自适应学习，在不引入模型失配偏差的前提下显著提升样本效率，并具备良好的泛化能力。SG-RL为可微最优控制与强化学习相结合的在线自适应策略提供了一种通用框架。

Abstract: In Model Predictive Control (MPC), cost-function weights shape closed-loop behavior, yet changing conditions often make fixed parametrizations suboptimal and motivate context-dependent online adaptation. Learning such policies is difficult because behavior depends implicitly on numerical MPC solutions, producing nonlinear, potentially nonsmooth, long-horizon dependencies on policy parameters. This creates a bias-variance tradeoff: Reinforcement Learning (RL) optimizes realized closed-loop return from environment samples but is sample-inefficient, whereas Gradient-Based Policy Learning (GB-PL) uses low-variance solver gradients from differentiable MPC to optimize surrogate losses on predicted trajectories but can be biased under model mismatch. We propose Solver-Gradient Guided Reinforcement Learning (SG-RL), a solver-sensitivity augmentation for RL-based online MPC cost-weight adaptation. SG-RL keeps sampled closed-loop return as the objective and uses bounded solver-derived gradients as auxiliary guidance to improve stability and sample efficiency. We instantiate SG-RL in Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) with four modular algorithms that inject solver-gradient guidance into actor-update scaling, policy loss, advantage estimation, and value-function learning. On two full-scale autonomous racing platforms with intentional model mismatch, SG-RL reaches PPO's best closed-loop return with up to 70.6% fewer samples, outperforms GB-PL baselines by at least 54% in closed-loop return, and generalizes zero-shot to unseen environments.

</details>


### [137] [Adaptive Depth-Map-Guided Bundle Adjustment for Correspondence-Free Multi-View Point Cloud Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01089)
*Yiran Zhou,Yingyu Wang,Shoudong Huang,Liang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种免特征对应的多视角点云配准框架ADM-BA，使用自适应分层的深度图引导的束调整（bundle adjustment）方法，针对光滑金属表面、重复结构、遮挡和部分重叠等工业废钢切割场景中的挑战。该方法通过全局2.5-D网格表示场景，每个单元自适应维护多个深度假设，直接投影原始深度观测形成深度约束，无需显式特征对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人处理不规则废钢需要密集3D测量来替代人工视觉评估，但现有基于特征提取和数据关联的多视角配准方法在光滑金属表面、重复结构、遮挡等工业场景中容易建立错误对应，导致位姿估计不准确和重建失真，误差会直接传播到下游测量与规划环节。

Method: 场景用全局2.5-D网格表示，每个单元可自适应维护多个深度假设。原始深度观测直接投影到全局地图形成深度约束，无需显式特征对应。在多个表面产生冲突深度的网格单元，采用基于softmax的层分配将每个观测与兼容深度假设关联。最终形成非线性最小二乘问题，联合优化传感器位姿和分层深度图，对应关系由深度图表示和投影模型隐式诱导。

Result: 在自采集工业数据集上实验表明，该方法在挑战性工业场景中持续达到有竞争力的重建精度，同时保持鲁棒性和低计算成本，并已开源代码。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了免特征对应的深度图引导束调整框架在工业视觉场景中的有效性，为光滑金属等难以提取可靠特征的重建任务提供了可行方案，并开源实现促进后续研究。

Abstract: Robotic processing of irregular steel scrap requires dense 3-D measurement to replace manual visual assessment in hazardous cutting workcells. The reconstructed map is used to estimate piece dimensions, boundary geometry, feasible preheating and cutting regions, and collision-aware torch paths. The reconstruction errors therefore propagate directly to downstream measurement and planning. Existing multi-view registration methods commonly rely on feature extraction and data association to establish correspondences between views. In workcells with smooth metallic surfaces, repeated structures, occlusions, and partial overlaps, however, wrong correspondences may be established, leading to inaccurate pose estimation and distorted reconstruction. This paper presents an adaptive layered depth-map-guided bundle adjustment framework for correspondence-free multi-view point cloud registration. The scene is represented by a global 2.5-D grid, where each cell can adaptively maintain multiple depth hypotheses. Raw depth observations are directly projected into the global map to form depth constraints without explicit feature correspondences. At grid cells where multiple surfaces produce conflicting depths, a softmax-based layer assignment links each observation to compatible depth hypotheses. The resulting nonlinear least-squares formulation jointly refines sensor poses and the layered depth map, with correspondences implicitly induced by the depth-map representation and projection model. Experiments on self-collected industrial datasets show that the proposed method achieves consistently competitive reconstruction accuracy while maintaining robustness and low computational cost in challenging industrial scenarios. We release the open-source code implementation at: https://github.com/YiranZhou-Robotics/ADM-BA.git

</details>


### [138] [DNC-IMM: Early Lane-Change Intention Recognition via Neural Calibration Based on Driving Context Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01120)
*Woong-Chan Byun,Seung-Hyun Kong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出双神经校准交互多模型（DNC-IMM）用于自动驾驶中车道变换意图的早期识别，通过神经网络编码驾驶情境信息来校准转移概率矩阵和测量似然，在highD数据集上验证了在换道前及2-3秒预测时域上的优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车道变换意图的早期识别对自动驾驶和先进驾驶辅助系统的主动决策至关重要，传统的交互多模型（IMM）方法虽具有概率结构和可解释性，但对驾驶情境的自适应性不足。

Method: DNC-IMM方法用神经网络编码目标车辆运动、周围车辆间隙及相对速度等驾驶情境信息，同时校准IMM的转移概率矩阵和测量似然，最终意图由校准后的IMM模式后验确定而非单独的直分类器。

Result: 在highD数据集上，方法能在车道穿越前可靠识别换道意图，且在较早的2-3秒预测时域上表现尤为突出。

Conclusion: DNC-IMM在保持传统IMM概率结构和可解释性的同时，通过神经校准提升了驾驶情境自适应性，为早期换道意图识别提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Early recognition of lane-change intention is essential for proactive decision-making in autonomous driving and advanced driver assistance systems. This paper proposes a Dual Neural-Calibrated Interacting Multiple Model (DNC-IMM) that improves adaptability to driving context while preserving the probabilistic structure and interpretability of a conventional IMM. The proposed method encodes driving-context information, including target-vehicle motion, gaps to surrounding vehicles, and relative velocities, with a neural network that calibrates both the transition-probability matrix and measurement likelihoods. The final intention is determined from the calibrated IMM mode posterior rather than from a separate direct classifier. Experiments on the highD dataset demonstrate that the proposed method reliably recognizes lane-change intentions before lane crossing and provides particularly strong performance at the earlier 2-3 s prediction horizons.

</details>


### [139] [On Global Regulatability of Robot Manipulators by Classical PID](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01207)
*Cheng Zhao,Jingru Zhu,Lei Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究一类不确定MIMO非线性系统的扩展PID（EPID）控制。针对具有非均匀向量相对度（各通道相对度不同）的系统，提出通道式EPID控制器，每个通道的控制输入由对应跟踪误差的比例、积分、微分项构成，微分阶数依据该通道相对度选取。在未知非线性满足适当增长条件下，构造出由初始状态界、不确定性和参考信号确定的可行EPID参数集，证明该参数集中任意选择都能保证闭环系统半全局稳定并实现设定点调节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PID型控制文献对非均匀向量相对度（各通道相对度不同）的MIMO非线性系统关注较少，而这类系统在实际工程中广泛存在（如飞行控制系统）。作者旨在填补这一理论空白，并为工程实践中常见的逐通道PID调参做法提供理论依据。

Method: 提出通道式EPID控制器：每个通道的控制输入由相应跟踪误差的比例、积分、微分项构成，最高微分阶数根据该通道的相对度选取。在未知非线性满足适当增长条件下，构造以初始状态界、不确定性和参考信号为参数的可行EPID参数集，并证明该参数集中任意选择均保证半全局稳定性和设定点调节。

Result: 证明了只要EPID参数选自所构造的可行集，闭环系统即具有半全局稳定性并实现期望的设定点调节。结果表明EPID参数可以逐通道独立设计，为强耦合、不确定MIMO系统（含飞行控制系统）中逐通道调参的常见工程实践提供了理论正当性。

Conclusion: EPID参数可逐通道独立设计，在理论和实践上为不确定MIMO系统的通道式PID控制提供了一致性支撑。

Abstract: This paper studies a class of uncertain multi-input multi-output (MIMO) nonlinear systems using extended PID (EPID) control. We focus on systems possessing a well-defined vector relative degree whose components may vary across channels, a setting that received limited attention in the existing literature on PID-type control. We develop a channel-wise EPID controller, where each control input is constructed from the proportional, integral, and derivative terms of the corresponding tracking error, and the highest derivative order is selected according to the relative degree of that channel. Under suitable growth conditions on the unknown nonlinearities, we construct an admissible set of EPID parameters specified by prescribed initial-state bounds, the uncertainties and the reference signal. We show that any parameter choice from this set guarantees semiglobal stability of the closed-loop system and achieves the desired setpoint regulation. These results indicate that the EPID parameters can be designed independently for each channel, justifying the common engineering practice of channel-by-channel tuning in strongly coupled and uncertain MIMO systems, including flight control systems.

</details>


### [140] [EmbodiedSkills: A Unified Framework for Orchestrating, Training, and Deploying VLA Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01281)
*Wei Wang,Wenqiao Zhang,Yutong Lin,Yuqian Yuan,Tianwei Lin,Jinhao Mao,Zhenxuan Fan,Mingjian Gao,Yang Dai,Wentong Li,Zheqi Lv,Zheng Dong,Yingjie Niu,Jiaqi Zhu,Jun Xiao,Chao Li,Yueting Zhuang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EmbodiedSkills 提出一个统一的 VLA 智能体框架，将每个技能决策视为"执行提案"：运行时代码在执行前检查前置条件、执行后验证结果。通过固定的可执行技能接口连接高层技能选择、受限的低层 VLA 执行和动作后验证，形成一个闭环智能体循环。该接口还可记录规划、执行、验证和恢复事件作为结构化轨迹，支持组件监督和在线适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时序机器人任务不仅需要动作预测，还需要感知、规划、执行、进度验证和恢复的协调。单纯的动作预测或模型生成的技能决策无法保证提议操作在当前状态下有效，也无法保证其结果被验证。现有 VLA 模型缺乏这种闭环验证与恢复机制。

Method: 提出 EmbodiedSkills 统一框架：将每个技能决策视为执行提案（execution proposal），运行时在执行前检查前提条件、执行后验证结果。通过固定的共享可执行技能接口连接高层技能选择、受限的低层 VLA 执行和事后验证。该接口同时记录规划/执行/验证/恢复事件为结构化轨迹，提供组件监督并支持在线适应。使用 Qwen3-VL 和 OpenPI/pi0.5 在 RoboTwin 2.0 和 LIBERO 上实例化。

Result: 在 RoboTwin 2.0 的 50 个任务上平均成功率 86.20%，在 LIBERO 四个套件上平均成功率 97.40%。在四个记忆依赖的 RMBench 任务上，相同的任务适应执行方法平均成功率仅 12.5%。

Conclusion: 任务适应的低层 VLA 策略在标准基准上表现优异，表明 EmbodiedSkills 提供了一个可训练、可检查的智能体层，可将 VLA 策略转变为闭环具身系统。但在记忆依赖的 RMBench 任务上表现显著下降，揭示了当前方法在此类场景中的局限。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models map visual observations and language instructions directly to robot actions, but long-horizon tasks require more than action prediction. An agent must coordinate perception, planning, execution, progress verification, and recovery as the physical state evolves. An action prediction or a model-generated skill decision does not, by itself, guarantee that the proposed operation is valid in the current state or that its outcome will be verified. We propose EmbodiedSkills, a unified framework that treats each skill decision as an execution proposal: the runtime checks its prerequisites before execution and verifies the outcome afterward. A shared executable-skill interface connects high-level skill selection, bounded low-level VLA execution, and post-action verification within a single agent loop. Because this interface remains fixed, low-level VLA policies can be replaced or adapted without changing the agent loop. The interface also records planning, execution, verification, and recovery events as structured trajectories, which provide supervision for individual components and can support optional online adaptation when interactive feedback is available. We instantiate EmbodiedSkills with Qwen3-VL and OpenPI/pi0.5 on RoboTwin 2.0 and LIBERO. Task-adapted low-level VLA policies achieve an average success rate of 86.20% across 50 RoboTwin 2.0 tasks and 97.40% across the four LIBERO suites. These results establish the execution performance of the task-adapted low-level VLA policies used in EmbodiedSkills. On four memory-dependent RMBench tasks, the same task-adapted execution approach achieves 12.5% average success. The framework provides a trainable and inspectable agent layer for turning these policies into closed-loop embodied systems.

</details>


### [141] [Integrating Traffic Noise Emission Modelling into Variable Speed Limit Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01339)
*Jiawen Meng,John Pravin Arockiasamy,Alexey Vinel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种噪声感知的限速控制框架，融合交通状态估计与简化CNOSSOS-EU排放指标，通过分段控制器动态调整变速限制（VSL），以响应短期交通噪声变化。在24小时微观仿真评估中，该策略相比无限制交通将接收点等效声级降低2.9 dB(A)，同时保持比永久低速方案高约11.3 km/h的平均车速，且仅在排放接近目标阈值时选择性激活减速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 道路交通噪声是重大环境挑战，但现有限速管理策略多为静态，无法响应交通噪声排放的短期波动。VSL系统虽广泛用于安全和拥堵缓解，却很少将交通噪声作为显式操作控制目标，缺乏将环境性能指标纳入实时速度控制的框架。

Method: 构建噪声感知VSL框架：集成聚合交通状态估计与简化CNOSSOS-EU排放指标；采用基于阶段（stage-based）的控制器，以时变参考阈值为基准，动态调整离散限速等级以响应估计的排放条件；通过经验公路数据标定的微观交通仿真进行验证，并跨多个随机实现进行复现。

Result: 24小时评估期内，自适应策略相对无限制交通将接收点等效声级降低2.9 dB(A)，同时平均车速比永久低速限制高约11.3 km/h。逐时段分析表明减速在排放接近校准目标时选择性激活，而非强制恒定中间限速。交通稳定性指标显示速度变异性较无限制运行适度增加，但制动强度显著低于统一低速限速方案。

Conclusion: 研究证实将环境性能指标整合到运行速度控制中的可行性，为传统基于基础设施的噪声缓解措施提供了实用补充方案，展示了面向噪声的智能交通控制的新方向。

Abstract: Road traffic noise remains a major environmental challenge, yet most speed management strategies are static and do not respond to short-term variations in traffic noise emissions. Although variable speed limit (VSL) systems are widely deployed for safety and congestion mitigation, traffic noise is rarely treated as an explicit operational control objective.
  This paper proposes a noise-aware VSL framework that integrates aggregated traffic-state estimation with a simplified CNOSSOS-EU-based emission indicator. A stage-based controller with time-varying reference thresholds dynamically adjusts discrete speed-limit levels in response to estimated emission conditions. The framework is evaluated using microscopic traffic simulation calibrated with empirical motorway data and replicated across multiple stochastic realisations.
  Over a 24-hour evaluation period, the adaptive strategy reduces the receiver-based equivalent sound level by 2.9 dB(A) relative to unrestricted traffic conditions, while maintaining an average vehicle speed approximately 11.3 km/h higher than a permanently imposed low-speed regime. Period-wise analysis shows that speed reductions are activated selectively when emission levels approach calibrated targets, rather than enforcing a constant intermediate limit. Traffic stability indicators reveal moderate increases in speed variability compared with unrestricted operation, but substantially lower braking intensity than under uniform low-speed enforcement.
  These results demonstrate the feasibility of integrating environmental performance indicators into operational speed control, providing a practical complement to conventional infrastructure-based noise mitigation measures.

</details>


### [142] [Scalable Rao-Blackwellized Online Planning for High-Dimensional POMDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01351)
*Jiho Lee,Nisar Ahmed,Kyle Hollins Wray,Zachary Sunberg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文扩展了Rao-Blackwellized在线POMDP（RB-POMDP）框架，通过混合连续-离散信念表示在高维状态下提高在线规划的泛化能力。该方法在树搜索规划中解析地传播被边缘化状态分量的不确定性，从而降低蒙特卡洛价值估计中的采样方差，并与FastSLAM 2.0集成用于机器人搜索救援任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分可观测环境中的在线规划仍是机器人系统的根本挑战，现有采样型POMDP求解器随信念维度增加性能退化，因为蒙特卡洛估计存在高方差。需要一种能在高维连续域中保持规划质量的方法。

Method: 扩展RB-POMDP框架，通过混合连续-离散信念表示，在基于树的规划过程中解析地传播与边缘化状态分量相关的不确定性，减少采样引入的方差。具体集成FastSLAM 2.0在搜索救援任务中验证。

Result: 在等效计算预算下，所提规划器以显著更少的粒子和规划模拟实现了比纯采样方法更高的累积奖励。

Conclusion: 具有可解析充分统计量的结构化高维机器人问题可在RB-POMDP框架内被有效利用，实现计算上可行的在线决策。该工作为高维POMDP在线规划提供了一种降低方差的理论与实用路径。

Abstract: Online planning under uncertainty remains a fundamental challenge for robotic systems operating in partially observable environments with high-dimensional state spaces. While sampling-based POMDP solvers enable approximate decision-making in large or continuous domains, their performance degrades as belief dimensionality increases due to the high variance inherent in Monte Carlo-based estimation. In this work, we extend the Rao-Blackwellized online POMDP (RB-POMDP) framework to improve its generalizability in high-dimensional settings through hybrid continuous-discrete belief representations. By analytically propagating uncertainty associated with marginalized state components during tree-based planning, the proposed approach reduces sampling-induced variance in value estimation. We demonstrate the effectiveness of this framework in a robotic search-and-rescue task by integrating it with FastSLAM 2.0. Experimental results show that the proposed planner achieves higher cumulative rewards using significantly fewer particles and planning simulations than purely sampling-based methods under equivalent computational budgets. These results suggest that structured high-dimensional robotic problems admitting tractable sufficient statistics can be effectively leveraged within the RB-POMDP framework for computationally feasible online decision-making.

</details>


### [143] [Obstacle-Aware Autonomous Coverage and Navigation for Outdoor Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01384)
*Leonardo Gargani,Matteo Frosi,Matteo Matteucci*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种统一的ROS 2户外覆盖架构，结合双天线RTK-GNSS+EKF鲁棒定位、控制器感知的覆盖规划优化、行为树任务管理（多目标执行、分层恢复、能耗感知自返航充电），在仿真和真实多场景实验中实现93.1%-96.1%的覆盖清扫率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 户外长时间自主覆盖任务在经典规划之外仍面临多个挑战：空旷场地定位漂移、杂乱场地障碍物、转弯机动中的控制器可行性、以及带能量管理的持续自主性。现有关键单独模块但缺乏统一系统级解决方案。

Method: 提出统一ROS 2架构：(1) 双天线RTK-GNSS融合EKF保持位置与航向精度；(2) 在成熟覆盖规划器上添加三个控制器感知的精化改进（转弯可行性等）；(3) Behavior-Tree任务管理器协调多目标执行、分层恢复、成本感知目标管理与自动停靠返充。通过仿真与多形状多障碍密度的真实场地验证。

Result: 在多个不同几何形状和障碍物密度的户外区域中，该架构可靠完成覆盖任务，实现93.1%-96.1%的计划覆盖区域清扫率，并有仿真与真实世界试验验证。

Conclusion: 该整合式系统表明通过鲁棒定位、规划精化与行为树自主管理三者的协同，可以有效解决户外长时覆盖任务中的系统级挑战，具备实际部署价值。

Abstract: Long-duration outdoor coverage with autonomous platforms remains challenging beyond classical planning: deployments face localization drift in open spaces, obstacles in cluttered sites, controller feasibility in turn-heavy maneuvers, and persistent autonomy with energy management. We propose a unified ROS 2 architecture for outdoor coverage that combines coverage planning, robust localization, and Nav2-based execution. A dual-antenna RTK-GNSS fused in an EKF keeps the robot pose, both position and heading, accurate across long missions; three controller-aware refinements are added to a mature coverage planner; a Behavior-Tree mission manager coordinates multi-goal execution, layered recovery, cost-aware goal management, and autonomous docking for return-to-charge. We validate the stack through simulation and real-world trials across multiple outdoor areas with varying geometries and obstacle densities. Overall, these results show that the proposed stack can reliably complete outdoor coverage missions across varied areas, sweeping 93.1% to 96.1% of the planned coverage area.

</details>


### [144] [Autonomous robotic bridging using distributed swarm control without inter-agent communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01394)
*Vishwaak C. Thamaraiselvan,Cody L. Lundberg,Michail Theofandis,Suhas Chelian,Nicholas R. Gans*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍SCARAB（Swarm-Capable Autonomous Robotic Aquatic Bridging），一种基于分布式群集控制和多模型感知的自主水面机器人群系统。智能体无需GPS或RF通信即可自主定位、编队并前往目标位置，用于军事浮桥架设和民用灾害救援。系统通过物理引擎仿真在风浪环境下的多USV场景进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前美军的Improved Ribbon Bridge（浮桥）架设过程可预测、分散性差，士兵暴露在危险环境中且后勤负担重。作者希望通过自主水面机器人群实现不可预测、分散式的渡河架桥能力，提高人员生存率并减小后勤足迹。

Method: 采用分布式群集控制算法，结合多模型传感（对智能体和对接目标的多源感知），使USV智能体能在无GPS/无RF通信的受限环境（竞争环境）中通过自主定位、编队形成和路径规划到达目标位置完成架桥。在非竞争环境中可选用GPS和RF通信增强性能。系统通过基于物理引擎的仿真验证，模拟了水流和风的影响。

Result: 在包含水流和风的物理引擎仿真中成功演示了多个USV智能体的自主架桥行为，验证了无GPS/无RF条件下分布式群集控制和感知方法的可行性。

Conclusion: SCARAB展示了自主水面机器人群在军事架桥和民用救灾中的应用潜力。当前仅为仿真验证，未来需要在真实水面环境中进行实体实验以验证鲁棒性和实际部署能力。

Abstract: We describe SCARAB--Swarm-Capable Autonomous Robotic Aquatic Bridging. Using distributed swarm control and multi-model sensing of agents and docking targets, agents can localize themselves, join into formations and proceed to desired target locations. Our technologies would eventually allow the Army to perform unpredictable, dispersed river crossings, enhance crew survivability, and minimize the logistics footprint compared to the current Improved Ribbon Bridge. Our methods operate without GPS or RF communications, though these can be used in non-contested environments (e.g., civilian disaster relief for flooding, etc.). We demonstrate our system via physics-engine-based simulation of several agents using unmanned surface vehicles (USVs) in the presence of currents and wind.

</details>


### [145] [Evaluating Multimodal LLMs as Generalist Vision-Language-Action Agents for Drone Control: Commanding, Approaching, Tracking and Searching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01404)
*Jaewoo Park,Minyoung Lee,Sukmin Seo,Moonbin Yim,Hyunwook Yoon,Dohoon Ryu,Daehee Kim,Myungseo Song,Jihyuk Byun,Seunggyu Chang,Taeho Kil,Jiseob Kim,Bado Lee,Geewook Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 DroneCATS 基准和 DroneCATS-Agent 架构，将 MLLM 直接放入无人机控制回路，通过纯提示词声明完整动作空间，让模型自主完成 yaw 搜索、犹豫决策和自我声明到达。评估从 2B 到前沿规模的模型，发现小模型导航不差但常过早/过晚声明到达，多无人机场景会盲目复制单坐标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有将 MLLM 用于无人机控制的系统不断收窄模型决策空间，本文希望反其道而行——把 MLLM 当作可独立变量，完全信任其在纯文本提示下完成端到端动作决策，并揭示小模型与前沿模型在嵌入端实际表现的本质差异。

Method: 提出 DroneCATS 基准（四类任务：接近可见目标、跟踪移动目标、搜索初始视野外目标、指挥多无人机机群）和 DroneCATS-Agent 架构（MLLM 作为可替换组件，全部动作空间仅通过提示声明，无微调、无函数调用 schema），评估包含 2B 小模型在内的多规模模型。

Result: 即使最简单的具身设置也远未解决。关键发现：飞行导航不是失败点——小模型进入成功半径的可靠性不输甚至优于前沿模型；但小模型在动作协议上崩溃（过早/过晚声明到达），多无人机场景中盲目复制单一坐标跨视角使用。模型的空间感知能力强，但动作协议纪律差。

Conclusion: 区分可部署边缘模型与前沿模型的关键不是导航能力，而是持续遵守声明协议并发出正确终止动作的纪律。开放问题是在机载计算成本下缩小这一差距，DroneCATS 旨在衡量该距离。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) are strong perceivers of images and video. We ask how far that reach extends into acting: dropping an MLLM directly into a drone's control loop, with its entire action space declared solely in the prompt. Recent systems approach this setting but increasingly narrow the model's decision-making. We widen it back. We introduce DroneCATS-Agent, an architecture where the MLLM is a swappable component, and DroneCATS, a benchmark treating the model as the independent variable. Beyond merely flying toward a pixel, our agent entrusts the model to yaw and search, deliberate when unsure, and self-declare arrival---all without fine-tuning or function-calling schemas. Evaluating frontier and open models across four core capabilities---approaching a visible target, tracking a moving one, searching outside the initial view, and commanding a multi-drone fleet---reveals that even the simplest embodied settings are far from solved. Crucially, to identify what breaks first at the edge, our roster scales down to 2B parameters. The findings expose a stark paradox: it is not the flying that fails. Small open models often navigate into the success radius more reliably than frontier models, yet lose the episode by declaring arrival prematurely or not at all. Multi-drone commanding amplifies this divide, with small models failing by blindly copying a single coordinate across distinct views. Viewed as vision-language-action agents, the models' spatial perception holds up, but their action protocol does not. What separates a deployable edge model from a frontier model is not navigation, but the discipline to sustain a declared protocol and emit the correct terminating action. The open problem is closing this gap at onboard compute costs---yielding a fast model that plans persistently and knows exactly when it is done---and DroneCATS is built to measure that distance.

</details>


### [146] [Vision-Based Leader-Follower Formation Control for Cooperative UAVs in GPS-Degraded Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01420)
*Deekshitha Angadi,Naveena Budda,Vikas Agarwal,Rojesh Arunkumar Mulasa,Ravi Killamsetty,Mohamed Samshad,Narsimlu Kemsaram*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于视觉的多无人机协同编队框架，采用前视RGB-D相机检测、跟踪和定位领航无人机，通过轻量级YOLO检测器与深度信息融合估计相对位姿，在GPS失效时提供备份定位，并在基于XTDrone的物理仿真中验证了编队保持性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多无人机系统的协同编队依赖可靠的相对感知能力，当绝对定位传感器（如GPS）退化或失效时，跟随无人机仍需要安全地维持编队和完成任务。现有方案缺乏轻量、实时且可在机载部署的视觉相对定位框架。

Method: 使用前视RGB-D相机，采用在专用无人机数据集上训练的轻量YOLO检测器预测领航机边界框，通过针孔相机模型将边界框与深度信息融合估计领航机相对位姿，构建leader-follower位置控制器，并在GPS/外部定位不可用时可作为备份。系统以ROS节点实现，在XTDrone物理仿真中评估。

Result: 在含传感器噪声和通信中断的XTDrone仿真中，框架在标称条件和定位传感器故障下均能保持稳定的leader-follower编队，检测精度和运行时间具有合理计算开销。

Conclusion: 该框架为将视觉协同编队控制扩展到真实多无人机系统提供了实用基础，验证了视觉相对定位在GPS失效场景下的可行性。

Abstract: Cooperation in multi-UAV systems requires reliable relative perception so that follower vehicles can maintain formation and continue their mission safely even when absolute positioning sensors degrade or fail. This paper presents a vision-based cooperative formation framework running on a follower UAV that uses a front-facing RGB-D camera to detect, track, and localize a leader UAV in real-time. A lightweight YOLO-based detector is trained on a dedicated drone dataset and deployed onboard to predict leader bounding boxes, which are then fused with depth information via a pinhole camera model to estimate the leader's relative pose. These estimates provide a leader-follower position controller and can also be used as a backup when GPS or external localization is unavailable. This framework is implemented as a set of ROS nodes and evaluated in a physics-based multi-UAV simulation built on XTDrone, with sensor noise and communication dropouts. We evaluate detection accuracy, runtime, and formation-keeping error under nominal conditions and under simulated failures of the positioning sensors. The results show that the proposed framework maintains stable leader-follower formations with reasonable computational cost and provides a practical basis for extending vision-based cooperative formation control to real-world multi-UAV systems.

</details>


### [147] [Does Imitation Learning Preserve Temporal Robustness in Dexterous Manipulation? An Expert-Learner Comparison Across Task Execution Speeds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01453)
*Clinton Enwerem,John S. Baras,Calin Belta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究模仿学习策略在任务执行速度变化下的时间鲁棒性。在接触密集的ParcelStow任务中，脚本专家和ACT策略在标称速度下均达到100%成功率，但在演示速度范围内，专家成功率84%，ACT仅53%。阶段分析显示ACT的失败主要源于插入错位。作者结论：相同的标称任务成功率并不意味着策略能保持专家在执行速度上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习的灵巧操作策略通常针对场景、物体或指令的变化进行鲁棒性评估，但很少检验它们在任务执行速度变化下的表现。理解学习器相对其模仿的专家保留了多少时间鲁棒性是开放问题。

Method: 在ParcelStow任务（获取、重定向、插入包裹的接触密集任务）中，作者在相同的任务条件、初始条件采样和加速因子下比较脚本专家和ACT策略。演示覆盖包裹获取后的操作阶段的速度范围。除了整体成功率，还进行阶段级失败分析，并比较具有不同参数初始化的ACT策略，以及相对运动交接下的阶段完成率。

Result: 专家和ACT在标称速度下都达到100%成功率，但速度增加后分歧出现：专家最高84%，ACT最高53%。两个不同初始化的ACT策略分别下降34和48个百分点，专家仅下降16个点。47次ACT失败中35次是插入错位。在相对运动交接下，所有ACT获取都保留包裹至自由空间转移阶段，但只有64%完成整体任务（专家95%）。所有414次无力闭合的获取均未完成任务。

Conclusion: 相同的标称任务成功率不能保证性能在速度变化下保持不变。速度鲁棒性应作为评估模仿学习操作策略的一个独立维度，而阶段级分析和力闭合检测为诊断时间鲁棒性差异提供了洞察。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation policies learned by imitation are typically evaluated for robustness to variation in scenes, objects, or instructions, but their performance across task execution speeds is less often examined. This leaves open how much temporal robustness a learner retains relative to the expert it imitates. We compare an expert and learner under the same task conditions, initial-condition draws, and speedup factors. We instantiate the evaluation in ParcelStow, a contact-rich task in which the robot acquires, reorients, and inserts a parcel. The demonstrations span the speedup range for the manipulation phases after parcel acquisition. A scripted expert and an Action Chunking with Transformers (ACT) policy trained from the expert's demonstrations both achieve 100 percent task success at nominal speed. Their success rates diverge within the demonstrated range: at its maximum, expert success is 84 percent and ACT success is 53 percent. Two ACT policies with different parameter initializations show similar degradation, decreasing by 34 and 48 percentage points from nominal speed to the maximum demonstrated speed, compared with 16 points for the expert. Stage-level analysis shows that 35 of ACT's 47 failures at the maximum demonstrated speed are insertion misalignments. Under the relative-motion handoff, every ACT acquisition retains the parcel through reorientation and transfer in free space, but only 64 percent complete the overall task, compared with 95 percent after expert acquisition. Across all evaluated policies and speeds, none of the 414 acquisitions without force closure completes the task. Equal nominal task success therefore does not imply preservation of expert performance across execution speeds. Code, data, and evaluation scripts are available at https://github.com/coenwerem/parcelstow.

</details>


### [148] [A System for Fast, Resilient, and Adaptable Loco-Manipulation Behaviors on Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01518)
*Duncan Calvert,Luigi Penco,Dexton Anderson,Tomasz Bialek,Arghya Chatterjee,Beomyeong Park,Robert Griffin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一套机器人本地的、运行时可编辑的行为编排与运行时系统，结合对象中心Affordance模板、行为场景与全身控制器，在Unitree H1-2和Alex上演示了六种任务变体，包括推门穿越（34秒）和颜色分球（45秒），并支持数小时内从零创建和适配新的操作行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人需要在为人类设计的空间中承担体力劳动、危险和重复性工作，但这要求协调运动、全身运动、感知、接触和操作者监督。现有系统在行为架构上缺乏运行时可编辑性，导致行为创建、适应、扩展和组合速度慢，限制了实用性。

Method: 提出一种行为架构，结合三部分：1) 对象中心的Affordance Templates（提供行为模板）；2) 树状结构组织与逻辑；3) 运行时可编辑的感知，通过行为场景（behavior scene）和原语场景操作实现。操作者界面与机器人持续同步，支持运行时编排、监控和修复。动作原语通过支持并发身体运动和步行的全身控制器执行。

Result: 在Unitree H1-2和Alex上演示六种任务变体：推门穿越34秒完成，人干扰下颜色分球45秒完成。计时编排会话显示从零创建新的移动操作行为和适配现有行为可在数小时内完成。与文献对比，该方法与近期学习的系统具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 行为架构可以成为能力、速度和可靠性的主要推动因素，运行时可编辑性使行为创建、适应、扩展和组合变得快速，为人形机器人在人类空间中执行实用任务提供了有效的系统级解决方案。

Abstract: There is tremendous value in humanoid robots taking on physically demanding, hazardous, and repetitive work in spaces built for humans. However, a useful robot for these spaces must coordinate locomotion, whole-body motion, perception, contact, and operator supervision. We present a robot-local, runtime-editable behavior authoring and runtime system that addresses these challenges. We argue that behavior architecture can be a primary enabler of capability, speed, and reliability, and that runtime editability enables fast behavior creation, adaptation, extension, and combination.
  Our behavior architecture combines object-centric Affordance Templates, a tree structure that provides organization and logic, and runtime-editable perception through a behavior scene and primitive scene actions. Our operator interface remains continuously synchronized to the robot for runtime authoring, monitoring, and repair. Action primitives execute through a whole-body controller that supports concurrent body motions and walking.
  Demonstrations of our system cover six task variants on Unitree H1-2 and Alex. We execute a push door traversal in 34 seconds and sort six balls by color in 45 seconds under human disturbance. Timed authoring sessions show scratch creation of new loco-manipulation behaviors and adaptation of existing ones in hours. Comparison against the literature finds our approach to be competitive with recent learned systems.

</details>


### [149] [SG-AMP: Scene-Graph-Guided Active Perception and Semantics-Aware Motion Planning for Pepper Plants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01579)
*Rohit Menon,Shiva Rudra Lolla,Niklas Mueller-Goldingen,Gokul Chenchani,Ribana Roscher,Maren Bennewitz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SG-AMP，一个将稳健深度补全（含输入条件不确定性）、持久全景建图、植物场景图推理和语义感知主动视角运动规划集成的系统。系统不仅检查不确定的已观测区域，还通过场景图显式假设未观测的辣椒-果梗连接，并引导近距感知向这些区域移动。候选视角依据期望信息增益选择，类相关运动成本区分受保护的辣椒、果梗和茎与可条件穿越的叶簇。在辣椒数据上，感知网络达到55.27%语义mIoU、38.67% PQ和40.62mm深度RMSE；输入条件不确定性将NYUv2 NLL从-1.6518改善到-1.6925，AUSE从0.0102改善到0.0087。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业机器人（如辣椒采摘）需要在复杂、遮挡严重的植物环境中精确感知和规划。现有系统要么忽视感知不确定性，要么缺乏对未观测区域的主动推理能力，导致采摘遗漏或损伤。本文动机是将深度补全的不确定性估计、全景语义建图与主动场景图推理结合，实现对未观测目标（如被遮挡的辣椒-果梗连接）的主动近距离感知，从而提升采摘成功率和安全性。

Method: SG-AMP集成四大模块：(1) 输入条件不确定性的鲁棒深度补全网络；(2) 持久（lifelong）全景语义建图；(3) 植物场景图推理，显式假设未观测的辣椒-果梗连接关系；(4) 语义感知的主动视角-运动规划器，基于期望信息增益和类相关运动成本（区分受保护的辣椒/果梗/茎与可穿越叶簇）选择最优视角。

Result: 在辣椒数据上，感知网络获得55.27%语义mIoU、38.67%全景质量PQ和40.62mm深度RMSE；输入条件不确定性显著提升不确定性校准：NYUv2 NLL从-1.6518改善至-1.6925（更优），AUSE从0.0102降至0.0087。系统展示了场景图驱动的主动感知在结构性推理未观测区域上的有效性。

Conclusion: SG-AMP验证了将场景图推理与不确定性感知的深度补全相结合的主动感知范式，能够超越单纯基于不确定性的探索，通过语义关系假设主动驱动视野规划。该方法对农业机器人在高遮挡环境中的精细操作（如辣椒-果梗检测）具有实用价值，但其泛化性和在更大规模真实场景中的部署仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: We present SG-AMP, integrating robust depth completion with input-conditioned uncertainty, persistent panoptic mapping, plant scene-graph reasoning, and semantics-aware active view-motion planning. Beyond inspecting uncertain observed regions, the scene graph explicitly hypothesizes unobserved pepper--peduncle attachments and directs close-range sensing toward them. Candidate views are selected according to expected information gain, while class-dependent motion costs distinguish protected peppers, peduncles, and stems from conditionally traversable foliage. On pepper data, the perception network achieves $55.27\%$ semantic mIoU, $38.67\%$ PQ, and $40.62\,\mathrm{mm}$ depth RMSE, while input-conditioned uncertainty improves NYUv2 NLL from $-1.6518$ to $-1.6925$ and AUSE from $0.0102$ to $0.0087$.

</details>


### [150] [Facet-0: A Robotic Foundation Model for Contact-Rich Precise Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01596)
*Haoyuan Deng,Haichao Liu,Wenkai Guo,Yuan Ling,Zaijia Yang,Yuanjiang Xue,Haosheng Sun,Liangzi Wang,Ziwei Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Facet-0 是一个面向亚毫米级机器人装配的机器人基础模型，通过联合动作-腕部力矩(wrench)预测统一了多模态表示学习和强化学习。它在ManuFacet-1K数据集（1000小时力同步语料，覆盖多种机械臂和制造单元）上训练，在五项亚毫米计算机装配任务上达到82%平均成功率（最强基线15%），0.5mm放置精度和50ms指令延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界机器人装配需要亚毫米公差的空间精度、柔顺交互和对接触失败的鲁棒性。现有方法缺乏对动作接触后果的预测和价值评估能力，导致在接触密集的精密装配场景中性能不佳。

Method: Facet-0统一了多模态表示学习与RL后训练：1) 因果力矩历史与视觉-语言语义和运动学状态对齐；2) 流匹配(flow matching)生成动作块及其期望诱发的未来腕部力矩轮廓；3) 部署rollout训练分布式的Action-Wrench Critic区分相似任务进度但不同接触结果的运动；4) 相位感知奖励和接触选择性信度分配聚焦策略改进；5) 轻量有界actor复用冻结表示进行机上自适应，RL在可执行笛卡尔动作上定义，辅助力矩头保持非指令的预测性动作-接触耦合。

Result: 在ManuFacet-1K（1000小时力同步语料，三个机械臂和多制造单元）上训练后，有界任务自适应系统在五项亚毫米计算机装配任务上达到82%平均成功率（最强基线仅15%），0.5mm放置精度，50ms指令延迟。

Conclusion: 通过将力矩/接触后果的预测与价值评估纳入动作生成和策略优化，Facet-0实现了亚毫米级真实装配性能，证明了"预测并估值接触后果"是机器人装配基础模型的核心能力方向。

Abstract: Real-world robotic assembly at sub-millimeter tolerances demands spatial precision, compliant interaction, and robustness to contact failures. We present Facet-0, a robotic foundation model that predicts and values the contact consequences of its actions. Facet-0 unifies multimodal representation learning and reinforcement learning (RL) post-training around a joint action-wrench proposal: a causal wrench history is aligned with vision-language semantics and kinematic state, and flow matching generates each action chunk together with the future wrist-wrench profile it is expected to induce. Deployment rollouts train a distributional Action-Wrench Critic to distinguish motions with similar task progress but different contact outcomes, while phase-aware rewards and contact-selective credit concentrate policy improvement on decisive interactions. To accommodate part-specific dynamics, a lightweight bounded actor reuses the frozen representation for on-robot adaptation; RL remains defined over executable Cartesian actions, while an auxiliary wrench head preserves predictive, non-commanded action-contact coupling. Trained on ManuFacet-1K, a 1,000-hour force-synchronized corpus spanning three embodiments and multiple manufacturing cells, the bounded task-adapted system reaches 82% mean success on five sub-millimeter computer-assembly tasks, compared with 15% for the strongest baseline, with 0.5 mm placement accuracy and 50 ms command latency.

</details>
